Text:

<1>
oṃ
(śrīmatpāṃcarātradivyāgamāṃtargata)
śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitā
prathamodhyāyaḥ śāstrāvatāravidhiḥ
maṃgalaṃ
namaśśrīvāsudevāya satyajñānasvarūpiṇe
svāśritāvaradīkṣāya mokṣaikaphaladāyine 1
muktyupāya viṣaye ṛṣi praśnaḥ
ṛṣayaḥ
śrutā vedāstvayā pūrvaṃ dharmaśāstrāṇyanekaśaḥ
eteṣu muktyupāyaṃ tu na paśyāma stapodhana 2
ta dvadasva mahacchāstraṃ niśreyasakaraṃ (param)śubhaṃ
eva sāṃsārikaṃ duḥkhaṃ tarāmojñānavartmanā 3
vasiṣṭhaprativacanaṃ
vasiṣṭhaḥ
pāṃcarātrāgama praśastatā kathanaṃ, tacchāstrāvataraṇaṃca
bhagavadbhakti reva syā dbhaktānāṃ muktikāraṇaṃ
tadbhaktibodhakaṃ śāstraṃpāṃcarātrāgamaṃsmṛtam 4
rajoguṇāvanodāya brahmaṇe viṣṇunā purā
yaduktaṃpāṃcarātrākhyaṃ rahasyāmnāyamuttamaṃ 5
tacchṛṇudhvaṃ muniśreṣṭhāssaṃsārāṃbudhitārakam
sārdhakoṭipramāṇena brahmaṇā keśavā cchratam 6
rātribhiḥ paṃcabhi ssarvaṃ pāṃcarātrāgamaṃ smṛtam
eta dāgamaśāstreṇa biṃbadevālayādikam 7
vinirmāya pratiṣṭhāpya nitya mārādhayannaraḥ
śāśvataṃ pada māpnoti puna rāvattivarjitam 8
poṃcarātrāgama
ṛṣayaḥ
sāṃpradāyaśravaṇārthaṃ ṛṣipraśnaḥ
bhagavanmuniśārdūla sarvaśāstraviśārada
kaivalyapada micchūnā masmākaṃ śaraṇārthinām 9
tatpāṃcarātraśāstrasya vidhānaṃ saṃprakāśaya
vasiṣṭhaprativacanaṃ
vasiṣṭhaḥ
śrīpāṃca rātrāgama śabdārtha nirūpaṇaṃnāmanirdeśānica ekāyanaṃ
śruṇudhvaṃ munaya ssarve vedamekāyanābhidam 10
pāṃcarātraṃ mūlavedaṃ sātvataṃ taṃtramityapi
bhagavacchāstra miti ta nnāmabhi paripaṭhyate 11
mokṣāyanāya vai paṃthā etadanyona vidyate
tasmā dekāyanaṃ ceti pravadaṃti manīṣiṇaḥ 12
āgamaḥ
ācārakathanāddavya gatiprāptividhānataḥ
māṅātmyatatvakathanā dāgamaśceti gaṇyate 13
mūlavedaḥ
mahato vedavṛkṣanya mūlabhūto mahā nayam
ṛgādyāskaṃdha bhūtāsteśākhābhūtāścayoginaḥ 14
jaganmūlasya vedasya vāsudevasya mūkhyataḥ
pratipādakatā siddhā mūlavedākhyatā dvijāḥ 15
sātvataṃ taṃtraṃ
satvasvarūpahariṇā proktatvā tsātvataṃ(śrutam)matam
tanoti vipūlānarthāntatvasaṃtrasamāśritān 16
trāṇaṃ ca kurute yasmāttaṃtrami tyabhidhīyate
pāṃcarātraṃtusarveṣāṃśāstrāṇāṃmūlakāraṇam 17
brāhmaṇa ssarvavarṇeṣu yatiścāśramiṣūttamaḥ
paṃcarātra praśastiḥ
yadvadgaṃgā ca tthīreṣudaivateṣvasi cācyutaḥ 18
tadvascarveṣu śāstreṣu pāṃcarātraṃ praśasyate
pāṃcarātraṃ mahaddivyaṃśāstraṃśrutivibhāvanam 19
viśeṣe ṇādhigaṃtavyaṃ gītaṃ bhagavatā svayam
yajeta puruṣaṃ sākṣādyathārhaṃ pratimādiṣu 20
bhagavannarūpaṇa
ṛṣayaḥ
tadarcanāvidhau ṛṣipraśnaḥ
ko vā puruṣa i tyukto vidhiḥ ko vā tadarcane
pradimālakṣaṇaṃ (pradimālakṣaṇaṃbrūhi vistareṇadvijottama)kīdṛk vadasvabhagavanmune 21
vasiṣṭhavacanaṃ
vasiṣṭhaḥ
śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāvataraṇaṃ
puruṣottamena devena pūrvaṃ bhagavatā svayam
pāṃcarātrāgamaṃ divyaṃ mamapitresvayaṃbhuve 22
proktaṃtacca mayā labdhaṃ vāridhe ramṛtaṃ yathā
saṃhitā praśastiḥ
karṣaṇādipratiṣṭhāṃtaṃ pratiṣṭhādyutsavāṃtakam 23
utsavaṃ tu samārabhya prāyaścittāṃta meva hi
yasyāṃsamagaṃ saṃproktaṃ puruṣottama saṃhitā 24
prayojanaṃ ca śāstrasya mokṣaḥ prakṛtidurlabhaḥ
abhidheyaṃ bhagavato samārādhana muttamam 25
saṃbaṃdho vīkṣyate sadbhi ssādhyasādhakalakṣaṇaḥ
śrutimūlamidaṃtaṃtraṃpramāṇaṃkalpasūtravat 26
vedaśāstātparaṃ nāsti na daivaṃkeśavātparam
tasmādbhajasvadeveśaṃ (vidhinānena vaidvijāḥ) vidhinānena kevalam 27
taṃtranāma
ṛṣayaḥ
śravaṇārdhaṃ ṛṣi
katibhedāni taṃtrāṇi nāmadheyānikāni va
praśnaḥ
śrotumicchāmivipreṃdraparaṃ kautūhalaṃhinaḥ 28
vasiṣṭhaḥ
vasiṣṭha vacanaṃ taṃtranāma saṃkīrtanaṃ
śata meka mathāṣṭau ca taṃtresmi nviditaṃ mayā
nāmadheyānicaiteṣāṃ śṛṇvaṃtu susamāhitāḥ 29
mārkaṃḍeyaṃ pārṣadaṃ ca nāradīyaṃca saṃhitā
viśvāmītraṃ vainateyaṃ saṃhitā garuḍasya ca 30
kāśyapaṃ paiṃgalākhyaṃca vāsiṣṭhaṃpuṣkarāhvayaṃ
satvākhyaṃ manakākhyaṃ ca viṣṇusiddhāṃta saṃhitā 31
pādmaṃ padmodbhavaṃ jñeyaṃ vihageṃdraṃ ca vāruṇaṃ
nalakūbara māgneyaṃ vāmanaṃ śaunakaṃ tathā 32
pāvikaṃ pāra maiśvaryaṃ pārāśarya mataḥ param
viṣvaksenaṃ(khageṃdraṃca)khageśaṃ ca vīramāṃgalikaṃ tathā 33
śrīviṣṇutilakaṃ caiva lakṣmītilaka saṃhitā
daśottaraṃ ca śāṃḍilyaṃ mārīcasya ca saṃhitā 34
maudgalaṃ romaśaṃ caiva vāmadevaṃ subodhakam
meru gaṃgā ca satyoktamaiṃdraṃ paramapūruṣam 35
nārasiṃhaṃ hayagrīvaṃ dūrvāsaṃ kṛṣṇasaṃhitā
puṣṭitaṃtraṃ mahātaṃtraṃ puruṣottama saṃhitā 36
brahmāṃḍaṃ cāpikaumāra mīśvaraṃ matsyasaṃhitā
bhāradvājaṃyājñyavalkyaṃgajeṃdraṃmanusaṃhitā 37
śrīpuṣkaramahālakṣmī kuśalānaṃda pāvanāḥ
paṃcapraśnaśriyaḥpraśnaṃ prahlādaṃsātvataṃtataḥ 38
kapiṃjalaṃ ca brahmoktaṃ agastyaṃ jaiminaṃ tathā
viṣṇuvaibhavikaṃ sauraṃ saumyaṃ hārīti mevaca 39
kātyāyanaṃ ca vālmīkaṃ hairaṇyaṃ kāpilaṃ tadhā
jābālaṃ vāyavīyaṃ tu vāruṇāṃgīrasaṃ tathā 40
nārāyaṇaṃ ca vyāsākhyaṃ brahmanārada saṃhitā
dattātreyaṃ kaṇvagārgya saṃhitā kalirāghavam 41
prācetasaṃ pauṣkaraṃ ca (madhusūdana saṃhitā)mathusaṃgraha saṃhitā
saṃvādamiṃtraśukrābhyāmumāmāheśvarāhvayam 42
bodhāyana manaṃtaṃ ca vārāhaṃ ca nanaṃdanam
pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ caiva vāsudevasya saṃhitā 43
saṃkarṣaṇaṃ bhṛguścaiva gāṃdharva gaṇasaṃhitā
nāradottaravijñāna mevamaṣṭottaraṃ śatam 44
anuktamanyato grāhyameva
anukta manyato grāhyaṃ saṃhitāsu parasparam
ācāryaṃ varayetpūrvaṃyajamānaḥprayatnavān
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi puruṣottama
saṃhitāyāṃ śāstrāvatāravidhirnāma
prathamodhyāyaḥ
<2>
oṃ
śrīmatpāṃcarātra divyāgame śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
dvitīyodhyāyaḥ
ācāryādi lakṣaṇa bhūparīkṣāvidhiḥ
ṛṣayaḥ
ācāryādi lakṣaṇa śravaṇārthaṃ ṛṣi praśnaḥ
maunīṃdra bhavatā prokta mācāryaṃ varaye diti
kīdṛśo yajamānastukimācāryasya lakṣaṇam 1
lakṣaṇaṃ vada cāsmāka mācāryayajamānayoḥ
śrīvasiṣṭhaḥ
śrīvasiṣṭha prativacanaṃ ācārya lakṣaṇaṃ
brāhmaṇaḥkṣatriyovaiśyaśśūdrovāpyanulomajaḥ 2
śraddhābhaktisamāyukto dhanāḍhyo dharmatatparaḥ
yajamānaḥprasannātmā devayāgārtha mādarāt 3
ācāryaṃ varaye tpūrvaṃ tatadhīnā hi siddhayaḥ
pāṃcarātravidaṃ śāṃtaṃ siddhāṃteṣu kṛtaśramam 4
bhagavadvaṃśajaṃ śuddha malolupa madāṃbhikam
mahābhāgavata śreṣṭha (mekapatnīdṛḍhavratam) mekapatnīvrataṃ śubham 5
brāhmaṇaṃ satyasaṃpannaṃ sadācārasamanvitam
sarvāvayava(saṃpannaṃ)sauṃdaryaṃ yuvānaṃ vedavedinam 6
sarvaśāstrārthatatvajña magnihotradharaṃ śucim
utpattisthitisaṃhāra vedinaṃ brahmavādinam 7
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
ślā śraddhābhaktisamāyuktaṃ prapannaṃ dīkṣitaṃgurum
paropakāranirataṃ yogavidyākṛtaśramam
śāṃtaṃdāṃtaṃkīrtimaṃtaṃ sarvasadguṇa śobhitam
pāpadūraṃ prasannātma maṣṭākṣaraparāyaṇam]
pratiṣṭhākarmatatvajñaṃ saṃdhyopāsanatatparam
ācāryavaraṇa kramaḥ
evaṃ lakṣaṇaṃsaṃyukta mācāryaṃ paraye ttataḥ 8
samaye yajamānastugāṃ bhuvaṃ kāṃcanaṃ tathā
nārikelaṃca karmādāvācāryāya pradāvayet 9
bhūparīkṣā
ācāryo yajamānaśca devāgārasya siddhaye
bhūparīkṣādikaṃ kuryāt (śāstroktenatuvartmanā)śāstradṛṣṭena vartmanā 10
vasudhābheda kathanam yogyāyogya vasudhā nirūpaṇaṃ
supadmābhadrakā pūrṇādhūmrāceti caturvidhā
ādyastistraḥparigrāhyāścāṃtyā styājyādvijottamāḥ 11
kuśadarbhaissamākīrṇā nityaṃ toyena saṃyutā
supadmālakṣaṇaṃ
hariṇoyatra tiṣṭhaṃti sā supadmeti kīrtitā 12
bhadrakā lakṣaṇaṃ
phalino yājñikā vṛkṣā latāgulmasamanvitāḥ
pūrṇā lakṣaṇaṃ
dṛśyaṃte bhadrakā sā ca pūrṇeti parikīrtyate 13
dhūmrā lakṣaṇaṃ
adhojalasamāyuktā gireśśikhara māśritā
vāyasaśyenagṛdhrāḍhyā kaṭhinā vṛkasaṃkulā 14
ūsarakṣetra saṃyuktā sādhūmreti prakīrtitā
dhūmrāṃcavarjayedhbhūmiṃ bhadrakādiṣukārayet 15
khananavidhiḥ
jānumātraṃ khanedbhūmiṃmṛdamudhṛtyapūrayet
uttamābhūmiḥ
pāṃsurabhyadhiko yatrasābhūmiścottamāmatā 16
madhyamā adhamā karṣaṇaṃ bījāvāpaḥ bījāvāpena grāhyāgrāhyabhū nirūpaṇaṃ
samatve madhyamā caiva hīnā syā dadhamā matau
samyakparīkṣyabahudhākarṣayellāṃgalaiḥkramāt 17
vāvaye ttila mudgāṃśca yavanīvārasarṣapān
adhavā sarvadhānyāni yājñīkāni samaṃtataḥ 18
trirātrā dadhirohaṃti uttamā bhūmirucyate
aprarūḍhena yābhūmiḥ prayatnenavivarjayet 19
phalakāle tu sasyānāṃ gavāṃtṛptiṃ ca kārayet
tatastu lokapālāṃśca pūjaye ddeśikottamaḥ 20
lājaiḥ phalairapūpaiśca niśīdhetu baliṃkṣipet
raktagaṃdhaiścapuṣpaiścakumudādīṃśca pūjayet 21
bhūpūjāvāstu pūjādikaṃ
(tatobhūmiṃ samabhyarcya)bhūdevīṃca samabhyarcya vāstupūjāṃ ca kārayet
śāṃtihomaṃtataḥkṛtvārātraujāgaraṇaṃcaret 22
tataśca vimale śuddhe prabhāte kṛtakṛtyavān
(puṇyāhaṃcayitvātu)puṇyāhaṃtuprakurvīta brāhmaṇānbhojayettataḥ 23
punaḥkhananaṃ
kṣetraṃ tatkhātaye tpaścā dājalāṃtaṃ samaṃtataḥ

tajjale varuṇaṃ pūjya śilāśaṃkuṃca kārayet 24
śaṃkusthāpana vidhiḥ
tacchaṃkuṃ trividhaṃ kṛtvā cā dhastā ccaturaṃ tathā
tanmadhyecāṣṭaphalakaṃtadagraṃ kamalākṛtiḥ 25
evaṃ lakṣaṇa saṃyuktaṃ śaṃkuṃ saṃpūjaye ttataḥ
rakṣāsūtreṇa saṃveṣṭyagarte ratnādikaṃ nyaset 26
kūrmayaṃtraṃ pratiṣṭhāvya yaṃtraṃ saudarśanaṃmahat
tasyopari ca vinyanyaśilāśaṃkuṃ niveśayet 27
pūraṇa vidhiḥ
prathame saikataṃ pūrya dvitīye śarkarāstathā
dhātrīphalaṃ tṛtīyetu caturthe jaṃbukāphalaṃ 28
paṃcametu kapitthaṃca nārikelaṃ tu ṣaṣṭhake
saptame tu phalaṃ bilvametāva tprastarāvadhi 29
saṃpūrya cāṣṭabhāgeca paṃkilaṃ pūrayettataḥ
caturaśraṃ samaṃ kuryā dastipādena ghaṭṭayet 30
śaṃku mevaṃ pratiṣṭhāvya cālayaṃtu samārabhet
vibhavānuguṇenaiva kārayedālayonnatim 31
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
ācāryādilakṣaṇa bhūparīkṣāvidhirnāma
dvitiyodhyāyaḥ
<3>
oṃ
śrīmatpāṃcarātradivyāgame śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
tṛtīyodhyāyaḥ
ālaya nirmāṇaphalakathanavidhiḥ
ṛṣayaḥ
devālaya nirmāṇa phalaśravaṇārthaṃ ṛṣi praśnaḥ
devālayaṃ vinirmāya devaṃ saṃsthāpya bhaktitaḥ
pūjāṃ kārayitu stasya puṇyamācakṣvabhomune 1
vasiṣṭha prati vacanaṃ devālaya nirmāṇa phalaśrutiḥ
vasiṣṭhaḥ
vāsudevā dyālayasya kṛtau vakṣye phalādikam
cikīrṣo rdevadhāmādi sahasrajanipāpanut 2
manasā sadmakartṝṇāṃ śatajanmāghanāśanam
yetu modaṃti kṛṣṇasya kriyamāṇaṃ narāgṛham 3
tepi pāpai rvimucyaṃte prāpnu vaṃti parāṃ gatim
namatītaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ ca kulānā mayutaṃ naraḥ 4
viṣṇulokaṃ nayatyāśu kārayitvā harekgṛham
vasaṃti pitaro dṛṣṭvā viṣṇuloke hyalaṃkṛtāḥ 5
vimuktā nārakairdukhaiḥ kartuḥ kṛṣṇasya maṃdiram
brahmahatyādipāpaughaghātakaṃ viṣṇatamaṃdiram 6
phalaṃ yannāpyateyajñairdhāma kṛtvāta dāpyate
devāgāre kṛte sarva tīrthasnānaphalaṃ labhet 7
devā dyaddehatanāṃ ca raṇe yattatphalādikam
śāṭhyeva prāsūnā cāpi kṛtaṃ dhānu ca nākatham 8
ekāyatanakṛtsvargītyragārī brahmalokabhāk
paṃcāgārī śaṃbhuloka maṣṭhāgārā ddharau sthitiḥ 9
ṣoḍaśālayakārītu bhaktimukti mavāpnuyāt
kaniṣṭhaṃmadhyamaṃśreṣṭhaṃkārayitvāharergṛham 10
svargaṃca vaiṣṇavaṃ lokaṃmokṣa māpnotica kramāt
śreṣṭhamāyatanaṃ viṣṇoḥkṛtvāyaddhanavān labhet 11
kaniṣṭhenaiva tatpuṇyaṃ prāpno tyaddhanavānnaraḥ
samutpādyadhanaṃ kṛtvānvalpe nāpisurālayam 12
kārayitvāhareḥ puṇyaṃ saṃprāpno tyadhikaṃvaram
lakṣeṇā pi sahasreṇa śat nārdhna vā hareḥ 13
kāraṇaṃ bhavanaṃ yāti ta trāsai garuḍadhvajaḥ
bolyetu krīḍamānā ye pāṃsubhirbhavanaṃ hareḥ 14
vāsudenasya kurvaṃti pitaro lokagāminaḥ
tīrthe cāyatane puṇye siddhakṣetreta thāśrame 15
kartu rāyatanaṃ viṣṇo ryathoktā triguṇaṃ phalam
devālaye sammārjanādi phala viśeṣaḥ
baṃdhūkapuṣpavinyāsai ssudhāsaṃkena vaiṣṇavam 16
ye vilipyaṃti bhavanaṃ te yāṃti bhagavatpuram
patitaṃ patamānaṃ tu tadārthapatitaṃ naraḥ 17
samudhṛtya harerdhāma prāpnoti dviguṇaṃ phalam
patitasya tu yaḥkartā patitasya ca rakṣitā 18
viṣṇo rāyatana syha na naro viṣṇulokabhāk
iṣṭakāni ca yattiṣṭhe dyā vadāyatanaṃ hareḥ 19
sa kula stasya vaikartā viṣṇuloke mahīyate
sa evapuṇyavān npūjya ihalokeparatraca 20
kṛṣṇasya vāsudevasya yaḥ kārayati ketanam
jāta ssa eva sakṛtaḥ kulaṃ te naiva pālitam 21
viṣṇurudrārka devyādergṛhakartā na kīrtibhāk
kiṃ tasya vittanicayai rmūḍhanya parirakṣiṇaḥ 22
duḥkhārjitai ryaḥ kṛṣṇasya nakārayati ketanam
nopabhogya dhanaṃ yasya pitṛvipradivaukasām 23
nopabhaugāya baṃdhūnāṃ vyarthastanya dhanāgamaḥ
yathā dhruvo nṛṇāṃmṛtyuḥvittanāśastato dhruvaḥ 24
mūḍha statrānubadhnāti jīvite dha ca vaidhane
yathāvittaṃ na dānāya ropabhogāya dehinām 25
nāpi kīrtyai nadharmarthaṃ tasya svā myedhako guṇaḥ
tasmā dvittaṃ samāsādyadaivādvāpauruṣā dathā 26
dadyā tsamyagoddvijāgrebhyaḥkīrtanāni ca kārayet
dānebhyaścādhikaṃ yasmātkīrtanebhyo varaṃyataḥ 27
ata statkālaye ddhīmān viṣṇvāde rmaṃdirādikam
vinivesya harerdhāmā bhaktimadbhirnarottamaiḥ 28
nivesitaṃ bhavetkṛcchraṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram
bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyaṃcasthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃtathetaram 29
ābrahmastaṃbhaparyaṃtaṃ sarvaṃ viṣṇo ssamudbhavam
tasya devādi devasya sarvaganya mahātmanaḥ 30
nivestya bhagavan viṣṇorna bhūyo bhuvijāyate
yadā viṣṇordāmakṛtau phalaṃtadvaddivaukasām 31
pratimākaraṇe phalakathanaṃ
śivabrahmārka vighneśa caṃḍīlakṣmyādi kātmanām
devālayakṛteḥ puṇyaṃ pratimākara ṇe'dhikam 32
pratimāsthāpane yāge phalasyāṃto na vidyate
mṛṇmayāddāruje puṇyaṃ dārujā daiṣṭakebhavet 33
yiṣṭakoddhācchailajesyāt hemāde rathikaṃ phalam
saptajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ prāraṃbhādevanasyati 34
devālayasya svargī syāt narakaṃ naiva gacchati
kāraya nbhagavaddhāma naya tyacyutalokatām 35
saptalokamayo viṣṇustasya yaḥ kurute gṛham
tārayatyakṣatān lokānakṣayāspratipadyate 36
iṣṭakācaya vinyāco yāvaṃ tyabdāni tiṣṭhati
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi tatkarturdivi saṃsthitiḥ 37
iti śrīpāṃcarātr mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyā
mālayanirmāṇa phalakathanaṃ nāma
tṛtīyodhyāyaḥ
<4>
oṃ
śrīmatpāṃcarātradivyāgame śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
caturthodhyāyaḥ
ālayalakṣaṇamūrdhneṣṭakāvidhānam
ālaya nirmāṇa vidhiḥ
vasiṣṭhaḥ
devālayapramāṇāni śvaṇudhvaṃmunisattamāḥ
uttamaṃ prastarā dārūmadhyamaṃ ceṣṭakāsmṛtam 1
ete ṣvekatame naiva kālaye dbhagavadgṛham
prasdharavarṇa phala nirūpaṇaṃ
prastharāṇāṃ ca varṇāni vakṣyāmi munipuṃgavāḥ 2
raktavarṇaṃ ca saubhāgyaṃ pītavarṇaṃ mahābhayam
dhūmravarṇaṃ vyādhirūpaṃ gauraṃ ca maraṇaṃ bhavet 3
śvetavarṇaṃ bhave tpuṇyaṃ nīla māyuṣyavardhanam
māṇikyaṃ mṛtyunāśaṃca ābhrakaṃmokṣadāyakam 4
ciṃtāmaṇiśilā brahman kartu riṣṭaśubhaṃ bhavet
pādenaliṃga nirṇaya vidhiḥ
kurviṃdaṃ catu saubhāgyaṃ varṇānyetāni nirṇayaḥ 5
puruṣaṃ tālanādaṃ ca veṇunādaṃ napuṃsakam
ghaṃṭānādi priyatamā i tyete liṃganirṇayam 6
diṃṇādiṣuyogyanāda nirūpaṇaṃ brāhmaṇā divarjāni
ghaṃṭānādaṃ ca biṃbānāṃ pīṭhānāṃ veṇunādakam
tālaṃ devālayānāṃ ca śilāsu krama īritaḥ 7
śvetaṃ dvijātivarṇaśca raktaṃ kṣatriyavarṇakam
pītaṃ vaiśya midaṃvarṇa mitarān śūdravarṇakān 8
saṃgṛhyatu śilā sūktāśśilpinaṃ pūjaye tsudhīḥ
aṃgulādimāna vidhiḥ
madhyamāṃgulimadhyaṃ ca aṃguṣṭhavalayārdhakam 9
yavatrayāṇāṃ mānaṃ ca aṃgulasya pramāṇataḥ
aṃgulairdvādaśairyuktastāla i tyabhidhīyate 10
garbhālayaṃ
ida metena tālena dviguṇaṃ hasta mucyate
aṣṭādaśakaraiḥ kāryaṃ garbhāyāmaṃ ca vissṛtiḥ 11
madhyamaṃ daśabhirhasai radhamaṃpaṃcahastakam
upānahādipaṃcāṃga madhiṣṭhānaṃ prākalbayt 12
paṃcahastā caturhastā dvihastāvā yathecchayā
daṃḍastadardhaṃ pādonaṃtasyotsredhaṃ samīritam 13
adhiṣṭhāne kṛte tasmin staṃbha maṃta rvivarjayt
ekadvitricaturhastaiḥkramaśovardhitā bhavet 14
maṃdirasya mahānāsā saktisālasamucchrayaḥ
upapīṭhasamāyuktāsālo daṃḍa strīrusyate 15
kalpanīyaṃ prayatnena tadaṃga syārdha maṃṭapam
grīvāgratulito vāsyāt pratisammitayevavā 16
vicchinnā saṃtare teṣāṃ sālānā mucchrayaṃ viduḥ
etadgarbhagṛhaṃproktaṃ (purato'syārdhamaṃṭapam) puratassyārdhamaṃṭapam 17
ardhamaṃṭapam
paṃcahastaṃ caturhastaṃ kāraye dardhamaṃṭapam
hīne'rthemaṃṭapedhāmnaḥpārśvābhyāṃnirgamastataḥ 18
āgrataḥ pṛṣṭato vāpi bhavetāṃ pārśvavartmani
samucchritaṃ pratisamaṃ adhikaṃ vāyadhecchayā 19
tāva dvistārayuktā śca diguṇaṃ cobhayo rbhavet
sopānaṃ syātsamuddiṣṭamaṃtaścāṃtaścanirgamaḥ 20
sopānapaṅtkayaḥ kāryā ṣṣaḍaṃgulasamucchrayāḥ
caṃḍaścaiva pracaṃḍaśca kalpayeddvārapārmvayoḥ 21
nṛtta maṃṭapam
tadagre maṃṭapaṃ kuryāt nṛtyārthaṃ dvijasattamāḥ
ṣaṭṭriṃśastaṃbhasaṃyuktaṃ ṣoḍaśastaṃbha mevavā 22
samucchritaṃ pratisamaṃ adhikaṃ vā yathecchayā
ekadvitricatuḥpaṃcahastai rmuttārdhamaṃṭapam 23
sāṃtarālaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ pārśvābhyā madha nirgamaḥ
sopānapaṅtayaḥ kāryāḥ pārśvayorubhayorapi 24
tāva dvistārayuktā śca dviguṇa ścobhayaṃ bhavet
sopānapaṅtkayohasti hastaṃ kuryāsthaviyasaḥ 25
yadvā rathāṃgaṃ kurvīta siṃhavyāla ma thāpiva
aṃtarāvaraṇaṃ kuryāt nṛttamaṃṭapamadhyataḥ 26
dvāraṃ vā tāyanaṃ vāpi bhittibaṃdhaṃ na jātuvā
kulakṣayo vināśaṃca vyāthi rvā bhittibaṃdhane 27
yadvārdhamaṃṭapā dardha sapādaṃ pāda meva vā
tattulyāvāṃtarālaṃ tu aṃtarālāya taṃ bhavet 28
vistāraṃ hrāsayettatta aṃtarālasya pārśvayoḥ
hastaṃ tālaṃtadhardhaṃvāhrāsayetpārśvayorapi 29
yugmastaṃbhasamāyukta stathābaṃdha mathāpi vā
lāṃgalaṃ vā prakurvīta ubhayaṃ vā vinā bhavet 30
caturaśraṃ vā yataṃ vā caturdvāraṃ caturdiśam
vātāyanaṃ sayugmaṃtunakuryādbhittibaṃdhanam 31
aṃtarāvaraṇasyāṃta rbhahi ryobhayapārśvayoḥ
kalpaye cchilpaśāstrajño garuḍāḥ kkoṇabhūmiṣu 32
ha stabaṃdhaṃ tadūrdhvaṃtu agrenaikaṃprakalpayet
prākāra maṃṭapāni
prākāreṣu ca sarveṣu maṃṭapāni samaṃtataḥ 33
dvāviṃśatidhanurmānaṃ maṃṭapaṃ cottamaṃ bhavet
aṣṭādaśadhanurmānaṃ maṃṭapaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet 34
adhamaṃ dvādaśadhanu rmaṃṭapaṃ paricakṣate
āsthānī maṃṭapaṃ
pūrvoktasaptasaṃkhyā vābhavedāsthāna maṃṭapam 35
uttamaṃ daśabhirhastairmadhyamaṃ cāṣṭa hastakaiḥ
ṣaḍbhistamadhamaṃviṃdyāt kṣutraṃhastatrayāyutam 36
dakṣiṇottarayoḥpṛṣṭhe bhittiyasyuḥ prakalpitāḥ
staṃbhāṣṭakayutaṃ tatrasopānaṃ parikalpayet 37
dīrghaśālā madhaḥkuryāt maṃṭapaṃ caiva kārayet
maṃṭapāḥ prāṅmukhāssarve sarvāvaraṇa saṃsthitāḥ 38
āsthāna maṃṭapānsarvān prakuryātsarvatomukhān
somasūtraṃ
śūdraḥpratiṣṭhitaṃ kuryāt somasūtrasyalakṣaṇam 39
pādavedyāṃ śilāyāṃ ca makarāsana mācaret
vaiśyaḥkapota vedyāṃca kuryādgajamukhākṛtiḥ 40
kṣatriyaḥ paṭṭikāyāṃca mṛgādhipaśirākṛtiḥ
dvijodvipaṭṭikāyāṃca kuryādgomukhavaddvijāḥ 41
garbhālaya dvārabhedena phalabheda nirūpaṇaṃ
evaṃ garbhagṛhāyāmaṃ tasya dvāraṃ vidhīyate
prāgdvāraṃ śubhadaṃ proktaṃ dakṣiṇe puṣṭivardhanam 42
paścime vijayaṃ caiva dhanadaṃ cottaraṃ bhavet
uttuṃgaṃ navatālaiśca vistṛtiḥ paṃcabhirbhavet 43
vimānāni ca bhidyaṃte talādhiṣṭhānabhedataḥ
prāsāda lakṣaṇaṃ
prāsādaṃ paṃcadhā bhedaṃ nāgaraṃ drāviḍaṃ tathā 44
vṛttaṃ vṛttāyataṃ caiva vesaraṃca prakīrtitāḥ
nāgaraṃ
sarvadiṅnāsikākāraṃ kūṭaśālāsamanvitam 45
ādyeca caturaśraṃtu cāgre vṛttāyataṃ yadi
nāgalaṃ tatsamākhyātaṃ drāviḍaṃ cādhunocyate 46
drāviḍaṃ
pādādisthūpiparyaṃtaṃ caturaśraṃ prakalpayet
ṣaḍaśramadhavākuryātyuktaṃtaddrāviḍaṃbhavet 47
vesaraṃ
pādādica śiroṃtaṃ tu vṛttaṃ vṛttāyataṃ tu vā
vesarākhyamiti prāktaṃ maṃdireṣu yathākramam 48
kūṭaśālāsamāyuktaṃ sarvadiṅmāsikāyutam
kaṃṭhordhvatalavistīrṇaṃ vesaraṃ maṃdiraṃ smṛtam 49
sarvato nirgamaṃ
podaprabhṛtisthūpyaṃtaṃ aṣṭāśraṃ dvāvaśāśrakam
yatkūṭaśālārahitaṃ sarvato nirgama ssmṛtaḥ 50
mūrdheṣṭakāvidhiḥ
mūrdheṣṭakāvidhiṃ brahman śṛṇuvakṣyāmi sāṃpratam
ālayasya purobhāge kāraye dyāgamaṃṭapam 51
vedikāṃ kāraye nmadhye ekadvitrisuhastakam
sarvālaṃkāra saṃyuktaṃ toraṇairupaśobhitam 52
sādhitāḥpūrvarātrau cacatvāraḥ prastareṣṭakāḥ
śilādoṣavimuktāsyuḥ supakvāmṛṇmayā dhavā 53
paṃcagavyena saṃprokṣya paristīrya kuśai stathā
puṇyāhaṃvācayi tvādhaprokṣayeccakuśāṃbubhiḥ 54
kautukaṃ baṃdhaye ttatra mūlamaṃtreṇa maṃtravit
pratyekaṃ vastramāsādya sthāpayettu tadagrataḥ 55
sādayetkalaśānpaścāt savastrān sāsidhānakān
paṃcakuṃbhā nvinikṣiṣya madhye kaṃbhaṃtu vinyaneta 56

gaṃdhodakena saṃpūrṇaṃkuśai raśvaddhapallavaiḥ
mūlamaṃtreṇa kuṃbhāstusādayetsādhakottamaḥ 57
kumudaḥ kumudākṣaśca puṃḍarī kodha vāmanaḥ
śaṃkhukarṇa ssarpanetra ssumukhaḥ ssupratiṣṭhitaḥ 58
paritaḥ kalaśā nyaṣṭau pūjaye ddeśikottamaḥ
vārāhaṃnārasiṃhaṃca śrīdharaṃhayaśīrṣakam 59
jāmadagnyaṃ ca rāmaṃca vāmanaṃ vāsudevakam
evaṃ vidyeśvarāḥproktāḥkramātkuṃbheṣu pūjayet 60
sakūrcaṃsādhaye dvidvān sarvālaṃkārasaṃyutam
ārabheta tato homaṃ vedīpārśvetu deśikaḥ 61
dikṣvaṣṭāsu tathādhāmmi śāṃtihomaṃ pravartayet
vedapārāyaṇai ssārthaṃ paṭhadbhiśśākunaṃ prati 62
ṛgvedaṃ pūrvadigbhāge yajurvedaṃ tu dakṣiṇe
paścime sāmavedaṃ tu adharvaṃcottare tathā 63
jūhuyā tsamidhājyena caruṇā payasā tadhā
aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā samidhājyaiḥ pṛthakpṛthak 64
saṃpātādyaṃgṛhītvātupūrṇāhuti mathācaret
rātriśeṣaṃ vyatikamya kṛtasnānaḥ kṛtāhnikaḥ 65
pūrvava tpūjaye tkuṃbhān gaṃdhapuṣpādhibhistataḥ
pradīpaye ddhūpadīpai rarcaye ddeśikāttamaḥ 66
balidānaṃ prakurvīta yathāva danupūrvaśaḥ
ācāryo takṣakai ssārdhaṃ yajamānasamanvitaḥ 67
(vedaghoṣa purassaram)dhāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryādvedaghoṣasamanvitaḥ
āropayeccha tāssarvāḥ vimānasyoparisthale 68
puṇyāhaṃ vācaye ttatra ṛtvigbhissaha deśikaḥ
murtheṣṭakānāmādhātāprāṅmukhastunamāhitaḥ 69
dhyāyannārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ hṛtpadmepraṇavaṃjapet
adadyādiṣṭakā ssarvāḥ pūrvādikramamāśritaḥ 70
praṇamya daṃḍava dhbhūmau yajamāno guruṃ punaḥ
vastraiśca dhanadhānyaiśca gobhūmikanakaistadhā 71
paṃcāṃga bhūṣaṇaṃ dadyāddāsīdāsāṃ stadhaiva ca
toṣaye drathakārāṃśca vastrānna dhanadhānyakaiḥ 72
sthūpikākīlavidhiḥ
stūpikākīla madhunā vakṣyāmi kamalānana
lohajaṃ dārujaṃ dā pi sthūpikākīla miṣyate 73
vimānakaṃṭhatulita matyarthaṃ dviguṇaṃ tu vā
yugmaṃ syāsthūpikākīla mapunarbhava kāṃkṣiṇaḥ 74
ita reṣā mayugmaṃ syāt sthūpikākīlālpanam
pradoṣe samaruprāpte gartaṃ kuryāttu sthūpike 75
vṛttaṃ vā caturaśraṃ dā kārayedgarta muttamam
puṇyāhaṃ vāṃcayedgarte navaratnāni vinyaset 76
kṣiptvāgarte tata sthūpiṃ pāṅmukho mūlavidyayā
ācārya sthsāpaye sthūpīṃ paṃcāgākalpabhūṣitam 77
sudhayā sthūpikākīlaṃ dṛḍhaṃ kuryā danaṃtaram
śikhākuṃbhasthāpanaṃ
śikhākuṃbhaṃ suvarṇaṃ vā rājitaṃ tāmrame vavā 78
paittalaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ vāpi sthāpaye ttakṣaṇeśubhe
śikhākuṃbhasya śirasi sthāpaye dviṣṇucakrakam 79
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
ālayalakṣaṇa mūrtheṣṭakā vidhānaṃ nāma
caturthodhyāyaḥ
<5>
oṃ
śrī pāṃcarātradivyāgame śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
paṃcamodhyāyaḥ
śilāsaṃgrahaṇavidhiḥ
pratimā lakṣaṇa
brahmauvāca
śravaṇārthaṃ brahma praśnaḥ
bhagavan devadeveśa pratimālakṣaṇaṃ mama
suvistaraṃ yathā brūhi śrotu micchāmitatvataḥ 1
śrībhagavānuvāca
śrīphagavatprati vacanaṃ beṃba nirmāṇāvasara nirūpaṇaṃ
pratimāṃ kāraye tpūrvaṃ sādhaka ssusamāhitaḥ
surūpāṃ lakṣaṇopetāṃ devadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ 2
tasyāṃ jīvaṃ samāropya samārādhya na sīdati
nirākāre tu deveśe cittaṃ na ramate yataḥ 3
vigrahārādhana viśeṣaḥ
tasmā dākārava tpūrvaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ kṛdvicakṣaṇaḥ
ciṃtayan satataṃtaṃcacittasthairyamavāpnuyāt 4
sthire cetasi tatraiha kleśahāni mavāpnuyāt
kṣīṇakleśastu puruṣaḥ paraṃ nirvāṇa mṛcchati 5
virakti mūlatāṃ yāti tasmā darcāṃ kṛte sati
tasmā darcāṃ prakurvīta vittasālaṃbanāya vai 6
pratimādravya bhedaḥ
ratnaṃ lohaṃ śilā mṛcca dāruspaṭika meva ca
saddravyāṇi praśastāni pratimākaraṇedvija 7
śilāsaṃgrahaḥ śilāsaṃgrahaṇa sthalanirdeśaḥ
prathamaṃ tu śilāṃ vakṣye yeṣu śaileṣu dṛśyate
hemakūṭaśca niṣadho himavānnīlaparvataḥ 8
maṃdaro mālyavāṃ ścaiva trikūṭo malayācalaḥ
gaṃdhamādana meru śca da śaite sarvatottamāḥ 9
eteṣu pratimā grāhyā tadalābhe śilāṃ śṛṇu
bhūgatāyā śilā grāhyā tadalābhe śilāṃ śṛṇu 10
girau ca citrakūṭākhye raivatākhye mahāgirau
siṃhācale citrakūṭe veṃgaṭedardu retathā 11
tathāśvetagirau grāhyā tadalābhe śilāṃ śṛṇu
bhūgata śilābheda kathanaṃ
bhūgatā yāśilāproktāstāsāṃvakṣyāmilakṣaṇam 12
vāruṇecaiva māheṃdrī āgneyī vāyavī tathā
catasrastu śilāḥ proktāstāsāṃ lakṣaṇa mucyate 13
vāruṇī
yā puṣpavana saṃkīrṇā jalāśayasamāvṛtā
sākṣitī vāruṇī jñeyā tatratyā kāmadā śilā 14
māheṃdrī
yasyāṃ cottarata syoyaṃ vrīhikṣetraṃ tu dakṣiṇe
paścime kṣīravṛkṣāśca sā māheṃdrīti kirtitā 15
āgneyī
pālāśakhadirāśvadtha kāśmīraḥ pūrvadakṣiṇaḥ
tiṃtriṇyāścakapotāścagṛddhrāścaivatu vāyasāḥ 16
balāhakā bakāścaiva dṛśyaṃteyatra saṃtatam
āgneyīsākṣitījñeyātatradāḥ kāmadāśśilāḥ 17
vāyavī
toya maṃtargataṃ svalpaṃ khanane yatra dṛśyate
pilu śleṣmātakākīrṇa snudvibhītakasaṃyutā 18
tṛṇodakavihīnā ca śarkarotkara saṃyutā
sṛgālāyatra dṛśyaṃte tathaiva mṛgatṛṣṇakāḥ 19
vāyavī sākṣitijñeyā śilātatratu varjitā
varjarīya sthalāni
naditire kūpataṭe pūtibhūmau catuṣpade 20
valmīke pitṛbhūvau ca lavaṇodaka saṃkule
āmedhyabhūmau śabara caṃḍālādiniṣevite 21
devālayasamīpe ca tapte vā tātapādibhiḥ
deśe varjā śśilāssarvāḥ gṛhītāce danarthatā 22
grāhyapradeśāni
puṇyakṣetreśubhe deśe brahma vṛkṣaniraṃtare
kuśakāśodakayute mṛgaiḥkṛṣṇairniṣevite 23
padmotpalasamākīrṇa vrīhikṣetraniraṃtare
hiṃtālapūgapunnāga nārikelasamanvite 24
tapasvijanasaṃbaṃdhe deśe grāhyāśśilā ssadā
varjanīyaṃ vidi tvaivaṃ guṇadoṣau nirūpya ca 25
deśabhedāṃśca bhūbhedān liṃgabhedān stadhaivaca
tyājyaśilā saṃgrahe pratyavāyaḥ
jñātvaivaṃ lakṣaṇairyuktāṃgṛhṇīyācchubhadāṃśilām 26
varjitābhi śśilābhiryaḥ pratimān lobhamohitaḥ
kuryā dvākāraye dvāpibhave tasyābhikārakam 27
śilāyāṃbrāhmaṇādi jātibhedāḥ tallakṣaṇāśca
śilāścaturvidhā brahman varṇabhedaiśśilādibhiḥ
brāhmaṇasya śilāśubhrākṣatriyasyatupāṭalā 28
pītā vaiśya sya śūdrasya kṛṣṇā mukhyāyathoditā
brāhmaṇasya śilā strīsrastisrorājñastathaivaca 29
vaiśyasya dveca vijñeyā śūdra syākā prakīrtitā
śilāsaṃgrahe kāla viniyamanam śilā saṃgrahaṇa vidhiḥ
praśastaṃ sarvamāseṣu śilā saṃgrahaṇaṃ dvija 30
śakunādinimittānāṃ anukūlena cānvitaḥ
rathakāreṇa sahitā sthapatīnāṃ gaṇaissahā 31
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvātukṛtvāṃ tesvastivācakam
nirgata stu śilāṃ paśyt tadā prabhrṛti nityaśaḥ 32
aṃkurārpaṇa kuṃbha sthāpanādi
śilāsaṃgrahaṇe deśe kāraye nmahatīṃ prapām
kṛtvāṃkurārpaṇaṃtatrabrahmacārīhaviṣyabhuk 33
ācāryo yajamānena sārthaṃ tatra vicakṣaṇaḥ
kuṃbhaṃ saṃsthāpayettatramūlamaṃtreṇa sādhakaḥ 34
āvāhayettato devaṃ mūrtimaṃtreṇa sarvagam
pūṇyāhaṃvācayitpātu cakramudrāṃpradarśayet 35
mahāmudrāṃ pradarśyādha praṇamyāṃjalimudrayā
śilāyāṃmaṃḍalīkṛtvātatraviṣṇuṃ mudārcayet 36
kartu micchati yāṃ mūrtiṃ tanmaṃtreṇa samarcayet
arcayitvātudigdaivān bhutānkrūrabhaliṃkṣipet 37
balipradānam
palalaṃ rajanīcūrṇaṃ salājaṃ dadhisaktu ca
bhūtaṃ krūra mitiproktaṃ taina bhūtabaliṃharet 38
homaṃ
homaṃ kuryā ccaturdikṣu kuṃḍe vā sthaṃḍilepi vā
palāśasamidhaṃ puṣpaṃ phala mājyaṃ sapallavam 39
juhuyā nmūlamaṃtreṇa vṛthagaṣṭottaraṃ śatam
caruṇā ca nṛsūktena pṛthakkuṃḍa catuṣṭayam 40
nakuṃḍe juhuyā ttatra sarpaṣā vanadevatāḥ
pārṣadā naṃḍaśailāṃ śca taṭaśṛṃgāni jharjarān 41
samudrāṃśca tathā vṛkṣānoṣadhīśca vanaspatīḥ
jarāyujā naṃḍajāṃśca svedajānudbhidastathā 42
tathā sarvāṇi bhūtāni maṃttraistaddevatāpadaiḥ
svāhaṃtairjuhu yātpaścā (huvedādyāhutīstathā) ddunedvyāhṛtibhistathā 43
svapnārthaśayanaṃ
svapnādhivatimaṃtreṇa pṛthagaṣṭottaraṃ śatam
pūrṇāhutyaṃta makhilaṃ kṛtvākarma vicakṣaṇaḥ 44
upoṣya dharbhaśayyāyāṃ prabhāte ca jiteṃdriyaḥ
ācāryassusthilobhūtvāimaṃmaṃtramudīrayt 45
oṃnamaḥ ssarvalokāya viṣṇave prabhaviṣṇave
viśvāya viśvarūpāya svapnādhipataye samaḥ 46
śata maṣṭottaraṃ maṃtraṃ imaṃ japtyā svape dbudhaḥ
suptvā svapne tata śśailaṃyadivaśyetsamujvalam 47
pravṛddhaśikharaṃ tatra kāṃcanadrumabhūṣitam

phalapuṣpasamāyuktaṃ rūpetvaṃ śailajairdrumaiḥ 48
evaṃbhūte yadi svapnaṃ pratimā śobhanā bhavet
prāta retu śilāgrāhyā śāṃtiṃ kuryādadarmane 49
śīlāṃ muhūrte saṃpaśyet śobhane deśikottamaḥ
yakṣāḥpiśācā nāgādyāyatra tiṣṭhaṃti nityaśaḥ 50
sarvete pyapagacchaṃtu sannidhattāṃ sadā hariḥ
ityuktvā maṃtrasaṃsiddhāsiddhārthān tatra nikṣipet 51
tena maṃtreṇa bhūtebhyaḥ pratyāśaṃ nikṣipedbalim
puṇyāhaṃvācayitvātu pauruṣeṇa śilāṃspṛśet 52
mūlamaṃtreṇa datvārghyaṃ japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam
sūtrapātādīni
biṃbasya cānurūpeṇa pātaye tsūtramabjaja 53
śilāṃ saṃsthāpayet sthitvā rathakārasya śāsanāt
grīvabhedena phalabhedaḥ
dakṣiṇottara māyāmaṃ tathā vaipūrvapaścimam 54
uttare dakṣiṇagrīvā pūrvagrīvā jayapradā
śrīkarī paśmimagrīvā kartrurbhavati nityaśaḥ 55
pūṣṭidā cottaragrīvā koṇagrīvātu varjitā
mūlabhāgaśilā yātu upariṣṭhā dbhavetsadā 56
adhasthā cche cchirobhāgo vipatyo netra daṃṣṭrayoḥ
mahākuṃbhasthatoyenasnāpayetsaṃbhṛtāṃśilām 57
mahākuṃbha snapanādi
svarṇagobhūtilāṃdatvā śāṃtihomaṃ samācaret
utpāṭyamānedṛśyaṃtesphuliṃgā yatracāgnivat 58
nādenavarcanīyaśilā nirūpaṇaṃ
nādaśca kāṃsyaghaṃṭāca etacchinhāni varjayet
kāṃsya vanmadhyabhāgetumūle vaikāṃsyatāḍane 59
nādenaliṃga nirūpaṇaṃ
dhpaniryasyā śsilāyāstu sapumā nitikīrtitaḥ
nyūna stasmā dhvaneḥkiṃcicchedane yatrasā ramā 60
śīlānādavihīnaṃtu napuṃsaka mudāhṛtam
strīpunnapuṃsaka śilānāmupayogāni garbhavatīśilāvarjanīyāṃ garbhaparīkṣā
pulliṃge pratimā proktā strīliṃge pādapīṭhikā 61
napuṃsakaśilāyāṃtu ratnanyāsa mudāhṛtam
garbhavatyaṃ śilā varjāgarbhāccāṃtarvyavasthitāḥ 62
tasmādgarbhaṃ parīkṣyeta cchedane bhedane pivā
navanītaṃ mahiṣajaṃ śṛṃgameṣasya cūrṇitam 63
cūrṇaṃ ca kuraviṃdasya saṃpeṣya payasā gavām
śilāyāṃ sarvato liṃpedgarbhavyaktistato bhavet 64
maṃḍalā yatra dṛśyaṃte tatra garbhaṃ vinikṣipet
māṃjiṣṭhavarṇasadṛśe maṃḍale darduro bhavet 65
pītake maṃḍalegodhā kṛṣṇekṛṣṇāhi reva ca
kapile mūṣikā proktākṛkalānastuvāruṇi 66
guḍavarṇtu pāṣāṇaḥ taṃḍule gṛhagaulikā
aṃbhaḥkṛpāṇasadṛśe vālukāpadmasannibhe 67
vicittevṛścikaṃjñeyaḥ nīle vīte pataṃgakaḥ
madhuvarṇetu khadyto maṃḍale nirdiśe dbudhaḥ 68
garbhabhedaphalāni
dardureśyā danārogyaṃ godhāyā durbhayobhavet
viṣeṇa mriyyate sarpiranapatyastumūṣake 69
kṛkalāse tu cālpāyuḥ pāṣāṇe śaninā hataḥ
gaulikāyāṃ dhanāpāyo jalevai garbhanāśanam 70
śikate tu jalāpāyaḥ vṛścike kalahastathā
śalabhe rāṣṭhranāśasvāt khadyotetu kulakṣayam 71
tasmādyatnā tparīkṣyeta garphaṃ nitya mataṃdritaḥ
garbhiṇī śilāsaṃgrahe pratyavāyaḥ
garbhayuktāṃśilāṃmohātpratimāṃkriyateyadi 72
tanmaṃḍalādhipo rājā kāraka stāpaka stathā
nīmilitā bhavaṃ tyete tasmādgarbhaṃ parīkṣayet 73
śrīyādibiṃbanirmāṇa viṣaye śilā saṃgralaṇe viśeṣaḥ
devināṃ ca ramādīnāṃ nirmāṇe strīśilā varā
puṃśśilā pādapīṭhaṃ syā dratnanyāsonapuṃsake 74
devī biṃbavinirmāṇe kuṃbhamadhye vicakṣaṇaḥ
āvāhya mūlamaṃtreṇaśriyaṃ trailokyamātaram 75
homādi pūrvavatkuryāt śrīsūktena tu sādhāḥ
astriyā śilayā devī nirmitā ce tpramādataḥ 76
lobhayuktena marasā sa nāśa madhigacchati
parivāra śilāsaṃgrahaḥ
tatraiva deśe saṃgrāhy parivāraśilā śśubhāḥ 77
śilāsaṃgrahaṇaṃ teṣāṃ (svasyamaṃtrairyadhākramam) kuryānmaṃtrairyadhā kramam
ācāryadacakṣiṇāṃ datyāt yathāvibhavavistaram 78
śilāṃ gṛhitvātāṃyatnā dāropya śakaṭādiṣu
nītvā dhāmni sthapatayo rathakāramatānugāḥ 79
kuryurarcāṃ yadhāśāstraṃ coditairlakṣaṇairyutam
iti śrīpāṃcarātremahopaniṣadi puruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
śilāsaṃgrahaṇavidhānaṃ nāma paṃcamodhyāyaḥ
śrīśrīśrī
<6>
oṃ
śrīpāṃcarātraśāstre śrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
ṣaṣṭhodhyāyaḥ
dārusaṃgrahaṇavidhiḥ
ratnapratimā
nirdoṣā ratnajā ssarvā ssarvakāmaphalapradāḥ
sphāṭikā pratimā yatra sā saubhāgyavivardhanī
lohajebhedāni suvarṇa pratimā phalaṃ
hiraṇyaṃ rajataṃ tāmraṃ trividhaṃ loha mucyate 1
hiraṇmayī prabhā neti sarvasaṃpatkarī bhavet
vittaṃ yaśa śca vibhavo vijñānaṃ rājate phalam 2
rājatapratimā phalaṃ
dhanaputrapradā kartu stāmrajā pratimā śubhā
vṛkṣajā śrīkarījñeyā mṛṇmayī sarvakāmadā 3
tāmrajā phalaṃ
kārtikādyaṣṭamāseṣu praśasto dārusaṃgrahaḥ
dārumṛṇmayādiṣu phalāni
praśastaṃ pakṣanakṣatraṃ yogayukte śubhe dine 4
aguru rdevadāru śca rājavṛkṣa śca caṃdanam
dārusaṃgrahaṇa kālaḥ
bilvakāmronameru śca niṃbaḥ khadira evaca 5
śirīṣaḥ panasaścaiva vakulaḥ kuṭajo'rjunaḥ
yogyavṛkṣajā tayaḥ
śiṃśupa śca kadaṃba śca dirīṣa ścaṃpakastathā 6
kāśmaryo madhuka ssālaśśamī punnāga eva ca
raktacaṃdana mityete pratimārtha mudāhṛtam 7
etaiḥkāryā pratikṛti varjitairna kadā ca na
tyājyavṛkṣāṇi
varjanīyā napi tarū nabhidāsyāmi tān śṛṇa 8
svayaṃśuṣkā ścayevṛkṣāḥsvayaṃbhagnāhatatvacaḥ
vakrārkiṇakṣatāścaiva koṭaravraṇasaṃyutāḥ 9
catuṣpadasthitā yeca ye ca putrakavṛkṣakāḥ
na devāyatanasthā śca na ca valmīkasaṃbhavāḥ 10
saṃprāptabhūtāyevṛkṣā śmaśānasthāśrayādrumāḥ
pakṣiṇāṃ nilayā yeca śuṣkā grāyeca pādapāḥ 11
nistrāṇi tānalahatā kuṃjarāśanidūṣitāḥ
paśubhiḥssevitā ye ca chāyādāye mahāpathi 12
ekaśākhā dviśākhā śca triśākhā śca vivarjitāḥ
caṃḍālādyadhamai śśaśva tsevitāḥ pratilomajaiḥ 13
śīrṇavarṇā śca mārgasthā anyavṛkṣasamāvṛtāḥ
kūpasthā śca taṭākasthāḥ vikrītā ścavivarjavet 14
tvājyavṛkṣa saṃgrahepratyavāyaḥ
varjitaistarubhiḥ kuryāt pratimā śca pramādataḥ
kartā kārayitā cobhau sānvavāyau ninaśyataḥ 15
śucau same vivikte ca keśāṃgārāsthivarjite
dāru saṃgrahārtha madhivāsādīni
prāgudakpravaṇe deśe hṛdye kaṃṭakavarjite 16
samaṃtā dupali pyādha tasyādhahtaṃ vasuṃdharām
gāyatyrā caparīpūte paritaḥ prokṣyavāriṇā 17
dārusaṃgrahaṇe deśe pūrvata ścādhivāsayet
śilāsaṃgrahava ddā maṃkuryā ttaṃtravicakṣaṇaḥ 18
anokahānā maṃgāni pravālāni ca pūrvavat
jūhuyā dagni koṇetu prācīne deśikottamaḥ 19
prakṣālaye dpṛkṣamūlaṃ praṇavena ca deśikaḥ
prachcādaye tsadarbheṇa vāsasā maṃtravittamaḥ 20
vṛkṣamūle kuṭhāraṃ tu prokṣa yitvānidāpayet
arcayedgaṃthapuṣpādyairastramaṃtreṇamaṃtravit 21
kṛtvāvyāhutibhiḥ paścā dupatiṣṭheta bhūruham
prāṃjaliḥpriyato bhūtvāimaṃmaṃtramudīrayet 22
karmaṇā pūrvavṛttena sthāvarākāra māśritam
vṛṇomi viṣṇubiṃbārthaṃ sarvasaṃpatkaraṃ tava 23
sthāvaratvādito gaccha divyaṃ rūpa manuttamam
bhuṃkṣva bhogāṃ śca vipulān vāsudeva prasāduḥ 24
hinasmi nāhaṃ vṛkṣatvāṃkiṃtu muṃcāmijanmataḥ
vṛkṣaśokasya śāṃtyarthaṃprāpyadevālayaṃśubham 25
devatvaṃ yāśyate tatra dāhacchedavivarjitam
jalapuṣpapradānena sadhūpairbalibhistathā 26
lokāstvāṃ pūjayiṣyaṃti tatoyāsyatinirvṛtim
ityupasthāya maṃtreṇa rakṣāsūtreṇa veṣṭayet 27
pūrṇāhutiṃ tatohutvāvahni mudvānayedguruḥ
prabhāte deśika ssnātvā muhūrte śobhaneśuciḥ 28
pūrvadgvaṃdhapuṣpyādaiḥpūjayedvṛkṣamuttamam
tadvṛkṣaṃtu kuṭhāreṇa prājñgmukhaḥ priyataśśuciḥ 29
rathakārastato vṛkṣaṃ chedaye dgurvanujñayā
vṛkṣavaṃne phalā phala nirūpaṇam
prācyā mudīcyāṃpatitaḥkarturabhyudayāvaham 30
nairutyāgneya yāmyādi dikṣu pāto na śobhanaḥ
vāyuvyavāruṇībhyāṃ tu dikprapāto na śobhanaḥ 31
yasyabāhyasthitā śākhā dikṣvaṣṭānu samaṃtataḥ
pūrvamevatutān cchitvāpaścādvṛkṣaṃtumaṃtravit 32
garbhaparīkṣā
yadhāśilāyāṃ garbhāstu tathā vṛkṣeṣu caivahi
kuṃbhodakena saṃsiṃceddhyātvā tatrasthito harim 33
gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt pūrvava tkamalāsana
devālayaṃ drumaṃ nītyā rathakāreṇa kārayet 34
biṃba matyadhchu tākāraṃ sarvāvayava śobhitam
iti samyakcamākhyātaṃ dāru saṃgrahaṇaṃ param 35
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śri puruṣottama saṃhitāyaṃ
dārusaṃgraha vidhānaṃ nāma
ṣaṣṭhodhyāyaḥ

<7>
oṃ
śrīmatpāṃcarātre śrī puruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
saptayodhyāyaḥ
pratimāmṛtsaṃgrahaṇavidhiḥ
śrībhagavānuvāca
mṛṇmayapratimāyāṃpakvāpakvavibhedaḥ
mṛṇmayapratimāṃ vakṣye yathāva tkamalānana
pakvāpakvedvidhāproktāmṛṇmayīpratimā kramāt 1
apakvā pratimā śastāsarvakāmaphalapradā
mṛtsaṃgrahaṇa yogyapradeśāni
puṇyachakṣetre nadītīre kāṃtāre parvate pivā 2
mṛdaṃ samagrāṃ gṛhṇīyā tsarvadoṣa vivarjitām
nīcai radhyāsitāṃ caitya śmaśānādi vivarjitām 3
brāhmaṇādijātayaḥ
brāhmaṇasyasitāmṛdvaikṣatriyasyāruṇā smṛtā
vaiśyasya pīto vijñeyā kṛṣṇā śūdrasya padmaja 4
mṛtsaṃgrahaṇānaṃtaravīdhiḥ
khadirauduṃbarāśvaddhanyagrodhārjuna bhūruham
samudhṛtya tvacassarvāṃmāsaṃ vāriṣu nikṣapet 5
toyai rmāsoṣi tairmṛtsnāṃ miśrayetpīḍayettataḥ
yuvānaṃ kuśalaṃ śāṃtaṃ kulālaṃ vyādhivarjitam 6
rathakāraṃ niyuṃjīta śāstrokte sarvakarmaṇi
rathakārokta vidhinā kulālaṃ sarva mācaret 7
tāṃ nūtaneṣu bhāṃḍeṣu nikṣi pyācchādyayatnataḥ
cchāyāyāṃnikṣipedbhāṃḍaṃyathāvātādinaspṛnet 8
dhātrīphalaṃ tribhāgaṃ ca ṣadguṇā ca harītakī
dhātarīya strayobhāgāḥcūrṇametatprakalsayet 9
puṇyakṣetre nadītīre parvate puline hrade
nirghare saṃgame soṣye vedikorvarayosthathā 10
śālikṣe tredevakhāte śṛṃtai ca vṛṣahastinoḥ
varāhakṛṣṇavalmīka kulīravasatau tathā 11
nalinyāṃ dīrghikāyoṃ ca mṛda ścko naviṃśatiḥ
mṛdāmamīṣāṃmātrābhiḥmiśrayetpīḍayettaraḥ 12
pūrvoktamānai striphalā cūrnaistāṃmiśrayetbunaḥ
nādeyai ślaṣṇasāṣāṇaiḥ mṛtturyāṃśesacūrṇitaiḥ 13
caṃdanaṃ kuṃkumaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ śrīveṣṭaṃ haricaṃdanam
guggulaṃ ca niśāṃ caiva haritālaṃ manaśśilā 14
tamālapatrauṇyetāni caṃdanādīni cūrṇayet
mṛdviṃśatyekabhāgaistu cūrṇaistatulyasaikataiḥ 15
cūrṇaistairloharatnai śca mīśrayitvā vimardayet
kapiddhasya rasaṃ tatra bhāga mekaṃ vimiśrayet 16
madhutailaghṛtakṣīra dadhibhi ssuktisammitaiḥ
adhivāsaḥ
imāṃ mṛdaṃ samāloḍhya navabhāṃḍeṣu nikṣipet 17
ācchādya navavastreṇa dhānyamadhye vinikṣipet
arcaye nmūlamaṃtreṇa mṛdbhāṃḍā ndeśikottamaḥ 18
homavidhiḥ
kuṃḍe vāstaṃḍile vāpi homaṃkuryāttato guruḥ
sarpiṣāṃ juhuyā dagnau mūlenāṣṭottaraṃśataṃ 19
caruṃ puruṣasūktena pratyekaṃ juhuyāttataḥ
saṃpātākhyena tāṃ siṃcenmṛdaṃ bhāṃḍeṣu saṃsthitāṃ 20
pūrṇāhutyaṃta makhilaṃ kuryāttaṃtravicaṇaḥ
māsaṃparyuṣitāṃkṛtvā pratimāṃkārayettataḥ 21
mṛtsaṃgrahoyaṃ saṃproktaḥpratimālakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu
ratnalohaśilādārumṛṇmayādivibhedataḥ 22
iti śrīpāṃcarātremahopaniṣadi puruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
pratimāmṛtsaṃ grahaṇaṃ nāmasaptamodhyāyaḥ

ṛṣayaḥ
[adhikapāṭhāni
bhagavan muniśārdūla āśritārthaphalapradā
kadhyatāṃ biṃba nirmāṇa vidhiṃ śāstra pramāṇataḥ
katibhedāni berāṇi arcanārthaṃ madhudviṣaḥ
teṣāṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃbruhi vistareṇa yathāvidhi]
śrīvasiṣṭhaḥ
dhvabera lakṣaṇaṃ
ata saraṃ pravakṣyāmi vigrahāṇāṃ ca lakṣaṇam
ālayasya pramāṇena kūryāddvārapramāṇataḥ 1
yajamānasya mānena kārayettrividhaṃ bhuvi
garbhālayamānena vigrahamānaḥ
garbhālayasya cāyāmaṃ navabhāgaṃ ca gaṇyate 2
yathā dravyānusāreṇa kuryāddvārānusārataḥ
ekādinavamāṃtaṃ ca kāraye nmūlavigraham 3
garbhālayadvāramānaṃ pādaṃ nyūnaṃ ca vaidvija
dvāramānena
dvīrārdhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā dvāramānaṃ caturvidham 4
yajamānamānena
yajamānasamaṃ kuryāt (netrāṃtaṃ vāyadhāruci)netrāṃtaṃ vā yathāvidhi
cubukaṃ cāsyamānaṃ vā vakṣojāṃtaṃ hṛdaṃtakam 5
saptamāna midaṃ jñeyaṃ yaja mānavaśaṃ viduḥ
śilpi nāgamaśāstreṇa kāraye dvigrahaṃ śubham 6
aṃgulādimānāni mānāṃgulaṃ
aṃtaḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi aṃgulasya pramāṇakam
aṇureṇubhi raṣṭaiśca sarṣapaṃ (tatpracakṣyate)savidhīyate 7
sarṣapai raṣṭabhi ścaiva tila mekaṃ vidhīyate
tilāṣṭabhi ryavaṃ proktaṃ yavāṣṭaisti ryagaṃgulam 8
(mānāṃgulami daṃproktaṃ)itimānā gulaṃ proktaṃ mātrāṃgula midaṃ śśruṇu
mātrāṃgulaṃ
madhyamāṃguli madhyasya ūrthvaṃ parvasya tiryagam 9
mātrāṃgulaṃ ca dvividhaṃ dehalabdhāṃgulaṃ tataḥ
dehalabdhāṃgulaṃ
aṃguṣṭhavalayārthaṃ ca aṣṭhārthaṃ ca tadhonnatam 10
idaṃ ca dvividhaṃ proktaṃ sarvaśāstreṣu niścitam
mānāṃgulādyupayogāni
mānāṃgulapramāṇena ālayādividhīyate 11
mātrāṃgula pramāṇena kuṃḍaṃkūrcaṃ sṛcaṃ sruvam
idhmādyanalapātrāṇi kuryātsarvāṇi deśikaḥ 12
dehalabdhāṃgulenaiva prakuryā tsarvadevatāḥ
(ālayasya pramāṇena tadhādvārapramāṇataḥ) ālayasya vase naiva tathādvāravaśena ca 13
biṃba nirmāṇa vidhiḥ
yajamānavaśe naiva mūrti kuryā ttridhā tataḥ
vigrahapramāṇaḥ
tadbiṃbasavyahastasya dehalabdhāṃgulena ca 14
pādādikeśaparyaṃtaṃ tathācāṣṭhottaraṃ śatam
dvādaśāṃgulamānena navatālāni gaṇyate 15
sarveṣāṃ vigrahāṇāṃ ca pramāṇa mili niścayam
vigraha vibhāgāni
liṃgasthānaṃ śarīrārtha adhaścordhvaṃca tatsamam 16
ūrऴvakāyaṃ ca turbhāga nābherliṃgasya māṃtaram
ekabhāga madha ścorthvhṛdaṃtaṃ caikabhāgataḥ 17
hṛdādicibukāṃtaṃ ca ekabhāgaṃ vidhīyate
tasyorthveca śirojāṃta mekabhāgaṃ prakarpayet 18
avayava pramāṇa vidhiḥ
karṇau caturbhi raṃgulyairnāśikaṃ caturaṃgulam
nāśikasya samaṃ netraṃ vaktraṃ netrasya tatsamam 19
caturaṃgulakaṃ kaṃṭhaṃ mehanaṃ caturaṃgulam
mehanārthaṃ ca vṛṣaṇaṃ stanamadhyaṃ daśāṃgulam 20
adhaḥkāyāṃtare jānuhastā jānvaṃta mucyate
nābhessamaṃ bāhumadhyaṃ tatsamaṃ maṇibaṃdhakam 21
hastalakṣaṇaṃ
tadardhaṃ syā tpāṇitalaṃ nakhāṃtaṃ kāraye dbudhaḥ
aṃgulatrayamānena madhyamāṃguli munnatam 22
parvatrayaṃ samaṃ kuryāt sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutam
tarjanyanāmikaṃ caiva māṣaṃ nyūnaṃ vidhīyate 23
kaniṣṭhikaṃ dvimāṣaṃ syāt trimāṣāṃguṣṭha mucyate
yathābiṃbapramāṇena tathā kuryā dvicakṣaṇaḥ 24
pādalakṣaṇaṃ
pādaṃ tālapramāṇena aṃgulānāṃ vidhiṃśruṇu
aṃgulatrayamānena pādamadhyāṃgulaṃ smṛtam 25
tataḥ pāṇyaṃgulā neva kārayecchilbisattamaḥ
avayava lakṣaṇaṃ
mastakasya ca madhyetu karṇasthānaṃ vidhīyate 26
śroṇīlalāṭaṃ kurvīta kūrmapṛṣṇava dunnam
karṇau netre nāsike ca jānūgulbhau kaṭībhujau 27
vṛṣaṇaṃ cibukaṃ coṣṭhau maṇibaṃdhaṃ ca bhrūyuge
bāhumūlau kucau yugmausamaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi 28
sthānake
(sthānake vigrahe kuryāt)tiṣṭhataḥ pratimāyāṃtugarbhāvartaṃ samunnataṃ
ūrthvahastau bhujasamau netrāṃtaṃ cāludhāgrakam 29
alaṃkārāsanaṃ kuryāt pramāṇaṃ na vicārayet
yathābeṃborthvamānena asanaṃ ca tadhonnatam 30
nāsādicubhukāṃtasya dviguṇaṃ makuṭaṃ tathā
yathā lābhaṃ tathākuryādgartastūpīṃ prakalpayet 31
depyādimānavidhiḥ
devīmānaṃ tato vakṣye nāsāṃtaṃ vigrahasya ca
oṣṭhāṃtaṃ madhyamaṃ proktaṃ cibukāṃta mathādhamam 32
āsīnā sā caturhastā tiṣṭhati dvibhujā tathā
devasya pārśvayoḥkkuryāttiṣṭhutyau pratimedvija 33
vāme śriyo vāmahastaṃ laṃbaṃ dakṣiṇapaṃgajam
tathā dakṣiṇadeśe ca śriyo (vāmena paṃkajam)vāme ca paṃkajam 34
dakṣiṇe laṃbahastaṃ ca śrībhūdevyośca lakṣaṇam
sarveṣāṃ viṣṇubiṃbānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ pārśvayośriyoḥ 35
ālayenye sthāpitavyā (vīralakṣmī ścaturbhujā) rājyalakṣmī ścaturbhujā
[adhikapāṭhāni
varadābhayahastau ca ūrdhvepaṃkajadhāriṇī
kamalāsanayuktā ca vīralakṣmīrvidhīyate
pūrṇacaṃdranibhaṃ cāsyaṃ sarvāgaṃ suṃdaraṃ śubhaṃ
sarvābharaṇa saṃyuktaṃ śrībiṃbaṃ sarvata smṛtaṃ]
varadābhayahastaṃ ca padmaṃ savyāpasavyayoḥ 36
padmapīṭhe samāsīnāṃ rājyalakṣmīṃ tu kārayet
sarveṣā mavatārāṇāṃ (vīralakṣmīṃ prakalpayet)rājyalakṣmīṃ prakalpayet 37
(ityekaṃ dhruvabiṃbasya lakṣaṇaṃ kadhitaṃ dvijāḥ) ityevaṃ dhruvaberasya lakṣaṇaṃ sarvasammatam
kautukaberādimānāni sadberāṇi
kautukaṃ dhruvaberārthaṃ kautukārthaṃ tathotsavam 38
snapanaṃ cotsavārthaṃ syāt snāpanārthaṃ bali stathā
sālagrāmaṃ ca dīrthārthaṃ iti ṣa ḍberalakṣaṇam 39
dhruvādisnapanāṃtastu dhṛvava tkārayetkramāt
[adhikapāṭhāni
avatāreṣu sarveṣu karmārcāsthānakaṃ smṛtaṃ]
sudarśanamūrtiḥ
ṣaṭkoṇacakramadhye ca mūrtiṃ ṣaḍbhuja mucyate 40
paṃkajaṃ hetirājaṃ ca gadāṃ dakṣiṇabāhubhiḥ
śaṃkhaṃ ca musalaṃ śārgñaṃdhāraye dvāmabāhubhiḥ 41
bhrāṃtaṃ ṣaṭkoṇa cakrasya ṣaḍaraissahitaṃ matam
dvārapālaka vigraha lakṣaṇāni
(dvāredvāre ca kurvīta dvārapālaka vīgrahau)sarveṣu dpāradeśeṣu dvaubiṃbau dvārapālakau 42
vāme cakraṃ tathā śaṃkhaṃ gadāṃ dakṣiṇahastake
vyastapādaṃ sapādaṃ vā dvāradakṣiṇavigrahaḥ 43
vāme śaṃkhaṃ tathā cakraṃ vāmahaste gadādharam
anyaṃ varadahastaṃ vāmavigrahalakṣaṇam 44
[adhikapāṭhāni
śaṃkhacakragadāpadmā ssarvasāmānyalakṣaṇaṃ]
dvāravigrahamānāni
dvāramānaṃ ca tasyārthaṃ tadarthaṃ ca tadarthakam
yathā vittānusāreṇa kāraye ddvārapālakān 45
(garbhālaya pramāṇena dhruvabiṃbānusārataḥ)garbhālayasya mānena dhruvamānena vā bhavet
pramāṇai ca śca turbhiścha kuryā dvittānusārataḥ
iti śrī pāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
dhruvaberādi vigraha lakṣaṇaṃ nāmāṣṭa
modhyāyaḥ
śrī śrī śrī
<9>
oṃ
śrīmatpāṃcarātre śrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
navamodhyāyaḥ
rathādiyānalakṣaṇavidhiḥ
rathalakṣaṇaṃ
ślo rathasya lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye devadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ
vimānocchrāyamānaṃvāgopurocchrāyamevanā 1
dhvajastaṃbhasamucchrāyaṃmaṃṭapocchrāya mevanā
lohena dāruṇā vāpi kāraye dratha muttamam 2
aṃtassāraṃ dārujaṃ celloha śctsvarṇarājitam
[adhikapāṭhāni
kāṃsyena vā dhanākuryāt yathāvittānusārataḥ]
navaratnaiścakhacitaṃ dvārastaṃbhaśatāvṛtam 3
ṣoḍaśadvādaśāṣṭau vā yathā vibhavavistaram
bhūrādinapta vā kuryā datalādi caturdaśa 4
bhūrbhuvassuva ri tyādilokatrayatalaṃ yathā
ucchrāya syānuguṇyena (kathāyāmaṃ prakalpayet) āyāmaṃ parikalpayet 5
bhūmikāyā śca prathame bhāge sakrāṇi kalpayet
pārśvayo rubhayoḥ kuryādrathasyānuguṇaṃyathā 6
tadagre ca śikhākuṃbhaṃ cakreṇojvalitaṃ mahat
ghaṃṭāśatasamāyuktaṃ patāka dhvajaśobhitam 7
hayā nvā dviradā nvāpi prāgbhāge parikalpayet
tasmadhye sārathiṃ sthāpyatotraṃ vāṃkuśadhāriṇam 8
citraracanā
atalādipade tattallokasthān parikalpayet
nāgā nyakṣānkinnarāṃśca gaṃdharvān garuḍastathā 9
siddhā nvidyādharāṃ caiva devadānavarākṣasān
piśācā nguhyagā ssādhyānmunī nparamavaiṣṇavān 10
rāmakṛṣṇādi vibhaguvanmūrtilīlāḥprakalpayet
rathamadhyapīṭha lakṣaṇaṃ
tanmadhye vedikāyāṃ tu kūrmapīṭhaṃ prakalpayet 11
tasyopari hyanaṃtaṃ ca sahasraphaṇimaṃḍitam
kalpayitmādha tanmadhye ḍūlikāṃ svarṇanirmitām 12
tanmadhyeṣṭadalaṃ padmaṃ karṇikā kesarānvitam
āsīnārthaṃ tu devasya svarṇapīṭhaṃ prakalpayet 13
rathanirmāṇaphala viśeṣaḥ
evaṃ kṛtvā rathaṃ divyaṃ viṣṇo ratulatejasaḥ
yerpayaṃ tyatibhaktyātu teyāṃti paramaṃpadam 14
taditaravāhanāni tallakṣaṇānica
ataḥparaṃ prapakṣyāmi vāhanānāṃ ca lakṣaṇam
pīṭha māditya somaṃ ca siṃhavyānikhagādhipāḥ 15
hanūmānhaṃsa mattebhā hayasyaṃdana eva ca
śibikāṃ sarvabhūpālavāhanāni yathākramam
garbhālayasamaṃ vā vāhanāni ca kārayet 16
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi puruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
rathādilakṣaṇaṃnāma navamodhyāyaḥ
śrī śrī śrī

daśamodhyāyaḥ
pratiṣṭhāmuhūrtanirṇayavidhiḥ
pratiṣṭhāvidhiśravaṇā
brahmauvācā
gthaṃbrahma praśnaḥ
pratiṣṭhālakṣaṇaṃ brūhi devadevajagatpate
tatsarvaṃ vistareṇaiva yadi tuṣṭo si me prabho 1
bhagavatprativacanaṃ
śrībhagavānuvāca
pratiṣṭhāvidhikārikā (kriyāsūnacam)
athavakṣye viśeṣaṇa pratiṣṭhāvidhi muttamam
maṃṭapaṃ prathamaṃ kuryāddvitīyaṃ jalavāsakam 2
tṛtīyaṃ snapanaṃ kuryā ccaturthaṃ śayanaṃ bhavet
paṃcamaṃ cāgnivinyāsaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanam 3
saptamaṃ śāṃtihomaṃ tu aṣṭamaṃsparśanaṃ bhavet
navamaṃ gehaśuddhistu daśamaṃratna mucyate 4
ekādaśaṃ karmapūrteḥ pūrṇāhuti mathā caret
dvādaśaṃ sthāpanaṃ kuryā tprokṣaṇaṃ catrayodaśam 5
nyāsaṃ caturdaśaṃ kuryā tsarivāra mataḥparam
ṣoḍaśī dakṣiṇā proktā kriyāsūcana muttamam 6
pratiṣṭhāmūhūrtanirṇayaḥ ayanaṃ pakṣaṃ
pratiṣṭhāsu muhūrtasya lakṣaṇaṃ śruṇu sāṃpratam
(ayanaṃ cottaraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ tyājyaṃ dakṣiṇamucyate) dakṣiṇāyana mutsṛjya uttarāyaṇa muttamam 7
uttamaṃ śuklapakṣaṃ tu kṛṣṇapakṣepi vā bhavet
tyājyadivasāni
gurāvastaṃgate śukre vyatīpāte tu varjayet 8
saṃkrāṃtau durdine caiva grahaṇe somasūryayoḥ
palastāśca purastāśca ṣoḍaśāhani varjayet 9
āticāre tathā māse parvayugmayute tathā
eva mādiṣu cānyeṣu garhiteṣu na kalpayet 10
yogyadivasāni
rājño rāṣṭrasya cānyasya grāmasya cagurostathā
yajamānasya dhiṣṇyasya śubheṣvanuguṇeṣu ca 11
tithinakṣatravāreṣu muhurteṣu śubheṣu ca
tithayaḥ
prathamā ca dvitīyā ca paṃcamī ca trayodaśi 12
daśamī paurṇamāsī ca śuklapakṣe śubhetithau
vārāṇi
gurubhārgavasaumyānāṃ vārā śreṣṭha tamā matāḥ 13
nakṣatrāṇi
uttarāsu ca revatyāṃ aśvinyāṃ dhātṛje tathā
puṣye punarvasau cāpi haste ca śravaṇe tathā 14
devasya sthāpanaṃ kuryādviṣṇo rabhyudayāvaham
saumyagrahāḥ
somo bṛhaspati ścaivabhārgavodhabudha stathā 15
śubhalagna nirṇayaḥ
ete saumyagrahāḥ proktāḥ praśastā sthāpanaṃ prati
saiṃhikeyaśca sauraśca arkaścaiva tu niṃditāḥ 16
rāsesttīy ṣaṣṭhe vāsthitā ścecchubhaśaṃsinaḥ
dvitīye ca tṛtīye ca tathāpaṃcamaṣaṣṭhayauḥ 17
saptame daśame caiva rāśau caikādaśe punaḥ
sthitaḥśśubhakaraścaṃdraḥ ṣaṭsaptadaśamasthitaḥ 18
bhayakṛ dbhārgavojñeya ścaṃdraḥ krūrai rnirīkṣitaḥ
sūryeṃdukujarāhvarke ketavo lagnagā yadi 19
kartu mṛtyupradā ssaumyaścāyuśrīputradā smṛtāḥ
dvitīye vittadā ssaumya nacaṃdrā neṣṭhadāḥpare 20
tṛtīya sthānagāḥ krūrāḥ gṛhaśrīputradā smṛtāḥ
caturtheśubhadā ssaumyaścaṃdraḥkrūrāśca duḥkhadāḥ 21
paṃcame dyādhidā krūrāḥ śubhāḥ putradhanapradāḥ
putradastatrapūrṇtaduḥ kṣīṇeṃdu rdoṣakṛ dbhavet 22
ṣaṣṭhiścaduḥkhadā proktā daśamī ca dhanapradā
pudrasaṃpatpradā ssaumyāḥ dharmasthānasthitā yadi 23
lābha sthānagatā ssarvegrahā śrīputrapautradāḥ
vyayasthānagatā ssarvebahuvyayakarā grahāḥ 24
sūryavāraśśubhaproktaḥ karapuṣṭayuto yadi
maṃdavāro hitaḥ kartuḥ svātī rohiṇiyogataḥ 25
tṛtīyā budhasaṃyuktā ṣaṣṭhī jīvasamāhitā
ekādaśī somayutā karoti prāṇasaṃśayam 26
pauṣṇa stu saptamī yuktādaha tyagni riva prajāḥ
uttarāṣāḍha saṃyukto somavārastu śobhanaḥ 27
vṛṣamatsyamṛgeṃdrākhyamuhūrtācottamāmatāḥ
kuṃbhaka śca dhanurmeṣa mīdhunā madhyamā smṛtāḥ 28
kartu ssūrya balopete caṃdratārābalānvite
paṃceṣṭakayu telagne pyaṣṭame śuddhisaṃyute 29
śubhagrahe kṣite yukte sumuhūrte su yogake
bahunātraki muktena sahamauhūrtikairguruḥ 30
sthāpanaṃ devadevasya kuryā tkāle yathāvidhi
tadardhaṃ maṃṭapaṃ kuryāt yathābhimatadiṅmukham 31
iti śrīpāṃca rātre mahopaniṣadhi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
pratiṣṭhāmuhūrta nirṇayonāma daśamodhyāyaḥ

bhagavatprati vacanaṃ
śrībhagavān
yāgamaṃṭapa lakṣaṇaṃ
devālayasya pūrvasyāṃ caiśānye cottarepi vā
dvitīyā varaṇe yadvā tṛtīyā varaṇepi vā 2

hastaṣoḍaśavistīrṇaṃ tadvadāyāmasaṃ mitam 3

pātvatī rājasīcaiva tāmasīti kramādbhavet

caturaṃgula mutsedhā dvādaśāṃgula vistṛtā 7

taddvadaunnatyasahitā tadūrthveyoni riṣyate 9

udaṃṅmukho yadi bhaveddhotā yoniṃ vinā pivā

ātirikta tṛtīyena bhrāmaye tpūrṇacaṃdravat
cāpakuṃḍaṃ
madhye sūtraṃ samāsphālyā vinṛjedutta rāthankam 13
svīkṛtya dakṣiṇārthaṃ tu pūrvana nmekhalātrayam
etaccāpākhyakuṃḍaṃ syāt pūrvanaccaturaśrake 14
vṛttakuṃḍaṃ
kṣetramādyātkoṇasūtraṃpūrvavadbhrāmayettataḥ

padmakuṃḍaṃ

trikoṇakuṃḍaṃ
aśravistṛtaṣaḍbhāgaṃ dakṣiṇottarayordhvayoḥ

mekhalā
tatsūtrasbhālane naiva trikoṇaṃ sādhayedguruḥ
yādṛgvidhaṃ bhavetkuṃḍaṃ tādṛgevatu mekhalā 18
paṃcakuṃḍa vidhānaṃ

(vahni bhāge trikoṇaṃ syāt)padmakuṃḍu śive kāryaṃ paṃcavahni prakalpane
catustoraṇāni teṣāṃ pramāṇāni

plakṣa ścaivatu catvāro toraṇārthaṃ prakalpayet
tadutsedhamadho vakṣye toraṇānāṃca sarvaśaḥ 21
maṃṭapadvāratulyaṃ vā pādāyata madhā pi vā
taddaṃḍaṃ dviguṇaṃ vāpi sthaṃbhotsedha madhā pivā 22
paṃcahastanayā vāpi caturhastaṃtu vāpunaḥ
tadarthaṃ paṭṭikākāraṃ toraṇānica kārayet 23
maṃṭapa nirmāṇe staṃbhādināvāni
daśatālaṃ tadhā yāmaṃ paṭṭikātu tadarthakam
staṃbhayormūlanāhaṃ syāddvātriśāṃgulamīritam 24
tamo rmadhyetu nāhaṃtu āṣṭāviṃśati raṃgulam
tadagrayo rnāhaṃ syāccaturviṃśāṃgulonnatam 25
staṃbhāyāmaṃ tridhākṛtvāmūlabhāgeyugāśrakam
mahābhāge tukāstvaśraṃ ūrdhvabhāge tu vartulam 26
adhavā staṃbhanāhaṃ syā nmūlamadhyāgrakeṣu ca
caturviṃśa dviṃśati śca ṣoḍaśāṃgula miṣyate 27
ṣauḍaśāṃgulanāhaṃ vā sādā dagrasamaṃ tataḥ
staṃbhayo rnāha muktaṃcayathāśobhaṃbhavettataḥ 28
staṃbhayo ragrataḥ kuryāt padmakuṭmala vadbhavet
adha vā gniśikhā vāpi yathā śobhaṃtu kārayet 29
staṃbhā dutsedhamānena vistāraṃ paṭṭikā ubhe
paṭṭikādvayavistāraṃ dvādaśāṃgula meva vā 30
aṣṭāṃgulaṃ vā kurvīta ṣoḍaśāṃgula māyatam
caturaṃgulavistāraṃ paṭṭikārthaṃ tu paṃkajam 31
staṃbhaṃ caturtha vibhaje dekabhāge kṣitau kṣipet
yajñavṛkṣāṃśca saṃgṛhya yadhākāma madhāpivā 32
dvārapramāṇaṃ vākuryā tpramāṇaṃ puruṣāyatam
evaṃ lakṣaṇayuktāni kārayettoraṇāni ca 33
aṣṭamaṃgala lakṣaṇe
vakṣyāmilakṣaṇaṃ cāṣṭa maṃgalāni yathā tadham
yajñavṛkṣān samādhāya kṣīravṛkṣaṃ viśeṣataḥ 34
āyāmamekahastaṃ syā dvistāraṃ ca ṣaḍaṃgulam
ghanaṃ trayāṃgulaṃ proktaṃraktāṃgaṃ śvetavarṇakam 35
pītābhaṃ paṃkajābhaṃ ca rājā rāṣṭraśubhāvaham
śrīvatsaṃ pūrṇakuṃbhaṃ ca bherīṃ darpaṇamaṃḍalam 36
matsyalugmaṃ ca śaṃkhaṃ ca cakraṃ kāśyapanaṃdanam
phalakeṣu likhedevaṃ maṃgalāni yathākramam 37
samidhaḥ
pālāśau duṃparāśvaddha plakṣāssa midhayastathā
idhmaṃ
idhmamekaviṃśatya kuśābhyāṃ baṃdhaye ttataḥ 38
samitprādeśayugalaṃ ca tu rviṃśāṃgulaḥ kuśaiḥ
brahmakūrcādayaḥ
caturviṃśati darbhaiśca brahmakūrcādayasmṛtā 39
mūlamaṃgulamu tsṛjya tadhāgraṃ vinṛjedbudhāḥ
pavitragraṃdhi vadgraṃdhi rveṇi rūpaṃ tadhā kṛtiḥ 40
triḥprādeśaṃtu paridhī nprādeśaṃ syātpavitrakam
sṛskṛvādīni
yajñāṃgaṃ khādirovāpi srukruvaukārayetsudhīḥ 41
caturviṃśāṃgulāyāmaṃ tadagre kamalākṛtiḥ
bilamaṃgulamātraṃ syā tsruvamevaṃ prakīrtitam 42
dvādaśāṃgula daṃḍaṃ syā ccaturaṃgulakaṃ bilam
juhuraṣṭāṃgulaṃ caiva sṛcamityabhidhīyate 43
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
yāgaśālākuṃḍa toraṇādi lakṣaṇaṃnāma ekādaśodhyāyaḥ
śrī śrī śrī
<12>
oṃ
śrīpāṃcarātrepuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
dvādaśodhyāyaḥ
ācāryaniveśanamṛtsaṃgrahaṇavidhiḥ
ācāryāvaraṇaṃ
ślo pratiṣṭhādivase prāpte yajamāno hariṃsmaran
brāhme muhurte coddhāya (kṛtanityāhni kaśśuciḥ)kṛtanityakriyastadhā 1
patnīputrādinaṃyukto brāhmaṇai rbāṃdhavai ssaha
nṛttagītādhibhi śaiva chatradhvajavitānakaiḥ 2
devasya sthāpanā yādau ācāryasya niveśanam
praviśya phalapuṣpādī ndatvā bhaktyāca bhūtale 3
sāṣṭhāṃgaṃ praṇivatyedaṃ vijñāpana madhā care
mayā nirmitabiṃbeṣu bhaktānāṃ mokṣasiddhay 4
bhavanmaṃtra prabhāvena bhagavaṃ taṃ praveśaya
iti saṃprārthya saṃpūjya puṣpasrakcaṃdanādibhiḥ 5
ārcakassa hari ssākṣāccararūpī na saṃśayaḥ
iti matvā mahāprājñaṃ (yānamāropayettutaḥ)namāropyatu yānake 6
vedavidbhi dbrāhmaṇai śca tūryaghoṣapurassaraṃ
grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ nītvā yāgaśālāṃ praveśayet 7
viṣvaksena pūja puṇyāha vācanaṃ saṃkalpaṃ
sumuhurtetu saṃprāpte viṣvaksenaṃ pravūjayet
saṃprokṣya sarvasaṃbhārān puṇyāhasya jalena ca 8
deśakālavibhāgena kartu ssaṃkalpa mācaret
dhṛtvā kaṃkaṇavastrādī nyajamānānvito guruḥ 9
ṛtvikpūjā pariṣaddakṣiṇā devatāvāhanaṃ
pūjaye nmadhuparkeṇa narvāna pyartvijastataḥ
dhārayitvā kautukāni kuryā tpariṣadarcanam 10
tadanujñā mavāpyaiva devā nāvāhya śāstrataḥ
vaiṣṇavā nbhojayetpaścāt madhyāhn baṃdhubhissaha 11
sāyaṃkālemṛtsaṃgrahaṇaṃ tadvidhiḥ
sāyaṃkāletu saṃprāpte mṛtsaṃgrahaṇamārabhet
ācāryo yajamānaśca gītavāditranisvanaiḥ 12
mahatā janasaṃghena (vedaghoṣa purassaraṃ)vedaghoṣa samanvitam
diśaṃ prācī mudīcīṃvā gatvātatra śucisthale 13
maṃtre śāstreṇa saṃprokṣya gomayena (vilipyada)vilepayet
bhūmirbhūmne ti maṃtreṇavṛtta maṃḍalamālikhet 14
tanmadhye medinīṃ devīṃ navatāhasamāyutām
īśānanairutinyasta śiraḥpādāṃbujadvayīm 15
ūrthvavaktrāṃ vilikhyevaṃ arcaye nnyāsapūrvakam
kūrcena paścime cakraṃ viṣvaksena madhottare 16
prāgbhāge garuḍaṃ yāmyekhanitre śeṣa marcayet
[adhikapāṭhāni
bhūvarāhaṃ samālikhya pūjaye dgaṃdhapuṣpakaiḥ]
nyāsaṃ kṛtvāvarāhasya vārāhāruṣṭubhaṃjapet 17
bhūvarāhaṃ tato dhyātvā khanitraṃdhārayanguruḥ
tvāṃ khanāmīti tatkukṣau nikhane (tprājmukhasthita)tprāṃjñmukhastataḥ 18
mṛdaṃ saṃgṛhya lohādi bhājane vetraje pivā
navena vāsasā cchādya bhūgarbhe bali mācaret 19
mṛdbhājanaṃ samāropya gajeyānādikepivā
[adhikapāṭhāni
nadītare vālukāṃca karīṣasyaca cūrṇakaṃ
bhūnūkta medinīnūkta śākunānūktapāṭhakai]
śrīsūkta pāvamānādi pāṭhakai rbrāhaṇai nsahā 20
grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ nītvāmaṃṭapaṃ saṃpraveśayet
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
mṛtsaṃgrahaṇavidhi rnāma dvādaśodhyāyaḥ
śrī śrī śrī
<13>
oṃ
śrīpāṃcarātre śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
trayodaśodhyāyaḥ
aṃkurāropaṇavidhi
aṃkurārpaṇaṃ
ślo grāmapadrakṣiṇenaiva aṃkurārpaṇa maṃṭapam
praviśya caṃdanārdrāṇi (sūtrāṇyāsphālayedguruḥ) sūtrāṇi sphālaye dguruḥ 1
maṃḍala racanā
prāgāyatāni prathamaṃ ṛjūnidvādaśasphuṭam
udagāyatasūtrāṇi daśapaṃcaca kalpayet 2
vīdhyarthaṃ mārjaye ttatra ṣaṭtriṃśatkoṣṭakāni ca
madhyadvādaśake tatra ghaṭikāsthāvaye tkramāt 3
pūrvavadghaṭikā ścaiṃdre vāruṇyāṃtu ghaṭānyaset
āgneye nairute yāmye pālikāḥ pūrvavanyaset 4
vāyuvyaiśānyasomeṣu śarāvā npūrvava nyaśet
dhānyapīṭha prakalpanaṃ
pātrāṇā madhivāsārdhaṃ vrīhibhiḥ staṃḍulaistilaiḥ 5
pīṭhīni kalpanīyāni pratyekaṃ mūlavidyayā
pānikāghaṭikā caivā śarāvaṃ ca tridhā matam 6
pālika ghaṭika śarāva lakṣaṇāni
sauvarṇaṃ rājitaṃ caiva tāmrajaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ tuvā
pālikāmukhavistāraṃ ṣoḍaśāṃgula mucyate 7
śarāvāṇāṃ mukhaṃ kuryāddvādaśāṃgulavistṛtam
caturdikthsitavaktrāṇāṃ vistṛtaṃ caturaṃgulam 8
ghaṭānāṃ mathyamaṃ vaktraṃ vistāreṇa ṣaḍaṃgulam
sarveṣāṃ pādapīṭhasya vistṛtistu daśāṃgulam 9
caturaṃgula monnatyaṃ aṃgulaṃ kaṃṭha miṣyate
duttūrapuṣpasadṛśaṃ vaktra maṃbhojasannibham 10
utcedhaḥ pālikānāṃ tu paṃcaviṃśāgulaṃ smṛtam
dvāviṃśatirghaṭānāṃ tu śarāvāṇāṃ tu viṃśatiḥ 11
etallakṣaṇayuktāni pātrāṇyānīya deśikaḥ
pālikādisthāpanavidhiḥ
mūlamaṃtreṇa saṃprokṣya dūrvā maśvaddhapatrakam 12
śirīṣaṃ sahadevīṃ ca tatkaṃṭheṣu ca baṃdhayet
taṃtuṃ ta nveti maṃtreṇa sūtreṇa pariveṣṭitam 13
bhūmirbhūmneti maṃtreṇa tribhāsaṃ nikṣipenmṛdam
yadhoktatattasthāneṣu ghaṭikāḥ parameṣṭhinā 14
brahmajijñānamaṃtreṇa ghaṭī nmadhye vinikṣipet
yata iṃdreti maṃtreṇa ghaṭadvādaśakaṃ nyaset 15
aiṃdre pade tathāgneye tvamagneriti maṃtrataḥ
pālikā sthāsyayāmyetu yamāyasomamaṃtrataḥ 16
mūṣaṇuḥparamaṃtreṇa nairutyāṃ pālikā nyaset
tatvāyaumīti vāruṇyāṃ pūrva vadghaṭikā nyaset 17
śarāvānnikṣipe dvāyā vāyumaṃtreṇa deśikaḥ
uttare somamaṃtreṇa śarāvānpūrvaṃ nyaset 18
(īśānyāṃśaiva maṃtreṇa śarāvānvinyasetkramāt) īśāna manuvāraudreśarāvānnikṣipe tkramāt
pālikāsu yaje dviṣṇuṃ ghaṭikāsu caturmukham 19
śarāveṣu śivaṃ dhyātvā samāvāhya prapūjayet
pālikānā maṣṭadikṣu kalaśā nvinyasetkramāt 20
thānyarāśiṣu saṃsthāpyaṃ (lokapālānprapūjayet)dikpālānprūjayettataḥ
toraṇadhvajadevādīn samāvāhya tato guruḥ 21
somakuṃbhasthāpanaṃ
pātrāṇāṃ paścimasthāne daśadroṇapramāṇataḥ
thānyarāśau vinikṣipya dhṛvaṃta itimaṃtrataḥ 22
somakuṃbhaṃ nyasettasmīnvāsasā śeṣṭyapūrayet
gaṃdhodakena paṃcatva kpaṃcaratnāni vinyanet 23
nuvarṇanirmitaṃ somaṃ tasmi nnāvāhya pūjayet
nivedya pāyāsānnaṃca somakuṃbhasya cottare 24
karakaṃ vinyase ttasmin hetirājaṃ samarcayet
karakakuṃbhasthāpanaṃ
mudgādiveṇubījāṃtaṃ trayodaśa manuttamam 25
dhānyaprakṣālanaṃ
mudganarṣapanīvāra śiṃbamāṣayava stadhā
priyaṃgu ścaṇakasyāma guluddhāṃśca taddhā tilān 26
vrīhiveṇuśca thānyāni payobhiḥ kṣālayetsudhīḥ
navadhānyāni
tilamudgayavavrīhi māṣagodhūmanarṣapāḥ 27
caṇako rājamāṣaśca navadhānyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ
bījapātrāsthānaṃ
navadhānyāni vā yadvā trayodaśa madhāpivā 28
aṃkurāropaṇārdhaṃ tu saṃgrahllohabhājane
tatpātraṃ taṃḍulepīṭhe navadroṇa mite nyaset 29
bīyādhidevātāvāhanaṃ
ācāryo viṣṇugāyatyrā bījādhīśā nadhārcayet
mudgāṇā madhipaṃ viṣṇuṃ sarṣapāṇāṇāmūmāpatim 30
nīvārāṇāṃ jagatprāṇaṃ śiṃbānāṃ hari riṣyate
māṣāṇāṃ yakṣarājaṃca yavānāṃbrahmadaivatam 31
priyaṃgūṇāṃ mahāsenaṃ caṇakānāṃ bṛhaspatim
śyāmākānāṃ śriyaṃ devīṃkuludthānāṃtukacchapam 32
etaddhānyādhipā npūjya payobhiḥ kṣālaye tsudhīḥ
bījābhimaṃtraṇaṃ
yājātā iti maṃtreṇa bījānā mabhimaṃtraṇam 33
tatpātraṃ mūrthni śiṣyasya nyasyavāditranisvanaiḥ
devāgāraṃ parikrama prādakṣiṇyena vaipunaḥ 34
tadbhājanaṃ vinikṣipya (navavastreṇaveṣṭayet)navavastreṇa chādayet
aṃkurārcaṇa homavidhiḥ
puṇyāhaṃ tatra kurvīta kuṃḍe vā sthaṃḍilepi vā 35
agniṃ saṃsthāpya vidhivanmūlamaṃtreṇa deśikaḥ
samidhājyacarū nhutvānṛsūktena caruṃ hunet 36
somamaṃtreṇa juhuyā dājye nāṣṭottaraṃ śatam
saṃpātājyena saṃspṛsya sparśamaṃtreṇapālikāḥ 37
ājyaśeṣaṃ bījapātre nikṣipya japa mācaret
śatavāraṃ vidhormaṃtraṃ tato viprābhyanujñayā 38
bījāvāpanaṃ
muhūrte śobhane bījā nvāpaye tpālikādiṣu
brahmeṃdrāgni krameṇaiva dvādaśākṣaravidyayā 39
ūdhṛtāsīti maṃtreṇa mṛdbhirācchādayetkramāt
somakuṃbhastato yeva siṃce dabliṃgamaṃtrakaiḥ 40
pāvamānādisūktaiśca dikṣudadyā dbaliṃtataḥ
yāgāṃtaṃ somakuṃbhasya pūjanaṃ ca nivedanam 41
kumudādigaṇebhyaśca baliṃ dadyāddivāniśam
dīpān rakṣe danirvāṇā nyāvatkarmāvasānakam 42
aṃkurārpaṇa mārabhya dīkṣito gururīritaḥ
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
aṃkurārpaṇavidhirnā matrayodaśodhyāyaḥ
śrī śrī śrī
<14>
oṃ
śrīpāṃcarātre puruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
caturdaśodhyāyaḥ
vāstuyāgavidhiḥ
vāstu pūjāvasara nīrūpaṇaṃ
vāstuyāgaṃ tataḥ kuryā dvāstunādhasya prītaye
anarcate vāstunādhe kṛtaṃ karmāsuraṃbhavet 1
tasmā dārabhya māṇeṣu devayāgādikarmasu
prāgeva pūjaye dvāstuṃ tadvidhānaṃ bravīmite 2
vāstu maṃḍala racanā
bhūsūktena bhuvaṃ tatra govayenma vilepayet
raktapītasitai śchūrṇaiḥ raṃgavallīḥ prakalpayet 3
tasmi nnūtanavastreṇa maṃḍale taṃḍulaiḥkṛte
karṣamātrasuvarṇena tadardhārthena vai punaḥ 4
vittaśāṭhyaṃ vinā kuryā dvāstoḥ pratikṛtiṃ budhāḥ
ūrdhvagā daśarekhāsyu stiryagrekhāstathādaśa 5
vilikhenmaṃḍale tasmin ekāśītipadaṃ bhavet
pratimāśodhanavidhiḥ
pratimāśodhanaṃ kuryā tpaṃcāmṛtarasaiḥ vṛdhak 6
tasmīnmadhyapade vāstupratimāpūjanaṃ caret
vāstoṣpatetimaṃtraudvau (yadhāśakti japettadā) yathāśaktijapaṃcaret 7
āyutaṃ vāsahasraṃ vā śata maṣpottaraṃtu vā
kuṃbhasthāpanaṃ
tasmi nneva pade paṃcatvaksallavasamanvitam 8
sūtreṇa veṣṭitaṃ kūrcaṃ gaṃdhādibhi ralaṃkṛtam
(vāstukuṃbhaṃ tu saṃsthāpyaḥ) vāstukuṃbhaṃ samāvāhyakuṃbhetupratimāṃnyaset 9
vāstupūjā
prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ kurvīta pūjaye dvāstu pūruṣam
ṣoḍaśai rupacāraiśca dpātriṃśairvā yathākramam 10
somamaṃṭapa nirmāṇaṃ maṃḍala racanā
tatprācyā muttaraśyāṃ vā vidhiva tso mamaṃṭapam
saṃsthāpya dhānyavastrādyai staṃḍulaiśca yathākramam 11
tanmadhye ṣoḍacaśadalaṃ padmaṃ caiva sakarṇikam
likhitvā padma mabhyarca ṣoḍaśai rupacārakaiḥ 12
kuṃbhasthāpanaṃ pratimāśodhanaṃ
karṇikāyāṃ somakuṃbhaṃ sahetumaṇikūrcakam
rajitapratimāṃ kṛtvā somaṃ karṣapramāṇataḥ 13
paṃcagavyena saṃśodhya kuṃbhe vinyasya śāstrataḥ
prāṇapratiṣṭhā arcanaṃca
prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ kurvīta somamaṃtraṃ samuccaran 14
ṣoḍaśai rupacāraiśca somaṃ saṃpūjayedguruḥ
brahmādyaṣṭa dikpālakārcana vithiḥ
vāstu maṃḍalamadhyastha pada mārabhya bāhyataḥ 15
iṃdrādyaṣṭapade brahman viriṃcīn nava cārcayet
brahmajijñānamitivai maṃtraṃ japtyā guru ssvayam 16
prajāpatiṃ vithātāraṃ brahmāṇaṃ kamalāsanam
ajaṃ vidhiṃ śatānaṃdaṃ druhiṇaṃ viśvaretanam 17
āvāhayā mītyuccāryapūjayetparameṣṭhinam
tatpūrvapadaṣaṭketu māheṃdraṃtu samarcayet 18
āsatyeneti maṃtreṇa cāryamṇaṃ bhāskaraṃ tadhā
adityaṃ caiva saptāśvaṃ mārtāṃḍaṃ lokabāṃdhavam19
tadyāmyabadaṣaṭketu dharmarājaṃ samarcayet
yamāyasoma mityeta nmaṃtraṃsamyaksamuccaran 20
yamaṃ daṃḍadharaṃ kālaṃ kālāṃśaṃ ravinaṃdanam
lulāyavāhanaṃ caiva padaṣaṭke samarcayet 21
tatpaścā tpadaṣaṭketu yādasāṃ pati marcayet
imammevaruṇetyeta nmaṃtreṇa vidhi vattataḥ 22
varuṇaṃ makarārūḍhaṃ pāśapāṇiṃ pracetasam
surūpiṇa mapāṃnnādhaṃ prādakṣiṇyena pūjayet 23
kuberaṃ pūjayetṣaṭke cāpyāya śveticoccaran
kuberaṃ yakṣarājaṃca dhaneśaṃ guhyakeśvaram, 24
gadāpāṇiṃ dhanādhīśe padaṣaṭke samarcayet
agniṃ dūtaṃ japitvādha dhūmaketuṃ hutāśanam 25
saptajihvaṃ havyavāhaṃ āgneyāṃtu catuṣṭake
mokṣaṇuṃ maṃtra muccāryanirutiṃ naravāhanam 26
karburaṃ khaḍgahastaṃca nairutyāṃ tu catuṣṭake
tato vāyuṃ samuccārya jagatprāṇaṃsamīraṇam 27
mārutaṃ padamānaṃ ca vāyuvyāṃ ca catuṣṭake
tamīśānaṃ samuccārya gaurīnādhaṃ maheśvaram 28
īśānaṃ vṛṣabhārūḍhaṃ īśānyāṃtu catuṣṭake
devatāvāhanaṃ
īśānakoṇā dārabhya tadvahi ścārcayetsudhīḥ 29
yasya nāmādibhi rmaṃtrai ssamaṃtrapraṇavādikaiḥ
prādakṣiṇyena vidhiva ddvātriṃśatsu padeṣvapi 30
īśānaṃ caiva parjanyaṃ jayaṃtāditya mevaca
iṃdraṃca satyasaṃbādhaṃ bhṛśaṃ caivāṃtarikṣakam 31
agniṃ ca tatra coṣṇāṃśuṃ pūṣāṇaṃ vitadhaṃ tathā
yamaṃ grahakṣataṃ paścāt gaṃdharvaṃ bhṛṃgarājakam 32
ṛṣiṃ ca niṛrutiṃ caivā dauvārika mataḥparam
sugrīvaṃ puṣpadaṃtaṃ ca sarikṣnatyasuraṃ tadhā 33
poṣaṇaṃ nāgajapanaṃ nāgamukhya mataḥ param
guhyakaṃ caiva bhallāṭu somaṃ cānargalaṃ tathā 34
dvātriṃśatkala śāvāhanaṃ
aditiṃ sauradevaṃca tadbāhyeṣu ca pūjayet
evaṃ pūjāṃca nirvṛtya sauvarṇā nadha rājitān 35
tāmrajā nvādhakalaśā syate dākalaśe ṣviti
paṃcāmṛta samāyuktān paṃcatvakpaṃcapallavaiḥ 36
saptamṛdbhissamāyuktānnavaratna samanvitān
caturdaśauvadhīyuktā nnavaratna samanvitān 37
dvātriṃśa tparita sthsāpya taṃtunā veṣṭaye ttataḥ
aṣṭadi ṣvaṣṭakalaśā nvinyasya vidhivatkramāt 38
prācyāṃ bahiḥ kumāraṃcadakṣiṇasyāṃtubhāskaram
praticyāṃ jaṃbukaṃ deva mudīcyāṃ pilipiṃcakam 39
īśānyāṃdi gviśravasaṃ āgneyāṃ ca biḍālakam
nairutyāṃ pūtanāṃcaiva vāyuvyāṃpāparākṣasīm 40
pūjayetkalaśeṣvevaṃ (tattanmatrai pṛthak pṛthak)nnāmamaṃtraiḥpṛdhak pṛdhak
vāstuhomaṃ
kuṃḍevā sthaṃḍile caiva saṃsthā pyāgniṃ tu deśikaḥ 41
ithmādhānājyabhāgaṃtu kṛtvā śāstroktavartmanā
vāstunādhaṃ tato dhyātvā kuṃḍamadhye yadhāvidhi 42
āyutaṃvā sahasraṃvā śata maṣṭottaraṃ tu vā
japāddaśāṃśamadhavā samidhājyacarūn hunet 43
somamaṃtraṃtato hutvā śata maṣṭottaraṃ tuvā
maṃṭapasthita devānāṃ ttattatpadasusaṃkhyayā 44
samiccarughṛtai rhomaṃ kāraye tsarvasiddhaye
śamyapāmārgakhadhira samidbhirhomamācaret 45
parivārān tato sarvadevatān ssasvamaṃtrakauḥ
samidhājyacarūnhutvāpūrṇāhutyaṃtamācaret
vāstunādhādhidevānāṃ baliṃ dadyā dvidhānataḥ 46
vāstukuṃbha statoyeva yāgamaṃṭapabhūmiṣu
aghamarṣaṇasūktena śecaye dvāstukhuddhaye 47
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
caturdaśodhyāyaḥ

oṃ
śrīpāṃcarātre puruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
paṃcadaśodhyāyaḥ
paryagnikaraṇa jalādhivāsavidhiḥ
paryagni karaṇa vidhiḥ
tataḥpagni karaṇaṃ tadarthaṃ brāhmaṇaṃvaret
hastihasta samaṃsthūlaṃ paṃcahastatrihastakam 1
kuśakāśakṛtaṃpuṃjaṃ trimukhāgreṇasaṃyutam
tatpuṃjaṃvāstuhomāgnau prajvālyavidhipūrvaka 2
paryagnimaṃtramuccārya paribhramaṇamācaret
śālādi sarvadeśeṣu cālayādiṣu sarvataḥ 3
pradakṣiṇakrameṇaiva vedavādya purassaram
paṃcagavyaistataḥ kuryāt prokṣaṇaṃ yāgabhūmiṣu 4
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kāryaṃ brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ
śāṃtisūktānadhīyānaiḥ prokṣayetchuddhavāriṇā 5
dīpāsprajvālayettatragoghṛtenaca pūritān
kṛṣṇāgurumukhairgaṃdhairdhūpayetsarvadikṣuca 6
staṃbhān saṃveṣṭyanāsobhi śśubhravasrairvitānayet
dhvajaiścasupatākābhi rmuktāhāraiścapallavaiḥ 7
anyaiḥpuṣsairbhalaiścaiva maṇikāṃcanabhūṣaṇaiḥ
naphalāḥkadalīsthaṃbhaiḥ kramukai storaṇādibhiḥ 8
śobhayenmaṃṭapaṃ sarvaṃ yathāvittānusārataḥ
yāgaśālājā
tatastu yāgaśālāyāḥ pūjākāryā caturmukha 9
dvārāṇāṃ pārśvayoḥghātvādvitālāsyavaṭānivai
śeṣuna stāpayet sthaṃbhān toraṇārdhaṃ yadhāvidhi 10
aśvaddhaṃ pūrvadigbhāge yāmye cauduṃbaraṃ tathā
syagrodhaṃ paścimdvāre plakṣaṃ caivottaretadhā 11
dhvajavdaye sthāpanīyaṃ pratidvārasya pārśvayoḥ
vrācyāṃraktadhvajesthā pyau pītavarṇauca dakṣiṇe 12
paścame nīlavarṇau ca uttare pāṃḍuradhvajāḥ
sthāpayetpūrṇa kalaśān sāpidhānān sacavastrakān 13
saratna hema kūrcāṃbu pūritānsūtraveṣṭitān
suśobhavaṃ subaṃdhaṃ ca subhadraṃ ca suhotrakam 14
ete toraṇadevā syuḥ dhvajādhīṣṭhāna devatāḥ
kumudaḥ kumudākṣa śca puṃḍarīka śca vāmanaḥ 15
śaṃkhukarṇa ssarpanetra ssumukha ssupratiṣṭhitaḥ
pūrṇaśca puṣkara ścaiva ānaṃdo naṃdaeva ca 16
vīrasenasuśeṣeṇau ca saṃbhavaprabhavautataḥ
kuṃbhādhidevatā hytāssamāvāhyayajetkramā 17
aṣṭadikṣupatākāsu digīśā strāsprapūjayet
aṃtarālikā pūjā
āvāhyapūjayedvidpān iṃdreśā nāṃtarevasūn 18
hudāśaneṃdrayormadhyairudrānāvāhyapūjayet
yamasyāgne raṃtarāle ādityānarcayetkramātḥ 19
yamarākṣasayo rmadhyesāthyānsaṃpūjyayetsudhī
varuṇakoṇapapadhye tu abhyarca maruttaḥ stataḥ 20
marujjaleśāṃtarāle aśvinau ca prapūjayet
kuberamarutormadhye viśvāndevā(nyajetkramāt)pprapūjayet 21
īśānasomayormadhye caṃdodevā sathārcayet
evaṃ saṃpūjaye ttattaddevatāśca yathākramam 22
śilpaśālāgamanaṃ śilpipūjanaṃca
madhyāhn samaye prāpte ṛtvigbhissahadeśikaḥ
śilpiśālaṃ rato gatvāpratisā ssaṃparīkṣayet 23
muhūrteśobhane prāpte vaiṣṇavānā manujñayā
śīlpībhirnirmite biṃbe śāstradṛṣṭena partmanā 24
parīkṣya lakṣaṇaṃ samyak kṣālaye tbaṃcagavyakaiḥ
tataḥ prakṣālyatoyena gaṃdhayuktena deśikaḥ 25
bhūmau samyaksamālipya puṣpāṇivikirettataḥ
tatraivaśāyayedbiṃbaṃ śilpinaṃ pūjayettataḥ 26
dhanaiśca karmānuguṇaṃ sthapatiṃ paritoṣayet
nayanodghāṭanaṃ kāryaṃśilpinā kuśalena vai 27
mānonmāna pramāṇaśāṃti homādīni
gāṃca datvāprayatnena śīlpinetu yathāvidhi
tathāparāhmasamaye śāṃtiromaṃ samācaret 28
mānonmānapramāṇekhyhāniryājāyatenṛṇām
sahānirvilayaṃ yāti śāṃtihomekṛte sati 29
śamīpallavasaṃyuktai stilaissājtyeścraruṃ svayam
saptavyāhṛtibhiścaiva pratyekaṃ juhuyādguruḥ 30
nṛsūktena caruṃpaścāt juhuyātṣoḍaśāhutīḥ
paṃcopaniṣadairmaṃtrai ssarpiṣājūhuyā cchatam 31
pratimāsannidhā veva hutvā pūrṇā hutiṃ tata
upatiṣṭhtatadanu maṃtreśānena deśikaḥ 32
namastubhyaṃ bhagavate jātavedasvarūpiṇe
nārāyaṇāya havyasya kavyasya ca yadhā tadham 33
bhoktreyaṣṭavyadevānāṃ ātmane paramātmane
sannidhatsyaciraṃ devapratimāyāṃ hitāyanaḥ 34
evaṃ vijñāpyadeveśaṃ devasyatveti saṃspṛśet
dukūlaśitaśiddhārthayukta mūrṇāmayena tu 35
suvarṇena tadā veṣṭhya badhnīyā ddakṣiṇekare
viṣṇorarāṭamaṃtreṇa saptavārābhimaṃtritam 36
rathaṃ vā kuṃjaraṃ vāpi śibikāṃ vāyathāruci
pratimā ssamyagāropya grāmaṃkṛtvāpradakṣiṇam 37
jalādhivāsavidhiḥ
pāṃḍurai rātapatraiśca cāmarai śca sitaissaha
cīnāṃśukamayai cchatraistabarṇamayai rapi 38
vyajanai rapi kalyāṇai rthvajairnānāvidhaistadhā
śaṃkhaduṃdubhinirghoṣau (nṛttageyasamāvṛtaiḥ)rnṛttagītasamanvitaiḥ 39
mahūpacārai raparairbrāhmaṇaissahadeśikaḥ
[adhikapāṭhāni
tūryavāditranirghoṣai rvedamaṃgalapāṭhakaiḥ
jayaghoṣaiśca stutibhiḥbhaktairbhāgavataissaha]
śanaiśśanairnayeddevaṃ jalapārśvaṃ yadhoditam 40
jalādhivāsadeśaṃ tu saṃprāpya śibikādibhiḥ
(avaropya jale devaṃ)avaropya jalābhyarṇe prapāyāṃ siṃhaviṣṭhare 41
jalādhivāsayogyasthalāni
prāṅmakhaṃ ca samaṃ sthāpya yadvākuryādudaṅmukham

saṃbhave sati kurvīta jalavāsaṃ yathāvidhi
jalādhivāsānarhasthalāni
alpatoye smaśāne ca lavaṇodakadūṣite 43

jalādhivāsakramaḥ
puṇyāhavācanā tpūrvaṃ sadyādiṣu susaṃsthitāḥ
āpaḥpuruṣamaṃtreca saṃjoṣyaguru rātmavān 48
āgnimaṃtreṇa tattoyaṃ saṃśoṣya tadanaṃtaram
jalādyamṛtabīyena puna rāpūrayedguruḥ 49
tasmin jale mahāpīṭhaṃ pratimāyāmasammitam
kāṣṭhajaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ snigdhaṃ(vinyase nmūrtimaṃtrataḥ)vinyane ccajalāśayaye 50
(maṃgalāstaraṇopetaṃ) vinyasyā staraṇopetaṃ sopadhānaṃ navaṃ śubham
prakalpayedyogapīṭhaṃ tasyopari yadhākramam 51
graṃthapūṣpādinā pīṭha marcayitvā samāhitaḥ
dvāratoraṇakuṃbhādī narcayettadanaṃtaram 52
pratimāṃ paṃcagavyena paṃcopaniṣadaiḥ kramāt
snāpayetprāṅmukhobhūtvāśuddhasnānaṃ samācaret 53
evaṃ kuryā ddvidhānena ghṛtāropaṇa pūrvakam
ārghyaṃ pādyaṃ tathācāmaṃ vastraṃ yajñopavītakam 54
bhūṣaṇāni ca sarvāṇi dvādaśākṣaravidyayā
datvātatorcāṃ saṃhāra mārgeṇaivatu saṃharet 55
tattvasaṃhārakramaṃ
saṃhārasya kramaṃ vakṣye prāṅmukho vā pyudaṅmukhaḥ
pūrvaṃ saṃhṛtya prāgādīn śrotrādīncatataḥparaṃ 56
śabdādīṃ ścatato dhyātvā pṛthivyādīnyathākramam
mana ādau mana statve mahānavyaktaṃ saṃjñake 57
pṛdhivī cāpsulīnāsyā dagnau cāpastathālayam
agne śca vāyu mārgeṣu ākāśe vāyutattvakam 58
ākāśasyā dahaṃkāre ahaṃkāro mahāṃtike
maha davyaktakelīna mavyaktaṃ cātmani sthitam 59
evaṃ saṃhṛtyatattvāni dhyāyeddeva manāmayam
cchādayitvā śubhairdarbhairnavavastrai sragādibhiḥ 60
āpīṭhamauliparyaṃtaṃ nicchidraṃ veṣṭhaye dbudhaḥ
jalamadhyagataṃ pīṭha mācāryo mūrtipaissaha 61
śaṃkhaduṃdubhinirṣoṣai rānayetpratimāstataḥ
udaṅmukhaṃ prāksirasaṃ (pratimā śāyayedguruḥ)pratimāṃ śāyayettataḥ 62
śayānasya tu biṃbasya dakṣiṇe tu mahāghaṭaṃ
vinyase dbrahmabījena pūjāṃtnaiva kārayet 63
(dikpālakuṃbhān saṃsthāpya cāṣṭadikṣu ca deśikaḥ) vinyase llokapālāṃ śca svāsudikṣu ca deśikaḥ
maṃgalā nyabhita sthāpya śakalīkaraṇaṃ tataḥ 64
(jalaṃ kṣīrārṇavaṃ matvā) jalaṃ kṣīrārṇavaṃ dhyātvā pīṭhaṃ śeṣaiti smaret
jalādu ttīrya maṃtrajño (rakṣāṃ kuryā dyadhāvidhi) rakṣāṃtatravinirdiśet 65
darmayeccakramudrāṃca rakṣāmudrāṃ pradarśayet
jalādivāsa kālaparimitiḥ nadyādyabhāve jalavāsavithiḥ
(trirātra meka rātraṃ vā yāmaṃ yāmārdha mevavā mūhūrtakālamapi vā jalavāsaṃ vidhīyate)trirātra mekarātraṃ vā jalavāsaṃ samācaret 66
nadyādyabhāve biṃbasya jalādhivasanaṃ bhavet

brahmā

śayane vedikāyāṃ ca kaḥ kālaḥ kamalāpate 1
śrī bhagavān
śrībhagavatprati jalādhivāsā nāṃ yogya nirūpaṇaṃ
pradoṣe pyadhivāsanya śayanaṃtu mahāniśi
pradiṣṭhāṃca divā kuryā nmuhūrte śāstracodite 2
idānīṃ pakṣyate samyak bhadrakasya tu lakṣaṇam
(caturasraṃ sthalaṃ kṛtvā)caturaśrakṛtaṃ kṣetraṃ cāṣṭadhā vibhajetpunaḥ 3
bhadraka maṃḍala racanākramaḥ
śatārtha mekahīnaṃ tu koṣṭhakāni bhavaṃti hi
madhyato navabhi rbhāgaiḥ padmaṃ ca parikarpayet 4
(karṇikā kesarayutaṃ)karṇikākesarai ryuktaṃ daladvādaśaśobhitam
padadvayesa dvārāṇi navakāni bhavaṃti hi 5
ṣaṭkaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ padānāṃtu pratikoṇekumārjayet
upakaṃṭha prasidhyardhaṃ dvārāṇevaṃ bhavaṃti ca 6
varṇaracanā
(paṃcavarṇai ścacṇūraiśca maṃḍalaṃ pūrayettataḥ) paṃcavarṇna cūrṇena krameṇaparipūrayet
prāgdvāraṃ śvetavarṇena kṛṣenaivatu dakṣiṇaṃ 7
paścimaṃ raktavarṇena (pīte vaivatu cottaraṃ)pītavarṇna cottaram
upadvārāṇicāṣṭautu śyāmavarṇena pūrayet 8
paṃcavarṇaistu padmaṃ syāt dvitīye naina karṇikā
catuṣkoṇān likhe dvarṇai(rnīla pītasitāsitaiḥ)rnīlapītasitādibhiḥ 9
bījākṣaralekhanaṃ
rajobhi revaṃ saṃpūrya madhye tu praṇavaṃ likhe
(dvādaśākṣara maṃtraṃtu)nāmāni keśavādīnāṃ daladvādaśake likhet 10
vāsudevādināmāni caturdvāreṣu vinyaset
iṃdrādīnāṃ likhennāmā nyupadvāreṣu cāṣṭasu 11
anenaiva prakāreṇa raṃjaye dbhadrakaṃ sudhīḥ

[adhikapāṭhāṇi

teṣāṃ praśasta meta nmaṃḍalaṃ śīghra dthikṛt
