vāstupuruṣāvāhanaṃ
tataḥprabhāte vimale mūrtipaissahadeśikaḥ
yāgamaṃṭapabhūbhāge dakṣiṇe bhūtale śucau 1
vāstunādhaṃ likhttatra paṃcavarṇai śca cūrṇakaiḥ
adhomukhaṃ prāksirasam pāṇipādauca koṇayoḥ 2
saptadarbhakṛtaṃ kūrcaṃ tasmannikṣipyapūjayet
vāstu pūjā vidhiḥ
vāstunādhaṃ ca gaṃdhādyaiḥ pūja yityā tataḥparam 3
vāstudevaśiro deśe pūjaye daṃśumālinam
dakṣiṇe bāhumūletu kāmamāvāhya pūjayet 4
kumāraṃ kūrpare caiva hasta vināyakam
aśvinau ca padadvaṃtve caṃdraṃ dehasya madhṛtaḥ 5
pāṇautu dakṣiṇe durgāṃ kūrpare mātara stadhā
vāme tu bāhumūletu sthāṇuṃ hṛdayapadmane 6
(māṃ hariṃ nābhideśetu) māṃcaiva nābhideśetu brahmāṇaṃtuprapūjayt
dikṣvaṣṭāsu ca dikpālān kṣetranādhamadhottare 7
pārśv saṃpūjya tadvāstu paścime deśikottamaḥ
agniṃ saṃsthāpya vidhiva tpaṃcopaniṣadā hunet 8
sarpiṣā śatavāraṃ tu sahasraṃ vā yadhāruci
āpāmārgśca śamyai śca khādhirai rjuhuyāttataḥ 9
samirbhirmūlamaṃtreṇa tattanmaṃtrārṇasaṃkhyayā
sūktena pauruṣeṇaiva carūṇā ṣoḍaśāhutīḥ 10
juhuyā dvāstudevān ca caruṇāca sakṛtsakṛt
dadyātkṣetrādhināthāya devebhyaśca baliṃkramāt 11
anena vidhinā kṛtvā vāstunādhasya pūjanam
jalāduddharaṇavithiḥ
aparāhṇtu saṃprāpte mūrtipaissahadeśikaḥ 12
sṛttagītādivādyaiśca nadītīraṃ praveśayet
jalādbiṃbaṃ samuddhāpya sṛṣṭikrama manusmaran 13
kalaśasthāśca kaṃbhastha (devatyodāvanaṃ caret)devatā visṛjettadā
bibāni viṣṭare tīre prāṅmukhaṃ vā pyudaṅmukhaṃ 14
samavasthākya biṃbeṣu lohajānāṃ viśudthaye
tiṃtriṇīphalatoyena śodhayitvātu mūrtivaiḥ 15
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
nayanonmīlanaṃ kṛtyā maṃgalāni pradarśyaca]
vastre rābharaṇaiḥ puṣpairgaṃdhaiśca vividhaiśśubhaiḥ
manohara malaṃkṛtya biṃbā nyāropya yānake 16
tūryamaṃgalavāditrairninaye dyāgamaṃṭapam
maṃṭapasyottare bhāge viṣṭare sanni veśayet 17
iti śrīpāṃcarātremahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
vāstupuruṣāvāhana jaloddharaṇavidhi rnāma saptadaśodhyoyaḥ
śrīśrīśrī
<18>
oṃ
śrīmatpāṃcarātre śrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
aṣṭādaśodhyāyaḥ
pratiṣṭhāsnapana vidhiḥ
nayanonmīlanaṃ
adhāta ssaṃpravakṣyāmi pratiṣṭhāsnapanakramam
nayanonmīlanaṃ kuryādbiṃbasya tu vithānataḥ 1
madhuvāteti maṃtreṇa dṛṣṭimudghāṭanaṃ caret
snapanārtharakṣā baṃdhanaṃ
mnapanārdhaṃ tataḥ kuryā drakṣābaṃdhana māditaḥ 2
(sauvarṇakṣaumakārpāsa sūtraiḥkuryā dyadhāruci)sauvarṇa sūtraṃ kṣaumaṃ vā nocetkārpāsamevavā
taṃtubhiḥ paṃcabhi rvāpi saptabhi rdākṛtaṃ śuci 3
ṣaṣṭyuttaraśataiścaiva kramukaistaccaturguṇai
kramukārthatadtharai rvā kadalyādiphalairyutam 4
khāridvayapramāṇena taṃḍulaina ca pūrit
sauvarṇe rājite vāpi tāmrapātredhavā guruḥ
nikṣipya vastramācchādya paricāraka mūrdhini
vinyasya nṛttagītādyaiḥ chatracāmaravaibhavaiḥ 6
aneka dīpasaṃyuktaṃ (vitānadhvajaśobhitam) vitānena samanvitaḥ
śākunādimahāsūkta paṭhadbhiḍbrāhmaṇainsaha 7
devāgāraṃ parikramya trivāraṃ yāgamaṃṭa pe
biṃbasya pūrvadigbhāge gomayāliptabhūtale 8
tatbātraṃtu vinikṣipya sūtramaṃtreṇa pūjayet
prokṣyatoyenatatsūtraṃgṛhītvāhastayordvayoḥ 9
aṃguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃtu aparājitamaṃtrataḥ
caṃdanaṃ ca samālipya sūtraṃ biṃbasya dakṣiṇe 10
hastecaiva śriyādīnāṃ badhnīyā ddakṣiṇe tare
sūtraṃ dakṣiṇahastena spṛśa nne kāgramānasaḥ 11
astramaṃtraṃ japitvādha devamarṣyādibhi ryajet
snapana maṃṭapa lakṣaṇaṃ
snapanārthaṃ prakurvīta maṃṭapaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam 12
daśahastapravistāraṃ dviguṇenāyataṃ śubham
caturdikṣu catuddvāraṃ toraṇaiśca virājitam 13
maṃṭapaṃ tattridhākṛtvā tṛtīyāṃ sedha paścime
caturhastāṃ snānavedīṃ caturaśrāṃ manoharām 14
hastocchedhā mūrthyabhāge vahayena virājitām
kṛtvā taduttare pārśve jalakulyāṃ prakalpayet 15
tanmaṃṭapasya caiśānye sugaṃtha jalapūritām
jaladroṇīṃ kaṭāhaṃ vā nikṣipyā cchādaye jjalam 16
navena vāsasā veṣṭhya pavamānaṃ samuccaran
abhimaṃtyratatorghyādi pūjā dravyāṃ śca kalpaye 17
ekāśīti kalaśasthāpana maṃḍalalakṣaṇaṃ
caṃdanārdrāṇi sūtrāṇi caturdaśanipādayet
tatodagāyatāni syuḥkoṣṭhānyekona naptatiḥ 18
śataṃtu sūtravistāraṃ ṣoḍaśāṃgula sammitam
aṣṭadikṣu tathā madhye pratyekaṃ navabhūmiṣu 19
navakāṣṭhāni saṃgṛhya śeṣakoṣṭhāni mārjayet
ekāśītipade ṣvevaṃ vrīhibhistaṃḍulaistilaiḥ 20
turyāṃśena kramā tkuryā tpṛdhakpīṭhānikalpaye
vīthikāsu kuśānyasya sauvarṇān rājitāstathā 21
kalaśa lakṣaṇaṃ
tāmrajān mṛṇmayā nvādhakalaśān lakṣaṇānvitān
pakvabiṃbasamānābhān acchidrā nphoṭavarjitān 22
svanavaṃtā nabhinnāṃśca kālamaṃḍalavarjitān
kalaśān viṣṇugāyatyrā prakṣālya navataṃtubhiḥ 23
tatuṃ tanveti maṃtreṇa veṣṭyapaścimabhūtale
dhyānyapīṭhaṃ vithā yāsmi nkalaśānadhivāsaye 24
viṣṇumaṃtreṇa prāgagrā sudagagrānkuśān nyaset
kuśeṣu teṣu tatkubhānnikṣive ttānadhomukhān 25
praṇavaṃ samya guccārya tatkuṃbhopari pūrvavat
paristīrya tato darbhāsmaṃtreṇa parameṣṭhinā 26
puruṣe ṇārghyatoyenaprokṣayetprāṅmukhassvayam
viśvenākṣatavinyāsaṃ kṛtvā kuṃbhāni tānivai 27
uttānāni tataḥ kṛtvā nivṛtyā hvayavidyayā
ājyena viṣṇugāyatyrā śu maṣṭottaraṃ huset 28
saṃpātājyena saṃssmasyanna panadravyasaṃcayam
kalaśasthāpana vidhiḥ
jalena pūrayedardhaṃ kalaśā nviṣṭuvidyayā 29
madhye kuṃbheghṛtaṃ nyasya prācyāmuṣṇodakaṃ nyaset
ratna kuṃbhaṃ ta dhāgneye phalakuṃbhaṃ tu dakṣiṇe 30
nairute lohakuṃbhaṃ tu vāruṇyāṃ mārjanodakam
gaṃdhodakaṃ ca vāyavyāṃ kauberyā makṣatodakam 31
yanodakaṃ ta dhaiśānyāṃ tadbahiḥ pūrvadiksthite
navake madhyame pādyakuṃbhaṃ tatra niveśayet 32
arghyaṃ yāmyetadhācāmaṃ vāruṇe cottaretathā
paṃcagavyaṃ tadhāgneyemadhye dadhighaṭaṃ nyaset 33
nairutau ca tadhā kṣīraṃ vāyau madhughaṭaṃ nyaset
kaṣāyakuṃbha mīśānyāṃ sarvatraparitoghaṭīn 34
śuddhodakāṃ śca tuṣṣaṣṭi maṣṭā vaṣṭau niveśayet
caturviṃśati darbhaiścakūrcāṃ kṛtvātu madhyame 35
ghṛtakuṃbhenyase tyeṣa jalakuṃbheṣu sarvaśaḥ
saptabhiḥ paṃcabhi rvādha kṛtānkūrcān vinikṣipet 36
pidhāya kuṃbhān cakreṇaśarāvairveṣṭayetkramāt
vāsobhirdravyakalaśān imaṃ vastrāṇi maṃtrataḥ 37
anyānekaikavastreṇacchādaye nmaṃtra muccaran
ṛtvigbhissahadevasya mannidheṃ prāpya deśikaḥ 38
vedavādyeṣu ghṣeṣu pravṛttenartanādike
homaṃ
ājyena mūlamaṃtreṇa juhuyātkuṃbhasaṃkhyayā 39
saṃpātājyena kuṃbheṣu devatāvāhanaṃ caret
kalaśādhidevatā vāhanavidhiḥ
ghṛtakuṃbhe vāsudevaṃ uṣṇakuṃbhe tu pūruṣam 40
phalakuṃbhe satyadevaṃ acyutaṃ mārjarodake
ānaṃtaṃ bījakalaśe ratnakuṃbhe tu keśavam 41
nārāyaṇaṃ lohakuṃbhe gaṃdhatoyetu mādhavam
yavodaketu goviṃdaṃ podye viṣṇuṃ samarcayet 42
madhusūdana marghyetu kuṃbhe cācamanīyake
trivikramaṃ paṃcagavye vāmanaṃ śrīdharaṃ tathā 43
dadhikuṃbhe kṣīraghaṭe hṛṣīkeśaṃ samarcayet
padmanābhaṃ madhughaṭetvakkaṣāyodakekramāt 44
dāmodaraṃ yajedatra pīṭhapūjāpurassaram
kalaśāspadadhānyeṣu kalaśeṣu vasuṃdharām 45
kūrceṣvastraṃ cakrikāsu hetirājaṃ tadhaivaca
vastreṣu viṣṇuṃ saṃpūjya snāpaye duktavartmanā 46
viṣṇugāyatyrāca ṛtiguddhṛtaṃ pādyakuṃbhakam
hastegṛhītva kūrcena tatkubhasthitavāriṇā 47
snapanaṃ
idaṃ viṣṇuriti pokṣya snāpaye tpuruṣottamam
mūlamaṃtreṇa cārghyādi dīpāṃtairarcayedvibhum 48
pratidravyaghaṭasnāne mūrdhānaṃ diva ityṛcā
mṛdbhi rekosaviṃśadbhi rālipya kamalāsanaḥ 49
paṃcavāruṇikairmaṃtaiḥjalaiḥprakṣālyadeśikaḥ
āpove tyarghyatoyena pūrvavatsnāpayedtharim 50
tadpiṣṭoriti maṃtreṇa tadhācamanavāriṇā
pavitraṃ teti maṃtreṇa paṃcaganyābhiṣecanam 51
ghṛtamaṃtreṇa madhyastha ghṛtakuṃbhābhiṣecanam
dadhikrāvaṇṇamaṃtreṇa dadhnā devābhiṣecanam 52
āpyāyasveti maṃtreṇa vayobhimnapanaṃ caret
madhuvāteti madhunā oṣadhīririmaṃtrataḥ 53
kaṣāyavāriṇā snānaṃ devadevasya śāṅinṇaḥ
mānastoketi maṃtreṇa uṣṇakuṃbhābhiṣecanam 54
vaṣaṭteti ṛcā kuryā dratna vāryabhiṣecanam
phaninītyanuvākena phalāṃbusnāna mācaret 55
hiraṇya garbhamaṃtreṇa lohatoyābhiṣecanam
trātāramiti maṃtreṇa snāpaye dakṣatāṃbhasā 56
idaṃviṣṇuritisnānaṃ kuryādvaiyavavāriṇā
haridrāsnapanaṃ kuryāt śrīsūktena tataḥ param 57
sahasradhārayā śuddhasnānaṃ kuryācchubhairbhalaiḥ
evaṃsaṃ snāpyadeveśaṃ maṃḍalārādhanaṃ caret 68
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
pratiṣṭhāsnapanavidhirnāma aṣṭādaśodhyāyaḥ
śrīśrīśrī
<19>
oṃ
śrīpāṃcarātreśrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
ekānaviṃśodhyāyaḥ
śayyādhivāsavithiḥ
śrībhagavānuvāca
śayyādhivāsavidhiḥ
ataḥparaṃ prapakṣyāmi śayyāvāsakramaṃ param
pratiṣṭhākarma saṃsiddhirbhava tyenana saṃśayaḥ
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
maṃṭapaṃ kalpaye dādau sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutaṃ
ṣoḍaśastaṃbhasaṃhitaṃ alaṃgārai ralaṃkṛtaṃ 1
kadalīpūgapunnāga kramukādi tarūntathā
kuśapuṣpādi mālāśca vitānadhvajaketanāḥ 2
dvārakuṃbhāṃstoraṇānidarpaṇāniyathākramaṃ
adhāṣṭamaṃgalāṃ caiva alaṃkṛtya samaṃtataḥ 3
guggulvāgarudhūpāni dīpamālāni kalbayet
sugaṃdhenā nulipyādha raṃgavallīṃ sama 4
palānicitrā nvinyasya mathye maṃḍalamālikhet
svastikaṃbhadrakaṃvāpi cakrābjaṃcetaraṃtuvā 5]
maṃḍalopariśayyāṃ tu kāraye duktamārgataḥ
śayyāparikalpanaṃ
dvādaśākṣaramaṃtreṇa vedikāṃ prākṣaye ttataḥ
prāgagrānudagagrāṃśca (kuśānāstīryamaṃtravit)darbhānāstīryapuṣkalam
śālīnāṃ navabhāraistu taṃḍulānāṃ tadarthakaiḥ 3
tilānāṃ taṃḍulā rthaiśca pīṭhaṃ (dhānyapīṭhaṃ prakalpayet)kuryā tsavistaram
tasyopari vyāghracarma tūlikāṃ tadanaṃtaram 4
mṛdvāstaraṇa saṃyuktāṃ (nikṣipeṃdratna kaṃbalaṃ)kaṃbalānyuparinyaset
kṣaumaṃ tasyopari nyanya citravastrāṇi nikṣipet 5
(dhautavastrāṇi tadanu)tata śśuklāni vastrāṇi sovadhānāni sarvaśaḥ
śayanāṃgāni cāsādya kastūrīgaṃdhavāsitām 6
nānāpuṣpasamākīrṇāṃ nānālaṃkāraśobhitām
bhagavatośayanaṃ
(kṛtvāśayyāṃtato devaṃ)śayyāṃ prakalpadeveśaṃ śāyayedvidhinā tataḥ 7
brāhmaṇaiḥ pauruṣaṃ sūkta muccaradbhiryadhātadhaṃ
tūryaghoṣairmaṃgalaiśca mūrtipāścagurustathā 8
uttiṣṭhetyādi maṃtraiśca biṃba muddhāpya yatnataḥ
śayyāvedībhuvaṃ nītvākuṃḍānāṃ bāhyamārgataḥ 9
pradakṣiṇakrameṇaiva śayyāyāṃ śāyayedguruḥ
viśvatacchakṣu riti ca yadvaiṣṇava miti smṛtam 10
(mūrti maṃtraiścāvayāccha)tanmaṃtrābhyāṃśāyayeccamaṃdirābhimukhaṃ(yathā)vibhum
śriyādīni biṃbāni pārśvayorviniveśayet 11
yuvāsuvāsa iti ca vastreṇācchādayettataḥ
dhvajatoraṇakuṃbhādīn pūjayitvā yadhākramam, 12
ācchādaye ttoraṇāni navairvāsobhireva ca
prāgādidvāradeśeṣu ṛgādipaṭhatoddvijān 13
saṃsthāpya deśiko devaṃ hṛdayetu samāhitaḥ
(dhyānaṃ kṛtvā)dhyātvā satvā tataḥ kuṃbhānaṣṭadikṣu vinikṣipet 14
thāsyarāśiṣu śaṃkhaṃ ca cakraṃ padmaṃ gadāṃ tadhā
śrīvatsagaruḍaṃ kūrmaṃ dhvajaṃ svarṇamayaṃkṣipet 15
kuṃbhānā maṃtarāleṣu nikṣive nmaṃgalāṣṭakam
sāukurāḥ pālikā sthāpya vedikāyāṃ samaṃtataḥ 16
aṣṭau viṣṇvādimūrtīn ca kuṃbhe ṣvaṣṭanupūjayet
devasya vāyudigbhāgethyā rāśau navastrakam 17
mahākuṃbhasthāpanaṃ
sāpidhānaṃ maṇisvarṇa divyagaṃdhāṃbupūritam
mahākuṃbhaṃtu saṃsthāpya tasya pārśv galaṃtikām 18
kūrcasūtrāṃbarai ryuktāṃpuṇyatīrthāṃbupūritām
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
phalapuṣpa samāyuktaṃ suvarṇapratimāyutām]
niveśya pāyasānnena brāhmaṇā nbhojaye(tsuthīḥ)ttataḥ 19
homaṃ
tato homaṃ prakurvīta ṛtvijo deśikājñayā
mathitaṃ maṇijaṃ vāpi laukikaṃ vāgni mānayet 20
dvādaśākṣaramaṃtreṇa prokṣya puṇyāhavāriṇā
divyāgniṃ manasā dhyātvāpratyakkuṃḍetuvinyaset 21
darbhaiśca sarvakuṃḍe paryagnikaraṇaṃ caret
sdhāvaye tsarvakuṃḍeṣu pratyakkuṃḍāgni mevaca 22
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
prācyā māhavanīyaṃ ca dakṣiṇedakṣiṇāgni
pratīcyāṃ gārṇapatyāgni rutture sadhyanāmakaḥ
agneye ādasadhyāgnimānikramaśojapet]
agnimadhyasthapadmeṣu āghārādīnyaje tkramāt
vāsudevaṃ tu pūrvāgnau yāmysaṃkarṣaṇaṃtathā 23
pradyumnaṃ paścime kuṃḍe tvanirudtha madhottare
(nārāyaṇaṃ padmakuṃḍe arcayeccha yadhātadhaṃ (padmakuṃḍe śriyaṃdevī māvāhya vidhinārcayet)padmakuṃḍe mahālakṣmīṃdhyāyayetpujayettataḥ 24
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
agni bījena matimān vāyumaṃtra manusmaran]
śālādakṣiṇapārśvtu sthāpyadhenucatuṣṭayaṃ
gaṃgā sarasvati godā yamunā stāsu pūjayet 25
tāduhitvā pāyasānnaṃ pācayessavicakṣaṇaḥ
prākkuṃḍe jūhūyā dagnā vaṣṭottarasahasrakaṃ 26
kuṃḍasthadevamaṃtreṇa pāyasānnaṃ tuhomayet
kṛsarānnaṃ dakṣiṇāgnau guḍānnaṃ paścimetathā 27
haridrānna mudīcyāṃ tu (mudgānna mītarāgniṣu)dugthānna mitarāgniṣu
tilaśāli (yavavrīhi)yavai ścaiva veṇu bījairyathākramaṃ 28
ṛtvijo juhuyā ssarve gurvarujñāpurassaraṃ
kuṃbhāvāhana vidhiḥ
evaṃ jutvikṣu juhvatsu mahākuṃbhe, guru ryajet 29
paraṃjyoti rjñānaghanaṃ samāvāhyacyucyutaṃ hariṃ
mūlamaṃtreṇa cārṣyādyai śpakalīkṛtya pūjayet 30
sudarśanaṃ tu karake jvālābhiḥ pariveṣṭitaṃ
sahaskādityasaṃkāśaṃ sahasrāraṃ yadhāvidhiḥ 31
ṣaḍakṣareṇa maṃtreṇa cāvāhya svayamarcayet
tattva saṃhāra nyāsahomādīni
śayanasdhanya devasya nyāsaṃ saṃhāra meva ca 32
kṛtvāsṛṣṭi krameṇaiva (sṛjttatvāni deśikaḥ)punastatvāni saṃsṛjet
tattvāni goghṛtenaiva prākkuṃḍe jūhuyāttataḥ 33
aṣṭottaraśaraṃ vāpi cāṣṭāviṃśatireva vā
aṣṭauvā tuyathāśakti(kuryā dāhutaya stadā (dadyā dāhutamo guruḥ)juhuyā ttupṛdhak pṛdhak 33
ttattvanyāsasyahomaṃtu kṛtvaivaṃ tadanaṃtaraṃ
daśaprāṇādivāyūnāṃ nyāsaṃ kuryādiḍādiṣu 34
ṣoḍaśanyāsakramaḥ
matsyādivyatirikteṣu ṣoḍananyāsa mīritaṃ
pradhamaṃ praṇavanyāsaṃ vyāhṛtī nāṃ dvitīyakaṃ 35
tṛtīya makṣaranyāsaṃ ṛkṣāṇāṃ ca caturthakaṃ
(kālanyā saṃ paṃcamaṃca)kālasya paṃcamaṃ jñeyaṃ vāsarasyatuṣaṣṭhakam 36
varṇanyāsaṃ saptamaṃ tu toyanyāsa madhāṣṭamaṃ
navamaṃ nigamanyāsaṃ devānāṃ daśamaṃ hitaṃ 37
ekādaśaṃ virājaṃ cakratunyāsaṃ tu dvādaśaṃ
trayodaśaṃ guṇanyāsaṃ mūrtinyāsaṃ caturdaśaṃ 38
śaktinyāsaṃ tataḥproktaṃ lokanyā saṃtu ṣoḍaśaṃ
śāṃtihomaṃ
kṛtvaivaṃ ṣoḍaśanyāsaṃ śāṃtihomaṃ samācaret 39
prākkuṃḍemadhuvāhutvābhūsvāhetiśatāhutīḥ
saṃpātājyaṃ tusaṃgṛhya spṛseddvasya pādayoḥ 40
tadhaiva dakṣiṇe kuṃḍe bhuvasvāyeti maṃtrataḥ
payasā ca tatohutvā hṛdayaṃ saṃnpṛśedbudhaḥ 41
tataḥ paścimakuṃḍe tusuvasvāhetimaṃtrataḥ
dadhnāhutvā mukhaṃ npṛnya cottaretriśrakuṃḍake 42
sarvairvyāhṛtibhiścaiva sarpiṣāhomamācaret
saṃpātena śirodeśespṛse dbhiṃbasya deśikaḥ 43
prākkuṃḍe sarpiṣā caiva guḍena madhunā svayam
hutvātu viṣṇugāyatyrā śata maṣṭottaraṃ guruḥ 44
kalāvāhanaṃ
saṃpātena mukhaṃ spṛśya vedīdikkalaśasthitaiḥ
khadhirāśvadthapālāśa bilvaśākhābhi raṃbubhiḥ 45
siṃceyurmūrtipāssarve cābliṃgaiḥ pāvanairapi
vedikākoṇakuṃbhastha vaitasyā śākhayā guruḥ 46
viṣṇugāyatriyā siṃcedbiṃbāni nakalāni ca
(sajīvakaraṇaṃ kṛtvānaive)sajīvakaraṇaṃ kṛtvā nivedya cayadhāvidhi 47
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
mahārājopacārai śca pūjayeddeva mavyayaṃ
sahasranāmapūjāṃ ca kṛtvā stutvā muhurmuhuḥ
mūrti maṃtraṃ yadhāśakti japitvātu gurussvayaṃ
tapaśśaktyāsvakīyena guruhṛtpadma saṃsdhitaṃ
devaṃ biṃbe samārosya brahmaraṃdhreṇa vartmanā
praṇamya śirasā bhūmau naivedyāṃtaṃ samarpayet
arghyapādyobacārāṇi cāmaravyajanāḍikān
samarpya prārdhayeddeva mācāryo nirmalātmavān]
kaṃkaṇa baṃdhanaṃ brali pradānaṃ svapnārdhaśayanaṃ
badhnīyā ddakṣiṇehaste kautukaṃ mūlajerake
dvāreṣu ca baliṃ datvā svapnādhipatimaṃtrataḥ 48
aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvāpūrṇāhuti madhācaret
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
aṣṭadigbaṃdhanaṃ kṛtvā devamācchādyavāsanā
vedaghoṣai stūryaghoṣai stotrairnānāvidhaiptadhā
nṛttagītajayadhvānairbhagavannāmakīrtanaiḥ
mūrtimaṃtrajapaiścaiva rātriśeṣaṃ samāpayet]
(ācāryo yajamānādyaiḥ)ācārya yajamānādyāḥ svapnārdhaṃ darbhaviṣṭare 50
śāyīra nprāta ruddāya bhagavannāma uccaran
[adhikāṭhāṇi
grāmaṃ pradakṣiṇī kṛtya mahotsava samanvitam
ālayaṃ saṃpraviśyādha nityakarma samāpyaca]
svapnābhāvepidussvapne śāṃtiṃkuryādgurussvayaṃ 51
śubhaṃ bhavati cetkarma prārabheta (yadhāvidhi)vicakṣaṇaḥ
ityevaṃ saṃgraheṇaiva proktaṃ śayana lakṣaṇam 52
iti pāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
śayyādivāsavidhirnāma ekonaviṃśodhyāyaḥ
śrīśrīśrī
<20>
oṃ
śrīmatpāṃcarātradivyāgame śrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
viṃśodhyāyaḥ
pratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ
pratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ
tataḥprabhāte puṇyarkṣevāre nirṇītalagnake
dvāravāstu homaṃ gartasthāpanakramaḥ
(pratiṣṭhāṃ deva devasya prakurvī tadvijottamaḥ) devapratiṣṭhāṃ kurvīta yajamānenadeśikaḥ 1
tatpūrvevāstu homaṃ tu kuryāddvāranya dakṣiṇe
garbhamaṃdira madhyastha pīṭhagarteṣu śāstrataḥ 2
ratnanyāsaṃ prakurvīta prathamāvaraṇeṣṭasu
garteṣu bījānyaṣṭauca nikṣipe (ttadanaṃtare)ttadanaṃtaram 3
ratnāni ca tṛtīyetu dhātūṃ śca sdhāpayedbhudhaḥ
caturthecāṣṭa (lohāni)lohāṃ śca madhyagartetu vinyaset 4
pāradaṃ brahmarāgaṃ ca suvarṇaṃ śālibījakam
tadūrdhve strīśilāpīṭhaṃ sthāpayitvā (dvijottama) vicakṣaṇaḥ 5
sudhayāsu dṛḍhīkṛtya puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ
aṣṭadhānyāni
vrīhaya śca yavā ścaiva niṣpāvā śca priyaṃgavaḥ 6
nīvārāśśālayomāṣāḥ tilāścāṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ
aṣṭaratnāni
vaiḍūryaṃ mauktikaṃ caiva jalajaṃ vajramevaca 7
puṣyarāgaṃ caṃdrakāṃtaṃ sphaṭikaṃ caiṃdranīlakam
aṣṭadhātapaḥ
etānyaṣṭauca ratnāni haritālaṃ manaśsilā 8
saurāṣṭraṃ rocanaṃ śyāmaṃ sīsaṃ gairikamaṃjanam
dhātvaṣṭakamitiproktaṃ krameṇaiva nineśayet 9
nyāsakramaḥ
strīśilāpadma madhyetu praṇavanyāsamācaret
karṇikāyāṃ svarāṇyepa vyaṃjanāni daleṣu ca 10
vimalādīnite ṣvevanyāsaṃ kuryā dyadhāvidhi
tataśca piṃḍikāṃ gaṃdhaiḥpuṣpairabhyarcayetkramāt 11
piṃḍikāyāṃ śriyaṃ dhyātvā āhatenaivavāsasā
pracchādyacāstraṃ vinyasya dvāredvāre yathāvidhi 12
atnyadvāsa nādi
tatoguru ssamāgatya dhvajatoraṇa devatāḥ
visṛjyacāgniṃhṛdayetvārovya brāhmaṇaissaha 13
samuddhare nmahākuṃbhaṃ mūrti kuṃbhāṃ ntataḥparam
mūlādi sarvabiṃbāni mūrtipairvāhayarguruḥ 14
galaṃtikāṃ svayaṃ dhṛtvāto yadhārāpurassaram
(śaṃkhabheryadinā dvaica śubhaghoṣaiśca sarvabhiḥ) śaṃkhabheryādighoṣaiśca maṃgalai rvividhai rapi 15
dhvajachatrapatākādyaiḥ jayaghoṣaiśca cāmaraiḥ
vedapāṭhai stotrajālairharisaṃkīrtanādibhiḥ 16
pradakṣiṇakrameṇaiva (cālayaṃsaṃ praveśayet)praviśedgarbhamaṃdiram
dvāretudeva marghyādyairarcaye ddeśikassvayam 17
bhagavato sthāpana vidhiḥ
pīṭhapradakṣiṇenaiva devamaṃtaḥ praveśayet
muhūrte śobhane prāpte sthāpaye ddevamavyayam 18
dhṛvā dyauriti maṃtreṇa cātvāhāruṣamitṛcā
pratiṣṭhāsīti vaisāmnā piṃḍikānālamadhyataḥ 19
aṣṭabaṃdhena cā pūryabiṃba śaṃkuṃ niveśayet
pauruṣaṃ sūktamuccāryakarmārcāṃpuratonyaset 20
tatpuraścotsa vārcāṃca dakṣiṇe śayanaṃ balim
uttare snapanaṃ tīrthaṃ laukikā niṣṭabhūmiṣu 21
evaṃ saṃsthāpya deveśa mārādhana madhācaret
ārādhanavidhiḥ
mūlaberasya purato baddhvā padmāsanaṃ guruḥ 22
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃkṛtvādhyāyedbrahmaṃsanātanam
taṃ viṣṇuṃ hṛdaye padme mānasaṃ yāgamāsthitaḥ 23
abhyarcya caśriyā bhūmyā maṃtreṇa puruṣātmanā
aṃjalisthita pīṭhetu samāvāhyahariṃ tataḥ 24
tadviṣṇuṃ pratimāyāṃ tu vinyasetsu samāhitaḥ
nivṛttimaṃtramuccārya tasminbiṃbesthitaṃ smaret 25
praṇamya svāgataṃ datvāmutrāṃbaddhvāca darśayet
sṛṣṭisthitilayeṣvekaṃ nyāsaṃ kuryā dyathāvidhi 26
śriyādīnāṃ prokṣaṇaṃ agni pratiṣṭhā
śriyādīnāṃ ca (devīnāṃ)devānāṃ mahākuṃbha jalenavai
prokṣayetsva svamaṃtreṇa ṣaḍaṃganyasa(pūrvakaṃ)mācaret 27
sthāpaye dgārhapatyāgniṃ kuṃḍedhiśravaṇeṣu ca
deśiko yajamānena prahvastiṣṭhan kṛtāṃjaliḥ 28
devasya purataḥ sthitvāgādhāmenāmu dīrayet
stotrapāṭhaḥ
bhaktavatsala bhaktānā mabhipretārthasādhā 29
prārthayetvā mahaṃ deva madanugraha kāmyayā
sannidhatsvaciraṃ sthāne kalpiteśraddhayā mayā 30
prasīdadevadeveśa pūjāmapi gṛhaṇame
grāmasya rājño rāṣṭrasya prajānā miṃdirāvara 31
dehituṣṭiṃca puṣṭiṃca gatiṃ ca paramāṃ tadhā
itivijñāpya devāya yajamānaḥ prasannadhīḥ 32
śāśvata pūjārdhaṃ devanāmnā arcakasya bhūdāna pradāna kramaḥ
ācaṃdrārka mavicchinna pūjanārdhaṃ madhudviṣaḥ
kṣoṇīṃ sasya jalopetāṃ devanāmnā vilikhyaca 33
arcakasya kuṭuṃbasya jīvanārdhaṃ prakalpayet
tatorcakaṃ samāhūya kartādevasya sannidhau 34
kṛtāṃjali puṭo bhūtvā vijñāpana madhācaret
devasyārādhanārthaṃ ca tvatkuṭuṃbasya vṛttaye 35
mayādattā mimāṃ bhūmiṃgṛhāṇetibṛvan tataḥ
śilāyāṃ tāmrapatrevā likhitvādānaśāsanaṃ 36
arcakasya kare dadyāt sahiraṇyaphalodakam
yadivārcaka nāmnā vākṛtvākṣoṇīṃ pradāpayet 37
devasya nāmnā bhūdāna muttamaṃ phalacakṣate
arcakasya bhave nnāmnā madhyamaṃ phalamucyate 38
bhūdānameva kurvīta bhṛtakaṃ sakadācana
bhūdānena vināyastu bhṛtakaṃ pradadāticet 39
tasya vaṃśakṣayaṃ yāti tasyāyuśśrīśca naśyati
navai devalakānāmā sarvakarma bahīṣkṛtaḥ 40
tasmātsarva prayatnena bhūmīmeva pradāvayet
sthānācāryādika niyojanaṃ
bhagavatbādatīrthāṃbu sevanārthaṃ varettataḥ 41
sthānācāryaṃ ca tatthsāne hastakaṃ ca niyojayet
adhyāpakān nṛttagīta vādyakān paricārikān 42
sthānaśuddhi karānanyān pātraśuddhikārāṃ stathā
devapūjābhivṛddhyarthaṃ tāṃstāṃ śca niyamettataḥ 43
eteṣāṃ jīvanārthaṃ ca bhūmiṃ dadyādya dhāvasu
tān sevāsu niyujyādha tattannā mabhirevasacaḥ 44
paricārakavargaiśca yaḥpratiṣṭhāpaye dvibhum
sayāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ tatrāste bhagavāniva 45
yajamānasya vibhave sarvānetānpṛkalpayet
noce darcaka mekaṃ vā varaye dbhaktito dvijaḥ 46
saṃpūrṇaphala māpnoti yāvadābhuta saṃplavam
ācārya dakṣiṇādi
anveṣāṃ caiva bhaktānāṃ nṛttagītādi kurvatām 47
sarveṣāṃ ṛtvijāṃ caiva yatīnāṃ viduṣāṃ tathā
yathāvittānusāreṇa toṣaye cca pṛdhak pṛdhak 48
yajamānastato bhaktyā kalpite viṣṭhare tathā
pratiṣṭhāpaka māyāryaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃte samarcayet 49
sauvarṇakaṭīsūtraiśca keyūraiḥ kaṭakai stathā
maṇihāraiḥ kuṃḍalaiśca ratnajai raṃgulīyakaiḥ 50
kṣaumavastrottarīyaiśca jīvā jīva dhanaistadhā
toṣayitvā yadhānyāyaṃ devavatsaṃsmaranhṛdi 51
yānādike samāropya tūryaghoṣa purassaram
grāmapradakṣiṇenaiva svagṛhe saṃpraveśayet 52
yaevaṃ sthāpayeddevaṃ (pāṃcarātra vidhānataḥ) pāṃcarātrāgamoktataḥ
sayātiparamaṃ sthānaṃ (purāvṛttivarjitam) apunarbhavalakṣaṇam 53
punātivaṃśajān caiva puruṣānekaviṃśatim
iti saṃkṣepataḥ prokto pratiṣṭhākarma(padmaja) śobhanam 54
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
pratiṣṭhāvidhirnāma viśodhyāyaḥ
śrīśrīśrī
<21>
oṃ
śrīmatpāṃcarātradivyāgame śrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
ekaviṃśodhyāyaḥ
vimānādipratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ
śrībhagavānuvāca
vimānapratiṣṭhā
prāsādamaṃṭapādīnāṃ pratiṣṭhālakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu
prāsāda syāgrataḥ kuryā dvidhivadyāgamaṃṭapaṃ 1
caturdvāra samāyuktaṃ catustoraṇa bhūṣitam
prāsādasyāpi paritaḥ kuryātkuṃḍāṣṭakaṃ guruḥ 2
yāgapaṃṭapabhūbhāge kuṃḍamekaṃ prakalpayet
samasta mūrtihomaṃtu tasmi(kuryādvijottamaḥ)nne vaprakalpayet 3
rātrāvevāṃkurāropaṃ yathāvidhi samācaret
śailoddhaṃpūrvavatkuṃbhaṃkṛtvādhātumayaṃtuvā 4
tadvigolakamānena maṃtrabiṃbenavaisaha
adhivāsya yadhānyāyaṃ sarvopakaraṇānvitaṃ 5
mnānārthamakhilaṃ tābhyāmāpādyacayadhāvidhi
tasminratnādisaṃyukte biṃbaṃkuṃbheniveśyaca 6
puṣpairābharaṇairvasrai cchannaṃ kṛtvārghyapuṣpavat
śanaiḥ prāsādaśikhara māruhenmūrtipaissaha 7
ratnanyāsaṃ tataḥkṛtvā (śāstroktenaina mārgataḥ)pūrvoktena krameṇataḥ
niveśayettatobiṃbaṃ śikhākuṃbha samanvitam 8
saṃpūjyavānasācchādya sudhayādārthya mācaret
prāsādaṃ sthāpaye tpaścāt ṣaḍbhissiddhārthakādibhiḥ 9
vimāna paṭṭikāyāṃtu paṃcaviṃśati taṃtubhiḥ
badhvātu kautukaṃpaścāt kuryācchāyādhivāsanam 10
darpaṇe snapanaṃ kuryāt pūrva vaddeśikottama
caturdikṣu vimānasya mūrtayaḥ kalpitāyadi 11
netrāṇi taiṣāṃ conmīlya yathāśāstrakrameṇatu
vimānasyāpi kurvīta kūrca dvārādhivāsanam 12
prākkuṃḍesakalaṃhomamācāryassvayamācaret
pūrṇāhutiṃtataḥkṛtvācakraṃ vighnakṣayaṃvaram 13
pratiṣṭhāpya samabhyarca tatostraṃ samanaṃtaram
triyaṃśena śikhāduccaṃ śikhāmaṃtreṇa vinyaset 14
prāsāda biṃbayorvi prasthāpanaṃ yugapadbhavet
vimānasya pratiṣṭheddhaṃ samāsātkīrtitaṃ mayā 15
maṃṭa pratiṣṭhā
śraṇudhpaṃ maṃṭapādīnāṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ munipuṃgavāḥ
maṃṭapaṃ prathamaṃ cāgyraṃ tadhai vāsthānamaṃṭapam 16
prākāraṃ vacanāvāsaṃ kośāgārāṃbarāssade
pānīya puṣpaśālā ca tathāvai yāgamaṃṭapam 17
thānyāgārādi sarvāṃca kṣāghaye dbahubhirjalaiḥ
paryagnikaraṇaṃ kṛtvā mārbanollepanādikam 18
paṃcagavyaiśca saṃprokṣya (cālaṃ kṛtya viśeṣataḥ)vitānādyaiśca bhūṣayet
gaṃdhaiśca dhūpayitvātu dīpānāropayettataḥ 19
suthācūrṇairalaṃkṛtva vikiredbhu vicākṣatān
vācayitvātu puṇyāhaṃ vāstunādhaṃ tatorcayet 20
(dhyānyapīṭhaṃ prakalyādha mahākuṃbhaṃ niveśayet)prakalpyathānyapīṭhaṃ tu tatrakuṃbhaṃ niveśayet
devaṃ tatrasamabhyarca sāṃgaṃ saparivārakam 21
sthaṃḍilevāpi kuṃḍevā pratiṣṭhāpyānalaṃ tataḥ
[adhikāṭhāṇi
homaṃ kuryādyadhāśāstraṃ
pūrvoktenaiva vartmanā
maṃṭapānā madhipatiṃ taurkhyaṃ hutvā svamaṃtrataḥ
sahasramayutaṃ vāpi aṣṭottaraśataṃtuvā
samidhājyacarūn caiva pratyekaṃtu pṛdhak pṛthak]
devamagnautu saṃtarpya vūrṇāhuti mathācaret 22
sumuhūrtetu saṃprāpteprokṣa metkuṃbhatoyutaḥ
vāhana radha gopurādīnāṃ pratiṣṭhā dvāraśākhā kavāṭādi pratiṣṭhā
ityevaṃ maṃṭapādīnāṃ pratiṣṭhāvidhirīritaḥ 23
rathātigaupurādīnāṃ vāhanānāṃ samaṃ smṛtam
dvāredvāre prakalpyeta kavāṭadvaya mabjaja 24
prokṣaye ddvāraśākhāśca puṇyāhasya jalenatu
saṃprokṣyādbhiśca vāsobhi rācchādya sthaṃḍile tataḥ 25
kavāṭāśca dvāraśākhā sthāpyakuṃbheyaje ttataḥ
aṣṭau kuṃbhāṃ śca paritaḥ vāsudevādimaṃtrataḥ 26
kuṃbhasyottaradeśetu karakaṃ sthāpayedguruḥ
toraṇādīni saṃpūjya athivāsaṃ ca kārayet 27
homaṃ ca vidhivatkṛtvā saṃpātājyena secayet
sumuhūrte sulagne ca kavāṭādhasthitāśmani 28
gartetu ratna vinyāsaṃ puṇyāṃhaṃ vācaye ttataḥ
paṃcagavyena saṃprokṣya kavāṭausthāpaye dvijaḥ 29
kūrcena kuṃbhatoyena devānāvāhaye ttataḥ
adhobhāgetu śāṃtiṃca vāgdevīṃ dakṣiṇe tadhā 30
uttaretu ratiṃcaiva lakṣmīmūrdhvetu vinyaset
kavāṭe dakṣiṇebrahman viśvadṛgbhūtabhāvanau 31
kavāṭe vāmakesthāpyau viśvavaktraḥ pratāpavān
daśāvadāramūrtīṃca tathāvāhya yadhākramaṃ 32
vetreṣu vetaśāstrāṇi purāṇāni kramānnyaset
nārāceṣutrayatriṃśa ddevānāvāhayettataḥ 33
kīleṣuca ṛṣīnnyasya gaurīśaṃ paṭṭikātale
kuṃdapaṭṭe kavāṭasya visvabhāvana marcayet 34
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
kavāṭādhipatiṃ vāyu māvāhyābhyarchya yatnataḥ
avāta vāyumaṃtreṇa japaṃ kuryā yadhāvidhi]
kavāṭasthāpanāproktā cakrasya sthāpanaṃ śruṇu
sudarśana pratiṣṭhā
athavakṣye viśeṣeṇa cakrasthāpana muttamam 35
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
grāmāriṣṭa vināśārdhaṃ nahnicorāmayādibhiḥ
devākārasya rakṣārthaṃ deśakṣemārḍamenaca
suḍarśana pratiṣṭātu kartavyaṃ bhagavadgṛhī
gopurā graisanā grāmamadhye staṃbhāgrataḥkramāt]
lakṣaṇaṃ cakrarājasya saṃkṣepā (dvakṣyatetdhunā)ddvijasattamāḥ
sudarśana lakṣaṇaṃ
vistṛtaṃ daśahastena paṃcahastena vā punaḥ 36
karadvayena vā kuryā dyadvādvātriṃśadaṃgulam
caturviṃśāṃgulaṃ yadvā ṣoḍaśaṃ dvādaśaṃ tathā 37
mūlabiṃbānanasamaṃ sahasrārasamanvitam
yadvāśatāra madhavā caturviṃśatyarānvitam 38
dvādaśāra mathāṣṭāraṃ yadvā paṃcāślamevavā
arānāmāruguṇyena jvālābhiḥ pariveṣṭatam 39
(ṣaṭkoṇanirmitaṃ)trikoṇa nirmitaṃ cakraṃ nābhimaṃḍalamaṃḍitam
svarṇādyaiḥ kāraye dvidvānnānāratnaiścaśobhayet 40
tasyapratiṣṭhākramaḥ
śilpinaṃ dhanathānyādyaiḥ stoṣayitvā yadhāvasu
saṃgṛhya sarvasaṃbhārān yāgadravyāṇyaśeṣataḥ 41
vimānasyottarebhāge kuryānmaṃṭapa muttamam
ṣoḍaśastaṃbha saṃyuktaṃ madhyevedīṃ prakalpayet 42
vedyāśca paritodikṣu catuṣkuṃḍāni kārayet
yadvaitakuṃḍaṃ kurvīta ṣaṭkoṇaṃtu manoharam 43
taduttaradvāradeśe samīpe snānamaṃṭapam
snānamaṃṭapa raudretu aṃkurārpaṇa maṃṭapam 44
kṛtvācābhyaṃtaredeśe sudhācūrṇaiśca maṃḍayet
aṃkurānarpaye tpuṇye kāle pūrve yadhodite 45
mathitvāgniṃ pratiṣṭhāpya sarvakuṃḍeṣu mūrtipāḥ
vāstuhomādikaṃ kṛtvā yathāpūrvaṃtu kārayet 46
paryagnikaraṇaṃ sarva vastūnāṃ kuṃḍavahninā
paṃcagavyena saṃprokṣya puṇyāhasya jalenatu 47
kṣīredhivāsya taccakraṃ saptabhiḥkalaśaiḥ snapet
pūrvokte naivamārgeṇa jalavāsādikārayet 48
tatauparāhṇasamaye cakramuddhāpayejṅalāt
yāgaśālāṃ praviśyaiva snapanaṃkārayetkramāt 49
maṃtreṇonmīlya netretu śayyāyāṃ śāyayedguruḥ
thānyapīṭhagate kuṃbhe hetirājaṃ samāhvayet 50
nyāsaṃkṛtvāvithānena śāṃtihomaṃca kārayet
cakraṃ sajīvaṃ kurvīta pūrvokte yāgamaṃṭape 51
darbhāstaraṇasaṃyukte mūrtipaissahitaḥ ssvapet
prabhāte samanuprāptemūrtipai ssahitoguruḥ 52
vāstudevādi devāṃca saṃpūjyodvāsyadeśikaḥ
pūrṇāhutiṃtatohutvāvahniṃcakre samarpayet 53
muhūrtetu śubheprāpte yajamāno jiteṃdriyaḥ
ṛtvigācāryasahito vādyaghoṣa purassaram 54
dhāmapradakṣiṇenaiva coparisthānamāviśet
sthāpaye ccakrarājaṃtu ratnanyāsa purassaram 55
dhṛvaṃta iticoccārya caturdikṣubaliṃkṣipet
ācāryadakṣiṇāṃ dadyā dyajamāno yadhāpriyam 56
evaṃ yaḥkurute bhaktyā cakraṃ saudarśanaṃ mahat
ihabhuktvākhilān bhogān cāṃtesāyujyamāpnuyāt 57
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrī puruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
vimānagopuramaṃṭapādi pratiṣṭhāvidhirnāma ekaviṃśodhyāyaḥ

brahmauvāca

bhagavan śrotu micchāmi devadīkṣāṃ parāṃśubhām

śrībhagavānuvāca
bhagavatprati vacanaṃ dīkṣāvidhi praśastatā dīkṣāśabdārtha nirvacanaṃ

dīkṣākālaniyamaṃ

sṛkksṛvaukāraye daṣṭa maṃgalāni yadhākramam
kṣāmaiḥkārpāsakai (rvrastraiḥ)ścitraiścitrayenmaṃṭapa prapām 17
staṃbhāṃśca veṣṭayetkṣaumaiḥ rmuktādāmānilaṃbayet
staṃbheṣu dīpānāropya patākābhi rvibhūṣayet 18
śubhrairvitānairbadhnī yātpuṣpamālyaiśca śobhayet
evaṃ śālāmalaṃkṛtya ācāryādīna varettataḥ 19
ṛtvigvaraṇaṃ
ṛtvijaṣṣoḍaśabrahman yadvāṣṭau caturopivā
teca bhūṣya yadhāvittaṃ svarṇahārāṃbarādibhi 20
sugaṃdhadravyalepaiśca uṣṇīṣairūrthvapuṃḍrakaiḥ
alaṃkāryā yadhā vittaṃ yajamānena bhaktitaḥ 21
utsavāraṃbhaḥ
devasya snapanaṃ kuryā dutsacāraṃbhaṇāṃtayoḥ
utsavāraṃbhaṇā tbūrvaṃ dhvajārohaṇa mucyate 22
dhvajārohaṇa pūrvedyu raṃkurārpaṇa mācaret
kauśeyevā dhakārpāse (garuḍaṃ lekhaye tpaṭe)paṭe garuḍamālikhet 23
dhvajapaṭa lakṣaṇaṃ
daśahasta samāyāmaṃ vistāraṃ paṃcahastakam
tanmadhye navatālena garuḍaṃ kāṃcanaprabham 24
nīla nāsāgrasaṃyuktaṃ kuṃcitaṃ savyapādakam
pakṣavikṣepasaṃyuktaṃ śvetāṃbaradharaṃ vibhum 25
gagane gamanākāraṃ lalāṭe racitālakam
hārakeyūrasaṃyuktaṃ nūpurābharaṇojvalam 26
nāgeṃdrābharaṇaṃ devaṃ garuḍaṃ lekhya yatnataḥ
paṃcavarṇaiścitrayitvā śilpinānyena vā guruḥ, 27
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
chatracāmarakuṃbhādi maṃgalāni yadhākramaṃ
paṭasya pārśve patimān yadhāvithi samālikhet]
garuḍādhivāsaḥ
tatpaṭaṃ gṛhyadhāmādi prādakṣiṇyena deśikaḥ
praveśaye dyāgabhūmiṃ paṭaṃdhānyedhivāsayet 28
nayanonmīlanādīni pratiṣṭhāyāmi vā caret
darpaṇe snapanaṃkṛtvā śayyāyāṃ śoyaye dguruḥ 29
tatpaṭasya ca vāyavye dhānya taṃḍulamaṃḍale
vinyasya ca mahākuṃbhaṃ uttare karakaṃ nyaset 30
aṣṭadikṣyaṣṭakalaśān śāstralakṣaṇa saṃyutān
saṃsthāpya madhyame kuṃbhe garuḍaṃ vedarūpiṇam 31
āvāhyarakarake cakra (manaṃtādī vyadhākramaṃ, kalaśeṣuyajetsamyagyadhoktenaivavartmanā)miṃdrādī naṣṭadikṣuca
naivedyāṃtaṃtu saṃpūjya paṭasthaṃ garuḍaṃ tataḥ 32
ādhivāsa homaṃ
saṃśodhyaśoṣaṇādibhyaḥ kuṃḍāgniṃjvālayettataḥ
darbhamūlena paryagniṃ kṛtvā deśikasattamaḥ 33
prāṇapratiṣṭhāḥ
tatvānāṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhāra nyāsahomaucakārayet
kuṃbhasthaśaktiṃ kūrcena samādāya paṭenyaset 34
tataḥ prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ ca gāruḍanyāsa pūrvakam
kṛtāṃjalipuṭobhūtvā prārthaye dvinatāsutam 35
garuḍaprārdhanā
ehideva maṣābhāga vainateya mahābala
sānnidhyaṃ kurupakṣīṃdra prasīdātra namostute 36
āhūtaḥ karmaṇāsiddhiṃ kurutvaṃ vinatāsuta
āhūtavyāstrayo lokāḥ devasyotsavasaṃpadi 37
gāyatyrāni hageśasya pūjaye dupacārakaiḥ
prabhātegrāma pradakṣiṇaṃ
prabhāte tatpaṭaṃ nyasya śibikādau yadhodite 38
nartagāyakanāditra (vedavāditranisvanaiḥ)vedaghoṣa samanvitam
dhvajārohaṇavithiḥ
grāmapradakṣiṇenaiva devāgāraṃ praveśayet 39
dhvajasthaṃbhasamīporvīṃ gatvādhvajapaṭenatu
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
ālaṃkṛtyadhvajasthānaṃ staṃbhaṃ ca tu yadhocitaṃ
darbhamālādibhiḥ puṣpaiḥ pallavaiśca gurussvaya
puṇyāhaṃvācayitvātu tūryavāditranisvanaiḥ]
puṇyai rabliṃgakai rmaṃtrai stsaṃbhaṃ saṃprokṣyavāriṇā 40
viṣṇusūktaṃ samuccārya darbhasūtreṇa veṣṭayet
staṃbhāvaṭe ratna loha thātubījāni nikṣipet 41
staṃbhāgrepaṭamābadhya suvarṇosītimaṃtrataḥ
ratnanyāsakṛtegarte staṃbhamūlaṃ dṛḍhaṃ yathā 42
pratiṣṭhāpanamaṃtreṇa sthāpaye ddeśikottamaḥ
devasyābhimukhaṃ sthāpya paṭasthaṃ vinatāsutaṃ 43
caturdikṣu caturdevā nāvāhyavidhipūrvakam
garuḍaprārthanā
garuḍaṃ prārthaye ttatra gādhayāpakṣyamāṇayā 44
pakṣīṃdrapakṣavikṣepa taraṃgānila saṃpadā
nirastāsurasaṃnnāha samare śatrusūdana 45
sannidhatsyapaṭeyāva dutsavāvabṛthāṃtikam
evaṃ vijñāpyapakṣīśaṃ tadārabhya dinedine 46
kāladvaye cārcanīyaṃ yāvattīrtha dināvadhi
evaṃ kṛtvātu sāyāhne devatāhvānamācaret 47
bherīpūjāsthānaśudthiḥ
devasya dakṣiṇe pārśve upaviśyāsaneśubhe
caraṇaṃ pavitramaṃtreṇa (bhūmiṃ prakṣālyavāriṇ)bhuvaṃ prakṣālyavāriṇā 48
viṣṇorarāṭamaṃtreṇa darbhai ssaṃmārjaye tkramāt
gaṃdhadvāretimaṃtreṇa gomayena vilepayet 49
āpavuṃdaṃtu maṃtreṇa (paṃcacūrṇralaṃkṛtam)raṃgavallīḥ prakalpayet
akṣatān vikiredbhūmau devasya tveti maṃtrataḥ 50
dhānyama syetimaṃtreṇa dhānyapīṭhaṃ prakalpayet
śannodevīti maṃtreṇa padmapatrairalaṃkṛtiḥ 51
trātāraṃ maṃtramuccārya padmaṃmadhye samālikhet
dhanvanāgetimaṃtreṇa (darbhānāstīryadeśikaḥ)darbhairūrdhvaṃ paristaret 52
mūrthnāna mitimaṃtreṇa samaskuryādbhuvaṃ guruḥ
nyasetkoṇa catuṣṭeṣu ghṛtadīpa catuṣṭayam 53
viṣvakcenapūjā puṇyāhādi
viṣvaksenaṃ vrapūjyādha puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ
tajjalaiḥprokṣayedbherī māpavuṃdaṃtu maṃtrataḥ 54
agni rāyuṣma maṃtreṇa prokṣaye tsarvavādyakān
aryamāyeti tadbherīṃ vinyasedthānyaviṣṭhare 55
saṃsthāpyabherīṃ tanmathye devānāvāhaye tkramāt
bhryathi devatā vāhanaḥ
bhūteśaṃ ca mahābhūtaṃ viṣṇubhaktaṃ gadādharam 56
prajāpatiṃ sūryacaṃdrau śeṣaṃ bhūmātṛkāstataḥ
vācaspatiṃ saptaṛṣī nnaṃdīśaṃ ca samarcayet 57
mardalādyupavādyāni
mardalaṃ kāhalaṃ śaṃkhaṃ mṛdaṃgaṃ vallakīṃ tadhā
saṭahaṃ jhallarīṃ kāṃśya tāla miṃdrādiṣu kramāt 58
paritaḥsthāpya tadbāhye sthāpaye nnṛttagāyakān
paṭasthaṃ garuḍaṃ pūjya bherīmāveṣṭhyavānasā 59
kadviṣṇo ritimaṃtreṇa bherītāḍana mācaret
śrīsūktaṃtu samuccāryasarvavādyāni ghoṣayet 60
devatāhvānaṃ
kumudādigaṇānāṃ ca ṛṣīṇāṃ brahmaṇa stataḥ
rudrāṇāṃcaiva sarveṣā māhvānaṃ samyagācaret 61
tattaddevāditṛptyarthaṃ tattattālāni ghoṣayet
balipradānavithiḥ
tatastusotsavaṃ biṃbaṃ śrībhūmibhyāṃsamanvitam 62
śibikādau samāropya cālaṃkṛtya viśeṣataḥ
balibereṇa nahito bhrāmaye tkramayogataḥ 63
nṛttagītayutai rvādyai ssarvaiḥ parijanai ssaha
arghyaidgaṃdhaiśca mālyaiśca tāṃbūlairdhūpadīpakaiḥ 64
vimānadvāra mārabhya grāmavīdhyāṃta mevaca
pāyasena baliṃ dadyāddevatāhvānapūrvakam 65
grāmasya prāgbhāge kartavyabaliḥ
grāmasyadiśi pūrvasyāṃ prāṅmukhassaṃsthitoguruḥ
devatāvāhanaṃ kuryāsmaṃtreṇānena deśikaḥ 66
āgacchatāmaragaṇāḥ prāgāśāṃyedhīśerate
virūpāśca surūpāśca saparyāmudyatāmimām 67
gṛhītvāpāṃtu nastṛptāḥ kumudasyānuyāyivaḥ
mahaṃtvi mamupāyāṃtu prītāstebhyonamostuvaḥ 68
maṃtreṇānena kumudān sagaṇān pūjayetkramāt
gaṃdhādidīpaparyaṃtaṃ (pāyasena baliṃkṣipe) pāyasānnaṃ baliṃ kṣipet 69
tāṃbūlaṃ ca samarpyādha tattatsaṃgīta tālakaiḥ
saṃtoṣayettataḥ paścātprāpyāgneyāṃdiśaṃguruḥ 70
āgneya digbhāga baliḥ
āgacchatāmaragaṇā yegnyāśāmadhiśerate
bhīmabhīmamukhāraudrā saparyāmudyatāmimām 71
gṛhītvāṃpāṃtunastṛptāḥ kumudākṣānuyāyinaḥ
mahaṃtvi mamupāyāṃtu prītāstebhyonamonamaḥ 72
anenakumudākṣaṃtu toṣayitvātupūrvavat
dakṣiṇabhāge
āgacchatāṃ pitṛgaṇāḥ yāmyāśāṃyedhiśerate 73
dāruṇādāruṇācārā ssaparyā mudyatāmimām
gṛhītvāpāṃtunastṛptāḥ puḍarīkānuyāyinaḥ 74
mahaṃtvi mamupāyāṃtu prītāstebhyonamostuvaḥ
anenapuṃḍarīkākṣaṃ samāvāhya ca pūrvavat 75
nairutibhāge
āgacchatāṃ yātudhānāḥ koṇeśāṃyedhiśerate
kravyāśanaḥ krūradhiya ssavaryāmudyatāmimām 76
gṛhītvāpāṃtu nastṛptā vāmanasyānuyāyinaḥ
mahaṃtvi mamupāyāṃtu prītāstebhyonamostuvaḥ 77
ityevaṃ nirṛtiṃpūjya pratīcīṃdi śamāśritaḥ
paścimadigbhāge
āgacchatāhi prathamāḥ pratīcyāṃyedhiśerate 78
mahāviṣādaṃdaśūkā ssaparyāmudyatāmimām
gṛhītvāpāṃtunastṛptā śśaṃkurṇānu yāyinaḥ 79
mahaṃtvi mamupāyāṃtu prītāstebhyonamostuvaḥ
iti saṃpūjyavaruṇaṃ vāyadīṃti śamāśritaḥ 80
vāyavyabhāge
āyāṃtugaṃdharvagaṇāḥ vāyuvyāśāṃyedhiśerate
saṃdarśanābhīmavegā ssaparyāmudyatāmimām 81
gṛhītvāpāṃtuna stṛptā ssarpanetrāmayāyinaḥ
mahaṃtvi mamupāyāṃtu prītāntebhonamostuvaḥ 82
vāyumevaṃtusaṃpūjya kauberīṃ diśamāśritaḥ
uttaradigbhāge
āgacchatāṃ yakṣagaṇaḥ udīcīyedhiśerate 83
virūpā daṃḍahastāśca saparyāmudyatāmimām
gṛhītvāpāṃtu nastṛptā ssumukhasyānuyāyivaḥ 84
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
idthaṃ kubera mabhyarcya māheśvaradiśaṃgataḥ
āyāṃtu paiśācakagaṇāḥ raudrāśāṃyedhiśerate 85
saśūlāyudhahastāśca saparyāmudyatāmimāṃ
gṛhītvāpāṃtunastṛptā ssupratiṣṭhānuyāyinaḥ 86
mahaṃtvi mamupāyāṃtu prītāstebhyonamostuvaḥ]
mahaṃtvi mamupāyāṃtu prītāstebhyonamostuvaḥ 87
evaṃ saṃpūjyagrāmasya madhyaṃgatvādhadeśikaḥ
āgacchatāṃ siddhagaṇāḥ gaganaṃyedhi śerate 88
śucaya ssatyahṛdayā ssaparyāmudya tāmimām
gṛhītvāpāṃtunastṛptāḥ pṛsnigarbhānuyāyivaḥ 89
mahaṃtvimamupāyāṃtu pkītāstebhyonamostuvaḥ
evaṃ saṃpūjyagaganaṃ pṛdhivīṃ pūjayettataḥ 90
āgaccatādharagaṇāḥ pṛdhivīṃyedhiśerate
bahurūpā bahujñānā ssaparyāmudya tāmimām 91
gṛhītvāpāṃtu nastṛptā mānavasyānuyāyivaḥ
mahaṃtvi mamupāyāṃtu prītāstebhyonamostuvaḥ 92
āvāhyatoṣayedevaṃ tattaddevasyatṛptaye
tato nṛttaiśca tālaiśca toṣayitvātu deśikaḥ 93
bherītāḍana pūrvaṃtu (devatāhvāna mācaret)devatāvāhanaṃ caret
grāmamevaṃ paribhrāmya ālayaṃ saṃpraviśyaca 94
dhvajasthānaṃ samāsādya (garuḍaṃ pūjaye dguruḥ)saṃpūjyagaruḍaṃ tataḥ
tālādinṛttagītaiśca viṣvakcenaṃtu toṣayet 95
kṛtvaivaṃ devatāhvānaṃ mahākuṃbhaṃnamarcayet
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
dhvajārohaṇa devatāhvānavidhirnāma trayoviṃśodhyāyaḥ
śrīśrīśrī
<24>
oṃ
śrīmatpāṃcarātradivyāgame śrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
caturviṃśodhyāyaḥ
brahmotsavavidhiḥ
śrībhagavān
kuṃbhārcanāvidhiḥ
kuṃbhārcanā vidhānaṃtu vakṣyāmi śṛṇu padmaja
ṣoḍaśastaṃbhasaṃyukte alaṃkārai ralaṃkṛte 1
maṃṭape madhyamebhāge kuṃbhāvāhana mācaret
tadarthaṃ maṃḍalaṃ kuryā ccakrābjaṃ paṃcavarṇakaiḥ 2
cakrābjamaṃḍala racanākramaḥ
caturaṃ kṣetravistāraṃ śatadvayayutenaca
ṣaṭpaṃcāśatsamadhikaṃ koṣṭhānivibhajettataḥ 3
ṣaṭtriṃśatkoṣṭhamadhyetu paṃcavṛttāni kārayet
prathamaṃ karṇikākṣetraṃ dalasthānaṃ dvitīyakam 4
nābhisthānaṃ tṛtīyaṃtu arakṣetraṃ caturthakam
paṃcamaṃ nemi bhūmistu cāṣṭāviṃśatikoṇakaiḥ 5
bahiḥpīṭhaṃ prakalpyādha bahiḥpaṃkti dvayenatu
latāvidhīṃ prakurvīta (caturdvārāṇikāraye)caturdvāraṃ prakalpayet 6
pratyekaṃ ca catuḥkoṣṭhai śśaṃkhānāṃ śuddhayepunaḥ
koṇe koṇe dvikoṣṭhaṃtu sammārjavidhinā tataḥ 7
arthaśobhāḥ praśidhyarthaṃ trīṇi trīṇi ca mārjayet
catvāricopaśobhārthaṃ mārjanīyaṃ pṛdhak pṛdhak 8
varṇacanāvidhiḥ
śobhayenmaṃḍalaṃ tatra paṃcavarṇaissitādibhiḥ
ratnajairthātu jaiścūrṇairgaṃdhacūrṇai rathāpivā 9
śālipiṣṭhai śśitaiścitraiścitrayenmaṃḍalaṃ tataḥ
karṇikāṃ pītavarṇena śuklairbiṃdūnvibhūṣayet 10
karṇikāṃ pāṭalenaiva kṛṣṇairvā kesarāvanim
śyāmaiḥ pītaiśca raktaiśca nābhirekhātrayaṃ (kramāt)likhet 11
dalānisveta varṇaiśca raktavarṇaiga rāṇica
kṛṣṇairnemyaṃta bhūmiṃca pīṭhaṃ pītaiśca śobhayet 12
citrairlatā vitānaiśca vīthikāṃ śobhayettataḥ
raktaiśśobhāṃ tathāpītairuvaśobhāṃca kalpayet 13
śuklaṃ raktaṃ tadhāpītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ dvāra catuṣṭayam
maṃḍalaṃ citrayittveva (mārabhenmaṃḍalārcanam)mārabhetasamarcanam 14
maṃḍalārcanam
maṃtrādhvānaṃ karṇikāyāṃ varṇādhvānaṃ daleṣuca
tatvādhvānaṃ kesareṣu kalādhvāna mareṣuca 15
padādhvānaṃ nābhibhāge bhuvanādhvāna marcayet
nemibhāge karṇikāyāṃ samāvāhyākṣarāṇivai 16
paramātmāna māvāhya sakalīkṛtya pūjayet
daladvāvaśake śaktīṃ kesareṣu yajettataḥ 17
śriyādīṃ ścatataḥ pūjyodalāṃtapalayehariḥ
dvitīyavalaye brahmān trinetrastu tṛtīyake 18
viṣṇvādayaḥ pūjanīyā areṣu dvādaśasvapi
matsyādpīlaye pūjyanemyāmāyudhapūjanam 19
pīṭhyaca caturdikṣu varāho narakesarī
ānaṃtaścahayagrīvotaṃtheṃdrāgnyādayaḥkramāt 20
dvārapālāḥ pūjanīyāḥ chaṃḍādīśca tatoyajet
viṣvakyena madhaiśānye garuḍaṃ puratorcayet 21
dakṣiṇe hetirājaṃca gadāṃca dvāripaścime
anaṃtaradaleṣvevaṃ pūjayetkramayogataḥ 22
vāsudevādikṛṣṇāṃtaṃ dvādaśādityapūjanam
aṣṭākṣarāṇāṃ syasana maṣṭabiṃduṣu kārayet 23
caturviṃśati padmeṣu caturviṃśati mūrtayaḥ
iddhaṃdevān samānāhya(kuṃbhasthāpanamācaret) mahākuṃbhaṃtuvinyaset 24
kuṃbhasthāpanaṃ
sovakuṃbhaṃ sakarakaṃ madhye cakrābjamaṃḍale
kṛtvāmānasayāgaṃtu (kuṃbhamadhye yajetprabhum)bahiḥkuṃbhe yajetprabhām 25
mahāhavirni vedyādha homaṃ kuryā dyadhāvidhi
samidbhirājyaiścarubhi rmūlamaṃtreṇadeśikaḥ 26
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ vā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ tuvā
aṣṭottaraśatābhiśca gāyatrībhi ssamāvṛtam 27
vaikuṃṭhapārṣada homakramaḥ
vaikuṃṭhapārṣadaṃ homaṃ kuryāttaṃtravicakṣaṇaḥ
ādonārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ śrīphūnīlāsamanvitam 28
nityasūritrayaṃ paṃcacāyudhā npuruṣān tathā
śrīvatsaṃ kaustubhaṃ caiva vanamālāṃ kirīṭakam 29
ratna kuṃḍalayugmaṃca śrīvaikuṃṭhavimānakam
dhātāraṃca vidhātāraṃ bhuvaṃgaṃca pataṃgakam 30
maṇikaṃ tāpasaṃcaiva siddhaṃ sādhyamataḥparam
matsyaṃca haṃsarūpaṃca hayagrīvaṃca kūrmakam 31
varāhaṃ nārasiṃhaṃca vāmanaṃ bhṛgurāmakam
śrīrāma jānakīṃ caiva lakṣmaṇaṃ bharataṃ tathā 32
śatṛghnaṃca hanūmaṃtaṃ balarāmaṃ carevatīṃ
śrīkṛṣṇaṃ rukmiṇīṃ caiva satyabhāmāṃ tadhaivaca 33
buddhaṃ ca kalkirūpaṃca dvāralakṣmīṃ dhvajaṃ tathā
brahmāṇaṃ sṛṣṭikartāraṃ vāṇīṃca sanakāvayaḥ 34
nāradaṃca bhṛguṃcaiva mārkaṃḍeya mataḥparam
vedavyāsaṃ śukaṃcaiva agastyaṃ kāśyapaṃ tathā 35
aditiṃ caiva cātriṃca anasūyā(tadhaivaca) samanvitam
bhāradvājaṃ kauśikaṃca gautamaṃca vaśiṣṭhakam 36
aruṃdhatīṃ jāmadagniṃ reṇukāṃca tataḥparam
iṃdrādicāṣṭadikpālān gāyatrīdvitayenaca 37
pārvatīcaiva gaṃgāca dvayorgāyatyranaṃtaram
uṣāpadmini chāyābhi ssahitāditya pūrvakam 38
navagrahaśsodadhīṃ śca gāyatyrāṣṭottaraṃśatam
anena vidhinā homaṃ kṛtvā deśikasattamaḥ 39
caturaśre vāsudevaṃ cāpesaṃkarṣaṇaṃ tathā
paścimecaiva pradyumnamuttarecā niruddhakam 40
pāyasaiḥ kṛsarairgauḍairharidrānnaiśca maudgalaiḥ
pauruṣeṇaiva sūktena hotavyaṃ pṛdhagagniṣu 41
caturaśretu kuṃḍetu (brahmarudrāṣṭadikpatīḥ)brahmarudrādidevatāḥ
tattannāma caturdhyaṃtaṃ parivārapadenaca 42
svāhāṃtaṃ maṃtramuccāryahotavyaṃtuyathākramaṃ
brahmaprajāpatī rudrā ssarvedevā yathākramaṃ 43
chaṃdāsi vedāṛṣayaḥ gaṃdharvāśca salīsṛsāḥ
yakṣāścāpsaraścaiva māsādyai ssahavatsarāḥ 44
saritaśca samudrāśca parvatāśca tathāpagāḥ
bhūtānipaśavo vṛṅāḥtadhaivoṣadhayasmṛtāḥ 45
vanaspatiśco rbhijāśca svedajāścaṃ ḍajāstadā
jarāyajāśca bhūrādisaptalokāstadhaivaca 46
atalādyāstadhālokā ssaptachaṃḍādi devatāḥ
ityetebhyobalidravyaṃ juhuyanmūlavidyayā 47
pūrṇāhutiḥ
idaṃ viṣṇuriti prokṣyapūrṇāhuti madhācaret
evaṃ pratidinaṃ kuryātsāyaṃ prātarviśeṣataḥ 48
utsave bali pradāna vidhiḥ
tato balipradānaṃtu kuryādyāgasyamaṃṭape
bali biṃbaṃ ca cakraṃvā śibikādauni vesyaca 49
ālayācca viniṣkramyataddvāre kumudādikam
samabhyarcyabaliṃdadyāt śucau bhūmitale punaḥ 50
pradhamādidivasa devabali dravyabhedaḥ
nityotsava prakriyayā baliṃ kuryā dyadhāvidhi
tilajaṃ rajanīcūrṇaṃ karaṃbhān lājasaṃyutān 51
caruṇāsahasaṃyojya pradhamehni baliṃ kṣivet
dvitīyehanisaṃyojya (caruṃca tilataṃḍulaṃ) caruṇātilataṃḍulam 52
miśritāṃ ścāruṇānlāja thānyānāpūpakāṃstathā
tṛtīyeca caturtheca nārikelajalaissaha 53
saktūṃśca śālipiṣṭaṃca saṃyojyacaruṇāsaha
paṃcame padmabījāni pāyasaṃ śālitaṃḍulam 54
ṣaṣṭhtvapūpān saṃyojya caruṇāca baliṃ kṣipet
saptamehani sarvatra (balirannai ścaturvidhaiḥ)sārdhamannaiścaturvidhaiḥ 55
naktū pūpān saṃyojya sthāneṣvaṣṭa supūrvavat
aṣṭame navame caiva balidānaṃ samaṃ smṛtam 56
apūrvābharaṇai rmālyai rapūrvai ścāṃbarai ssaha
utsavaṃ pratyahaṃkuryādyava ttīrthadināṃtikam 57
grāmotsava vidhiḥ
grāmotsava vidhānaṃtu śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana
nityapūjāvasānetu viśeṣotsava mācaret 58
tadarthaṃ cautsavaṃ biṃbaṃ bhadrapīṭhe niveśyaca
nṛttagītaiśca vādyaiśca chatracāmaraketubhiḥ 59
svastisūktādikaṃ sarvaṃ paṭhadbhirbrāhmaṇaissaha
snapanavidhiḥ
dhāmapradakṣiṇaṃ (nītvā)kṛtvāprāpayetsnāna maṃṭapam 60
devasyatu purobhāge snapanārdhaṃtu maṃḍalam
tilataṃḍūlathānyaiśca kārayitvā (tu deśikaḥ)tataḥparam 61
kalaśānla kṣaṇopetān navavā paṃcavā kramāt
vinyasya pūrayetsarvān kalaśān gālitāṃbhasā 62
tattaddravyāṇinikṣipya tattaddevān samarcayet
vidhina tsnapanaṃkṛtvā śrīsūktena haridrayā 63
sahasrathāra yākuryātsasnapanaṃ ca nṛsūktataḥ
plotaśāṭyātu saṃmṛjya vastrābharaṇa mālyakaiḥ 64
bhagavaṃta malaṃkṛtya dhūpadīpān samarpya ca
bhakṣyabhojyādikaṃ sarvaṃ vinivedya (yadhāvithi)tataḥ param 65
tāṃbūlavīṭikāṃ datvā nīrajana mathācaret
niveditānnabhakṣyādīn bhaktebhya ssaṃpradāyaca 66
utsave vāhana kramaḥ
tato devaṃtu saṃsthāpya pūrvoktai bhadrapīṭhake
vāhanastu samīporvīṃ gamaye tsarvavādyakaiḥ 67
vibhavānukuṇaṃsthāpya maṃtranyāsaṃ yathāvidhi
kṛtvā devasya cārghyādyai rupacāraistu pūjayet 68
devasyavāhanārūḍhāḥ chatracāmarathāriṇaḥ
arcakādeva pārśvetu sthātavyaṃ bhaktitatbarāḥ 69
tato bhagavata ssamyaggrāmotsava mathācaret
nṛttavāditra gītaiśca vedaghoṣai rmahājanaiḥ 70
chatradhvajapatākābhirvitānai ścāmaraiśśubhaiḥ
vīṇāveṇuravaiścāpi śaṃkhaduṃdubhivisvanaiḥ 71
ākāśe saṃcara dbhiśca siddhagaṃdharvavigrahaiḥ
draṣṭūnāṃ prītijanakai rvinodai (rvividhai ssaha) rvibhavaissaha 72
evamādibhi ranyaiśca devasyotsava mācaret
tatkāle bhagavadbhaktā dadyuḥ puṣpaphalādikam 73
tatsarvaṃtu samādāya prokṣaye tpūjakassvayam
nivedya devadevāya puṣpādīn vikire dguruḥ 74
vāhanakramaḥ
pradhameha nidevasya haṃsavāhana miṣyate
(dvitīyehani siṃhaṃca)dvitīyadivase siṃhaṃ tṛtīye tu khageśvaraḥ 75
caturthehani śeṣaśca paṃcamecāṃjanīsutaḥ
ṣaṣṭetusūryacaṃdrauca rathārohaṃtu saptame 76
aṣṭametu turaṃgaṃ syānnavame gajamevaca
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
yālīpuṣpa kayānādi vāhanāni yadhcchayā]
evaṃ krameṇa devasya navāhotsavamīritam 77
(utsavāṃtetu deveśaṃpuna ssnapana pūrvakam)utsavānaṃtaraṃ devaṃ punassnapana mācaret
(yadhāvidhisamabhyarcya naivedyāṃtaṃ prapūjayet) bhagavaṃtaṃ samabhyarca apūpādīn nivedayet 78
śrībhūmibhyāṃtato devamālayenaṃ praveśayet
viśeṣotsava kramaḥ nanāhnirotsavaḥ
pradhamehani kalyāṇaṃ vanavihāra manaṃtare 79
plavotsavaṃ tṛtīyehni caturthe ḍolikotsavaṃ
paṃcame pūraṇaṃ proktaṃ jaladroṇītu ṣaṣṭhake 80
saptamedivaseprāpte rathārohaṇa mācaret
aṣṭame mṛgayātrāca navameva bṛdhaṃsmṛtam 81
ityevaṃ kadhitaṃ brahman navāhotsavapaddhatiḥ
brahmauvāca
kalyāṇotsava vithiḥ
bhagavan devadeveśa bhaktābhīṣṭaphalapradā 82
śravaṇe brahma praśnaḥ
śriyādīnāṃ vivāhaṃtu brūhitemagrahoyadi
vistareṇaiva tatsarvaṃ vadatāṃ vadatāṃvara 83
bhagavatprati vacanaṃ
śrībhagavānuvāca
kalyāṇotsava vidhiḥ
adhaśriyādi devīnāṃ pāṇigrahaṇa muttamam
kadhyate śruṇu tatsarvaṃ sāvadhānena cetasā 84
yasya śravaṇamātreṇa janmasāphalya māpnuyāt
kalyāṇotsava divasa nirṇayaḥ
yasyāvatāradivase tasya kalyāṇa mācaret 85
avatāreṣu sarveṣu anukai janmatārake
caikādaśyāṃ prakurvīta kanyāṇaṃ bhaktibhāvataḥ 86
jagatkālyāṇaśiddhyarthaṃ pratyabdaṃ kārayetsavam
kalyāṇotsava kramaḥ
viṣvaksenaṃ prapūjyādo puṇyāhanya jalenatu 87
saṃprokṣya sarvasaṃbhārān rakṣāsūtrāṇidhārayet
dhṛvādimūlabiṃbānāṃ śriyādīnāṃ tadhaivaca 88
ācāryāṇāṃca sarveṣāṃ ṛtvijāṃśca yadhāvidhi
rakṣāsūtrabaṃdhanāvasara nirūpaṇaṃ
utsavetu pravartetu sṛṣṭidoṣasya nāśane 89
āśauca parihārārthaṃ yogyatāpādarāyaca
svarṇekṛta sūtrāṇi dhārayeyuḥ pṛdhak pṛdhak 90
homādiḥ
agni pratiṣṭhāṃ kurvīta aśrevṛtteyadhātatham
saṃskārāṇi tataḥ kṛtvā cāghārātaṃhunettataḥ 91
agne rvaiṣṇavasidthyarthaṃ paṃcasūktairhunetkramāt
mūrtimaṃtreṇa juhuyā tṣoḍaśāhutayastathā 92
dvādaśākṣara maṃtreṇa cāṣṭāviṃśati saṃkhyayā
samidhājyavarūn hutvā homaśeṣaṃ samāpayet 93
kalyāṇamaṃṭapa praveśaḥ
tadrātrau vādivādevaṃ vivāhāgāra muttamaṃ

pāṇigrahaṇavidhiḥ
viṣvaksenaṃ prapūjyādha puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ
vijñāpya pāṇigrahaṇa karmadevāyabhaktitaḥ 95
pāṇigrahaṇotsava kāraṇa nirūpaṇaṃ
kadācinnaparityādyaṃ varṣe varṣetu niścitaṃ
nityotsavavihīnetu prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate 96
akṣatāropaṇaṃ nāmahyutsavaṃ prativārṣikaṃ
bhaktiśradthānvito devaṃ pāṇigrahaṇa mācaret 97
pāṇigrahaṇavidhyuktaṃ karmasarvaṃ samācaret
pradhānahomādi
kuṃḍasya caturaśrasya samīpe prāṅmukhaṃharim 98
devībhyāṃ sahadeveśaṃ niveśya susamāhitaḥ
idhmādhānādiṣuskārā nāghānāṃtaṃ yadhāvidhi 99
pradhānahomaṃ kurvīta praveśākhya mataparam
lājahomaṃ prakurvīta tāṃtrikaṃ homamevaca 100
saptāvaraṇa homā kṛtvāvaikuṃṭha pārṣadān
śrīsūktahomaṃkurvīta tidhivārādhidevatān 101
nakṣatrāṇāmadhipatīn ca jayādīṃścahunetkramāt
praṇītimārcanāṃtaṃtu kṛtvākarma yadhāvidhi 102
yānedevaṃ samāropya nṛttavādyapurassaraṃ
(dhāmapradakṣaṇaṃnītvā, ālayaṃtu samāviśet)grāmaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya devāgāraṃ praveśayet 103
itisamyaksamābhyātaṃ pāṇigrahaṇamutsavaṃ
vanavihārotsavavidhiḥ
tato vana vihārākhya mutsavaṃ tūcyatedhuvā 104
dvitīyadivase devaṃ nityapūjāpasānike
śriyāvasudhayāsārthaṃ vāhanetu niveśayet 105
bhadraṃ karṇetimaṃtreṇa savarṇaprabhayāsaha
puṣpamālyai ralaṃkṛtya pūrvattūryamaṃgalaiḥ 106
udyāna vanamāsādya tanmadhyasthitamaṃṭape
devībhyāṃ sahadedeśaṃ bhadrapīve niveśayet 107
snāpayedgālitāṃbhobhiḥ paṃcaviṃśatibhirghaṭaiḥ
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
kuṃḍevā sthaṃḍilevāpi homakṛtyā yadhoditaṃ]
alaṃkārāsanebharca vanyamū pharādikam 108
śītalaṃtarpaṇa jalaṃ mudgasūpaṃ guḍūnvitaṃ
tāṃbūlaṃ ca nivedyādha nīrājana madhācaret 109
stotraistutvātu deveśaṃ sāṣṭhāṃgaṃ praṇipatyaca
devasyapādayordadyāt puṣpāṃjali mataḥparam 110
devasya puratonṛttagīta vādyaviśāradaiḥ
vādyaṃ bahuvidhaṃ nāṭyaṃ darśaye yurmuhurmuhuḥ 111

evaṃ vanotsavaṃkṛtvā plavotsava mathācaret
plavotsavavithiḥ
tṛtīye divase prāpte devaṃ devī samanvitam 113
śībikāyāṃ samāropya cālaṃkṛtyavibhūṣaṇaiḥ
pradakṣiṇa krameṇaiva sarastīraṃ samānayet 114
tatsarovaramadhyastha maṃṭape sumanohare
siṃhānane samāropya naivedyāṃtaṃ supūjayet 115
divyaṃplavaṃ samādāya phalakābhiddṛḍhīkṛtaṃ
alaṃkṛtaṃ nitānādyaiḥ puṣpamālyopaśobhitaṃ 116
dhvajaiśca vividhākārairdarpaṇaiścāmaraistathā
nārikelasya pūgasya kadalyāḥ phalasaṃcayaiḥ 117
ghaṃṭābhiḥ kiṃkiṇībhiśca sarvata ssamalaṃkṛtaṃ
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvādha plavaṃsaṃprokṣyadeśikaḥ 118
tasmin khageśamāvāhya pūjayitvā yathāvidhi
naivedyaṃ bhaktitodatvā saṃprārthyavihageśvaraṃ 119
narastoyaṃtu saṃskṛtya dahanāpyāyanādibhiḥ
tasmindugdhābdhimāvāhya iṃdrādīnnparitoṣayet 120

ājyabhāgāṃtimaṃkṛtvā śeṣamaṃtreṇadeśikaḥ 121
aṣṭottarasahasraṃvā śatamaṣṭottaraṃtuvā
caruṃ puruṣasūktna juhuyātṣoḍaśāhutīḥ 122
saṃpātājyaṃ samādāya (secayetlpavamathyataḥ)secayitvāplavaṃ tataḥ
tatlpave devadeveśaṃ namyagāropaye dguruḥ 123
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
bhadraṃ karṇetimaṃtreṇa dhṛvādyauritimaṃtrataḥ]
ārcakādīkṣitāścaiva tadhaiva paricārikāḥ

ḍolotsavavithiḥ
caturtha divase kuryā ḍḍolotsava masuttamaṃ 128
ḍolāsaṃsthāpanārthāya nirmite maṃṭape guruḥ
suramyaṃ samalaṃkṛtya viṣvaksenaṃtu pūjayet 129

iṃdrādibhyo baliṃdatvā devaṃyāne niveśyaca

[adhikapāṭhāṇi
nṛttagītavinodāni kāraye ddevasannidhau]
tatradevaṃ samabhyarca citrānnāniphalodakaṃ

ḍolārajjūn gṛhītvātu cālayettu śanaiśśanaiḥ 133

yogapīṭhaṃ samabhyarca biṃbamuttiṣṭha maṃtrataḥ
deśika ssvayamādāya paṃjare (sanni veśayet)vini veśayet 139

tūryādivedaghoṣeṣupravṛtteṣuhariṃsmaran 140

sarvakratuphalaṃ prāpya viṣṇusāyujyatāṃ vrajet

sadyaḥkratuphalaṃ prāpya viṣṇusāyujyamāpnuyāt
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
pūraṇa dravyabhevena maṃtrabhedaḥ pravakṣyate
sahiratnā nītiratnai ssvarṇādīni hiraṇyataḥ
yāḥ phaletyādi ca phalaiḥ mṛtaṃ tanmaṃtra muccaran
madhuvā tetimadhunā pūraṇaṃ kurutāṃ guruḥ]
iti samyaksamākhyātaṃ pūraṇotsava muttamam 146

karpūraiḥ kuṃkumaiḥpuṣpai ssugaṃdha dravyasaṃcayaiḥ 148

tatodevaṃ samānīya pravṛtte vedapāṭhake
vādyaghoṣe pravṛttetu svastisūktaṃ japa nguruḥ 150
nimajjayettatodevaṃ devībhyāṃ sahadeśikaḥ

dvātriṃśa dupacāraistu pūjayitvā yathāvidhi 153
ālaye saṃpraviśyaiva kuryānni tyārcanaṃ vibhoḥ
evamuktaprakāreṇa ṣaṣṭhamyatsavaṃ caret 154
rathotsavavidhiḥ
rathotsavaṃ prakurvīta saptamehni yathākramam

muktādāmaiśca sauvarṇairmaṃjīrāṇāṃgaṇai ssaha

cakradaṃḍadvayemāyāṃ (manaṃtaṃca samarcayet) samarcayet 159
keśavādīn talebhyarcya dharmādīnpādabhāgake
sthaṃbheṣu cāyudhān pūjyasthūpikāyāṃsadāhariṃ 160
śālāsu vāsudevādīn kūrmādīnvedikānuva
anaṃtaṃ śikharecaiva padmanābhaṃ tadūrdhvake 161
chaṃḍādidvārapālāṃśca kumudādigaṇānyajet
dikpālāṃca samabhyarca aṣṭarikṣu yathākramam 162
koṇeṣu ca samabhyarcya puruṣaṃ satyamacyutam
anaṃtaṃ ca tataḥpūjya turageṣu khagādhipam 163
atalādīni lokāni cāṃtareṣu tato yajet
hetirājaṃ samabhyarcyaradhasyāṃta rbahisthare 164
viṣvaksenaṃtu saṃpūjya (rathasārathi vigrahe)sāradhyāṃ vigrahai guruḥ
balidānaṃ tataḥ kuryāt radhasya paritaḥ kramāt 165
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
tilalājākṣatayuta caruṇā bali mācaret]
tato devaṃ ca devībhyāṃ yānamārosyadeśikaḥ
ratha madhye bhagavanniveśana vidhiḥ
jayaghoṣairnṛttagīta bherīpaṭahanisvanaiḥ 166
śaṃkhatūryamṛdaṃgādyai rvīṇāveṇuravai ssaha
rathaṃ pradakṣīṇi kṛtya śākunaṃ sūkta muccaran 167
dhṛvaṃta itimaṃtreṇa devamāropaye ttataḥ
tatradevaṃ samabhyarcya naivedyāṃtaṃ yathāvidhi 168
rathasthabhagavannivedita prasāde strīṇāṃ saṃtāna prāptiḥ
naivedyānnaṃ tato dadyāt strīṇāṃ saṃtānasiddhaye
tadradhaṃ bhrāmayedgrāme sugaraṃ tu yadhābhavet 169
radhasthaṃ devadeveśaṃ dṛṣṭvāpāpaiḥ pramucyate
rathastha bhagavaddarśane pūjaneca viśeṣaḥ
lokoyaṃ niścayaṃ cai tat jñātavyaṃ kamalāsana 170
yastugatvāradhasthaṃmāṃ tṛptyate bhaktibhāvataḥ
dhanāśvanagaragrāma gajapuṣpaphalādikai 171
upāhārairanekaiśca bahumānapurassaram
sostubhuktvākhilānbhogānaṃte matsāmyamāpnu yāt 172
tato devaṃ rathādyāne sthāpayitvā yadhāpuram
(dhāmapradakṣiṇenaiva)pradakṣiṇa krameṇaiva cā taḥpraveśayet 173
evaṃ rathotsavaṃ prākta vadevasyaśārjiṇaḥ
mṛgayotsavavidhiḥ
adhavakṣye viśeṣeṇa mṛgayotsava muttamam 174
bhagavatprītijanakaṃ sarpāriṣṭa vināśakaṃ
mṛgayotsavayātrāyai cāṣṭame divasevibhum 175
yātropakaraṇai ssārtha malaṃkṛtyaviśeṣataḥ
aśvaratne samāropya nānālaṃkāraśobhite 176
mahatājana saṃghena gamaye dvipilaṃ mahat
tatrābhyarcyaviśeṣeṇa mṛgayāṃkārayettataḥ 177
mṛgabaṃdhana dhanuḥ pūjādi
senāṃvinyasyaparitaḥ madhyekrūramṛgasthitiṃ
tathākṛtvā bhaṭā statra mṛgānvathvāhareḥpuraḥ 178
darśayeyurmṛgāṃstān tān mocayeyuḥkramādbahiḥ
hatānāṃ krūrajaṃtūnāṃ bhavetpuṇya manaṃtakaṃ 179
devasya tu purobhāge dhanurbaṇau prapūjayet
caturdikṣu caturbāṇā nūrdhvecaikaṃ visarjayet 180
tato devaṃ tu saṃpūjya yathāvibhavavistaram
grāmapradakṣiṇenaina cālayaṃ saṃpraveśayet 181
avabṛdhotsavavidhiḥ
tasyāmapararātrautu tīrthabiṃbādhivāsanam
biṃbasya dakṣiṇakare kautukaṃ baṃdhaye dguruḥ 182
thānyarāśsyautu vinyasya yathāvidhi samarcayet
niśācūrṇa ghaṭṭanādayaḥ
devasya tu purobhāge dhānyarāśāvulūkhalaṃ 183
sthāpayitvā tatolakṣmīṃthyāye cchrīsūktavartmanā
kṣālitaṃ mūlamaṃtreṇa maṃtritaṃ munalaṃguruḥ 184
mūrtipaiśca samādāya niśācūrṇaṃtu ghaṭṭayet
caturbhi rvaiṣṇavai ścāpi vaiṣṇavībhistuvāyathā 185
ghaṭṭayecca niśācūrṇaṃ tathāsūkṣmataraṃ bhavet
kuṃbhe nikṣipya taccūrṇaṃ veṣṭaye nnavavāsasā 186
tatkuṃbhaṃ taṃḍulepīṭhe sthāpaye nmūlavidyayā
gaṃdhairabhyarcya taccūrṇai śśrīsūktenābhiṣecaye 187
vāhanedeva māropya tīrthabiṃbapurassaram
nadyādipuṇatīrthaṃvā narastīra mathāpivā 188
paurṇamāsyāṃ, darśeca kartavya viśeṣaḥ (samudrasnānaṃ)
paurṇamāsyāṃ ca darśeca sthitaṃ cedyo janāṃtaraṃ
samudratīra madhavā nītvā tatrastha maṃṭape 189
prapāyāṃ bhadrapīṭhevā prāṃṅmukhovāpyudaṅmukhaḥ
vāhanādya palopyādha devaṃ tatra niveśayet 190
agrataḥ kalaśān paṃcaviṃśati rdaśasaptavā
avabhṛdhasnapanavithiḥ
navavā paṃcavā (sthāpya tairdevamabhiṣecaye)nyasya kalaśairabhiṣecaye 191
cakrasnānavithiḥ
tīrtheṣu teṣu gaṃgādyāścāvāhyavidhivattataḥ
cakrabiṃbaṃ samādāya deśikeṃdrassamūhitaḥ 192
tīrthenimajjayettatra trivāraṃtu yathāvidhi
abliṃga pavamānādi sūktoccāraṇapūrvakam 193
cakreṇa sahāsnānāṃ phala viśeṣa kadhanaṃ
tātatkāle sahadevena nimajjaṃti ca ye sarāḥ
te nirdhūyā śubhaṃ sarvaṃ mahāpātaka saṃcayaṃ 194
dhṛvaṃyāṃti maduṣprāpaṃ sthānamācaṃdratārakaṃ
tato devaṃ samāniya maṃṭape viniveśyaca 195
arghyādyaiḥ pūjayettatra pauruṣeṇaivasūktataḥ
nivedyānnādikaṃ tatra bhojaye dbrāhmaṇān tataḥ 196
yānedevaṃ samāropya prāpaye dālayāṃtaram
tadrātrau pūjaye ddevaṃ puṣpayāśākhyamaṃḍale 197
mahotsave acāryosvecchayā utvavādikaṃkāryaṃ
mahotsave pravartetu deśikeṃdreṣṭavartmanā
ūhāpohavithānena kartavyaṃ sunoharam 198
navāhotsavamadhyepi deśikastu yadhocitaṃ
mahadāśīrvāda, āsthānādyanuktotsava viṣayaḥ
mahādāśirvacākhyaṃca āsthānotsavanāmakaḥ 199
puṣpadāmavilāsaṃca yathāvibhapavistaram
kavāṭa baṃdhanaṃ devadevī saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ tuvā 200
anyānyaneka citrāṇi vinodāni ca kalpayet
iti śrīpāṃcarātremahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
brahmotsava vidhirnāma caturviṃśodhyāyaḥ
śrīśrīśrī

śrīmatpāṃcarātra divyāgame śrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
paṃcaviṃśodhyāyaḥ
puṣpayāgavidhiḥ
puṣpayāgavidhi śravaṇārthaṃ brahma praśnaḥ
brahma
deva deva jagannādha sṛṣṭisthityaṃtakāraka
(bhaktābhīṣṭha prasiddhyartha mutsavakramamīritaṃ
tadutsave pravartetu cā spṛśyasparśanesati
taddhoṣa parihārārdhaṃ kartavyaṃ kiṃ jagatprabho
tatsarpaṃ vistareṇaiva vadatvaṃ vadatāṃ vara)
bhaktābhīṣṭa pradānārthaṃ hyutsavoktaṃtvayāpurā 1
tadutsave paryaṭane cāspṛnyasparśane pivā
yatkiṃciddhoṣaśāṃtyarthaṃ saṃbhavece nmahotsave 2
tattaddoṣa praśāṃtyarthaṃ tasya niṣkṛti siddhaye
upāyaḥ kiṃ devadeva tadvadasya yagatprabho 3
bhagavatprativacanaṃ
śrībhagavān
śrīpuṣpayāgavidhiḥ
śṛṇu vatcapravakṣyāmi puṣpayāgākhya mutsavam
kartavyaṃ mamasaṃpītyai (sarvadoṣāpahārakaṃ) sarvadoṣān dahetsunaḥ 4
tasmātsamāhito bhūtvā samāptadivaseniśi
tatsaredyurniśivādha puṣpayāgaṃ samācaret 5
āsthāna maṃṭape vāpi yāgamaṃṭapamadhyataḥ
talochredhāṃ dvitālena vistārāyāma saṃmitāṃ 6
mṛdāvedīṃ prakalpyādha tasyāṃ devaṃ niveśayet
tatpurobhūtalecaiva dvihastāyāmavistṛtām 7
tālamānonnatāṃ vedīṃ darpaṇodaravatsamām
taddakṣiṇe paṃcavarṇa cūrṇāni kusumānica 8
puṣpayāga maṃḍala racanā kramaḥ
prokṣya nikṣipya tadvedyāṃ maṃḍalaṃ racayedguruḥ
pradhamaṃ śyāmacūrṇena karṇikāṃ kārayettataḥ 9
tadūrthve pītacūrṇena pṛttaṃ kṛtvā tadhāparam
iṃdrādidigvibhāgena caturdikṣu caturdalam 10
svetaṃ raktaṃ tathāpītaṃ śyāmaṃcūrṇaistuphūrayet
dalāṃta rekhātanmadhye svetapītadvayaṃ tataḥ 11
tadūrthve śvetavarṇena cūrṇena bhramaṇaṃ caret
tatoṣṭadalamālikhya dikṣu varṇaṃtu pūrvavat 12
īśānyaṃ diśipītaṃ ca śyāmaṃ śvetaṃ cakartakam
dalāṃta rekhātanmadhye raktadhūmraprapūraṇam 13
rakta cūrṇena saṃveṣṭhya tadūrthveṣṭadalaṃ likhet
vītavarṇena saṃpūrya dalāṃte kṛṣṇavarṇakam 14
dalamadhye śvetavarṇa cūrṇena pravibhājayet
kṛtvānemiṃ dhūmravarṇacūrṇena paritolikhet 15
tadūrthve dvādaśadalaṃ (paṃcavarṇavimiśritaiḥ)paṃcavarṇairvimiśritaiḥ
saṃpūrya dalarekhāṃṇe svetarakta dalānvitaiḥ 16
miśritenaiva cūrṇena pītaṃ syāddalamadhyake
paritaśyāmacūrṇeva vṛttaṃ kṛtvā manoharam 17
tatsamaṃtā tsvetarakta kṛṣṇacūrṇatrayaṃ nyaset
dvāraiścaturbhissaṃyojya devānāvāhayetkramāt 18
maṃḍale devatāvāhanakramaḥ
pradhamaṃ karṇikāmadhye devadevaṃ sanātanam
(śyāmakomalaśārīraṃ)mayūrakaṃṭhavacchyāmaṃ pītakauśeyavānasam 19
śrīvatsavanamālādyai (śśaṃkhacakrādibhiryutaṃ)ścakrādaiśca samanvitam
dvādaśākṣara maṃtreṇa pratipādyaṃ (jagatprabhuṃ) manoharam 20
parātparaṃ vāsudeva mabhiṣṭaphaladaṃ yajet
tato dala catuṣketu ceṃdrādikramayogataḥ 21
śuddhaspaṭika saṃkāśaṃ vāsudevaṃ samarcayet
jājvalyamānavapuṣe raktavarṇaṃ mahaujanam 22
saṃkarṣaṇaṃ dvitīyetu tṛtīyekanakaprabham
jñānādiguṇasāmānyaṃ pradyumnaṃ pītavarṇakam 23
vyūhāṃtarvartinaṃ śyāmavarṇaṃ devaṃ mahāprabham
aniruddhaṃ samabhyarcya evaṃdalacatuṣṭaye 24
aṣṭākṣaraṃ tvaṣṭadale tārakārṇaṃ tu ṣaḍdale
tadūrthvaṃ dvādaśadale dvādaśārṇaṃtu saṃlikhet 25
karṇikāvṛttamārabhya vṛttarekhāsu paṃcasu
pṛdhivyādīni bhūtāni (paṃcacāvāhyapūjayet) samānāhyatatorcayet 26
taddalāgrastharekhāsu caturāvaraṇeṣvapi
sātvikīṃ rājasīṃ caiva tāmasīṃ triguṇātmikān 27
taddalāṃteṣu bhāgeṣu ṛgādīṃ śca yathākramam
tadūrthvā varaṇecaiva brahmadīnarca yetkramāt 28
brahmaviṣṇu maheśānāṃ raktapāṃḍu ramecakaiḥ
varṇairguṇānusāreṇa cāvāhyāvaraṇatraye 29
pradhamāvaraṇadvārā catkuṣke pālikā nyaset
dvitīyāvaraṇadvāra catkuṣīcāṣṭamaṃgalān 30
tṛtīyāvaraṇadvāra catkuṣ kumudāstadhā
āvahyagaṃdhapuṣpādyai rarcayecca pṛdhak pṛdhak 31
maṃḍalāṃtastha devānāṃ tattadvarṇīma rūpataḥ
sugaṃdhakusumai ścaiva pūjayecchāstravartmanā 32
tanmaṃḍalecotsavārcāṃ (sthāpyābhyarcyayadhāvithi) dhyātvābhyarcayadhākramaṃ
puṣpayāgaidvādaśārādhanavidhiḥ
śrīvaiṣṇava mahāyāganāmānaṃ yāgamuttamam 33
upacāraiṣṣoḍaśabhi rdvādaśāvartisaṃmitaiḥ
yajeccaivaṃ niśāyāṃ tu vedavādyapurassaram 34
pāyasādīni cānnāni phalānāmrādikānica
bhakṣyāni ghṛtapakvāni nivedyabahuvistaram 35
pānīyācamanaṃ datvā karpūrayutamuttamam
tāṃbūlaṃbhaktitodadyāt pādaupuṣpāṃjaliṃkṣipet 36
devaṃ yāne samāropya devyauśayanamaṃṭape
sarvālaṃkāra saṃyuktemuktājālavirājite 37
maṇikāṃcana dīpaiśca sarvatra samalaṃkṛte
kastūryagurudhūpaiśca dhūpitesu marohare 38
śayyāsanopakaraṇai ssarvairvastubhi ranvite
puṣpaśayyāsamāyukte suvitāneṣu toraṇe 39
saṃsthāpyābhyarcya yatnena devaṃ yānena deśikaḥ
saptapradakṣiṇāni
devāgāraṃ parikramya saptadhātvaritenaca 40
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
yadvā dvādaśavāraṃ vā nītvā dhāmadakṣiṇaṃ
yekaika vādyaghoṣeṇa prativāraṃ pradakṣiṇeḥ]
yānāddevaṃ maṃḍapasya madhyabhāge manohare
lakṣmīsaṃvādotsavaḥ
bhadrapīṭhe niveśyādha devyorabhimukhaṃ yadhā 41
lakṣmīsaṃvādanāmāna mutsavaṃ (samyagācaret) parikalpayet
kavāṭabaṃthasahita mutsavaṃ naṭanaṃ caret 42
lakṣmī kaṭākṣairatyaṃta manugṛhṇaṃtamacyutam
tataḥpraveśa yeddevaṃ devyormadhye jagatprabhum 43
darpaṇadarśanaṃ
saṃsthāpya darpaṇecaika kāledevaṃ ramāsakham
devībhyāṃ sahapaśyaṃti janāstvatyaṃta harṣitāḥ 44
teṣāṃ pāpāni naśyaṃti siddhyaṃte sarvasaṃpadaḥ
poyasānnaṃ nivedyādha kṣīraṃ śarkarayāmutam 45
karbūreṇa lavaṃgena yuktaṃ tāṃbūla muttamam
nīrājanaṃ samarpyādha puṣpāṃjani samarpaṇam 46
praṇamyadaṃḍavadbhūmau bhaktiyuktena cetasā
śayyopacāravihitā nupacārān samācaret 47
pālikāṃkuraiddevasyārādhanavidhiḥ
pālikāpātranaṃsthāgane cāṃkurāṇi samācaret
samarpya devadevasya pāvayoḥ prītipūrvakam 48
homavidhiḥ
tatogniṃca pratiṣṭhāpya samidhājyacarūnhunet
aṣṭhottaraśataṃ caiva mūlamaṃtreṇa deśikaḥ 49
prāyaścittāhutīnhutvā paṃcopaniṣadhāghṛtam
pūrṇāhuti devato dvāsanādi
pūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ kuryā (dagnimudvāsyadeśikaḥ)dagnīṃcodyāsyadeśikaḥ 50
toraṇadhvaja devādī nudvāsyaca yadhākramam
garuḍaṃ tu tatobhyarcyakṛtvācāpi pradakṣiṇam 51
paṭādudvā sayettṣāryaṃ saṃhārakramayogataḥ
dhvajastaṃbha dhvaja paṭādikaṃ ācāryasyadadyāt
dhvajastaṃbhaṃ ca gṛhṇīyāttadrajjuṃ tatprapāṃ guruḥ 52
deśikagrahaṇenaiva garuḍaḥ paritṛptyate
devānāvāhitān narvān baliṃ datvāvisarjayet 53
devaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya yānedevaṃ niveśyaca
bhaktān deśāṃtarātprāptānviṣṇusevārthamāgatān 54
svadeśān gaṃtu mājñāpya deśikasya mukhenatu
pradakṣiṇaṃ parikramya sarvāvaraṇabhūmiṣu 55
sarvamaṃgalavādyaiśca devamaṃtaḥ praveśayet
ācāryadakṣiṇādi
mahotsavāṃte tatkartā deśikaṃ pāṃcarātrikam 56
vastraiśca svarmabhūṣādyairgaṃdhapuṣpādibhissaha
saṃpūjyadakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt śataniṣkāṃ yadhāvasu 57
ṛtvijāṃ śca tadarthaṃ syāt tadarthaṃ paricāriṇām
nṛttavādya janānsarvān yathāvittānusārataḥ 58
saṃbhāvya viduṣāṃ cāpi brāhmaṇān dakṣiṇādibhiḥ
dīnāṃdha kṛpaṇebhyaśca vastrānnādyaistutoṣayet 59
dakṣiṇadānāvasara nirūpaṇaṃ
kriyāśarīramityuktaṃ dakṣiṇājīvamucyate
kriyādakṣiṇayāhīnā sajīvati viniṣphalam 60
tasmātsvalpāhidātavyā dakṣiṇāyāgakarmaṇi
(dakṣiṇāhīnayāgena kartāśreyāna viṃdati) yāgasya dakṣiṇāhīvo kartṛdoṣāyakalpate 61
tasmādvittānusāreṇa deyātsvalpāpidakṣiṇā
ācāryaṃ gaṃdhapuṣpādvai ryajamānoti bhaktitaḥ 62
abhyarcayānamāropya kṛtvāgrāma pradakṣiṇam
utsavakaraṇe phala śṛtiḥ
gṛhaṃ praveśayedbhaktyā sarvavādyapurassaram 63
evamuktaprakāreṇa yaḥkaroti mahotsavam
(pacābhīṣṭhānprāpyaviṣṇoḥ) uttārayan svayaṃviṣṇossāyujyaṃgacchatidhṛvam 64
yāgaḥ pravartate viṣṇoḥ grāmevā paṭṭaṇesivā
deśe rāṣrepivā tatra nāstidurbhikṣa saṃbhavaḥ 65
[adhikapāṭhāni
yatrapravartate viṣṇoḥ kalyāṇaṃ deśikottama
tatracānyānyutsavāni samakāletu nācaret
pravartite daivayāge cānyaṃ mānavayāgakaṃ
yaḥkaroti samūḍhātmā tvacireṇaivanaśyati
viṣṇorutsavamadhyetu daivatāṃtaramutsavaṃ
bhavaccettasya rāṣṭrasya rājñonāśo bhavedhṛvaṃ;]
sarvaduḥkhavinirmuktābhavaṃti sukhinonarāḥ
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
tasmātsarvaprayatnena hareḥ kalyāṇamadhyataḥ
nakadāpi ca kartavya manyamutsava mādarāt]
ityevaṃ puṣpayāgākhya mutsavaṃ kamalāsana 66
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyāṃ
puṣpayāgavidhirnāma paṃcaviṃśodhyāyaḥ

pavitrotsavavidhiḥ
