brahma
bhagavanpuṃḍarīkākṣa pavitrāropaṇotsavam
kadākāryaṃ pavitrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ pavamenagha 1
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
kimardhamutsavaṃ proktaṃ vidhānaṃ kiṃ jagadvibho
tatparvaṃ vistareṇaiva brūhimepuruṣarṣabha]
bhagavatprativacanaṃ
śrībhagavān
pavitrotsavakāla nirṇayaḥ
śṛṇuṣva paramaṃ guhyaṃ paditrārovaṇotsavam
śrāvaṇevādha kārtikyāṃ māsecāśvayuje tadhā 2
māsibhādrapatevāpi pavitrāropaṇaṃ smṛtaṃ
dvādaśyāṃvādaśamyāṃvā paurṇamāsyāṃyathāvidhiḥ 3
dvitīyāyāṃtrayodaśyāṃpaṃcamyāṃvāśubhetidhau
śuklapakṣeśubhelagne sunakṣatresuśobhane 4
savitrāropaṇaṃ kāryaṃ maṃgalāṃkura pūrvakam
pavitrotsavakramaḥ
pālikā ghaṭikāścaiva śarāvaṃ tu tribhedataḥ 5
pratyekaṃ dvādaśaṃ grāhyaṃ maṃgalāṃkurakarmaṇi
aṃkurāropaṇaṃ kṛtvā yadhāvithi tataḥparam 6
pavitra maṃṭapa lakṣaṇaṃ
maṃṭapaṃ kārayetpūrvaṃ prāsādasyā grataśśubhaṃ
trayodaśakaraṃ samyak navahastāca vedikā 7
ekahastocchrayaṃ madhye kṛtvālaṃkārabhūṣitaṃ
dvāratoraṇa saṃyuktaṃ patākadhvajaśobhitaṃ 8
vitānādibhirākīrṇaṃ darbaṇādibhiranvitaṃ
ārdraśākhāsamāyuktaṃ phalaguccai ssamanvitaṃ 9
kramukādiphalaiścaiva nārikelaphalaissaha
nāraṃgalikucai ścaiva śobhaye nmaṃṭapaṃ tataḥ 10
navamyāmeva saṃgṛhya yāgadravyāṇi (deśikaḥ) sarvaśaḥ
pavitra nirmāṇa vithiḥ
pradhamaṃ garṇasūtraṃ tu paṭṭasūtramataḥparaṃ 11
sūtrabhedāni
kṣaumasūtraṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu padmasūtraṃ caturthakaṃ
kārpāsaṃ sūtramadhavā kanyābhiḥkṛtasūtrakaṃ 12
malinaṃ nīlasūtraṃ ca prayatnena visarjayet
brāhmaṇībhiḥ kṛtaṃ sūtraṃ triguṇīkṛtya taṃtubhiḥ 13
(pavitrāṇi prakurvīta)pavitraṃ tena kurvīta kaniṣṭhottama madhyamaṃ
pavitrabhedaḥ
vanamālāpavitraṃ tu pavitraṃ cādhivānakaṃ 14
parivārapavitrāṇi pavitraṃ dīkṣitasyatu
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
evamādi vibhedaistu pavitrāṇi cakārayet]
uttamādibhedāni
mūrdhādinābhiparyaṃtaṃ pavitraṃtu kaniṣṭhakaṃ 15
urumātraṃ tadārabhya pavitraṃ madhyamaṃ smṛtaṃ
uttamaṃjānumātraṃtu pavitrāṇāṃ pramāṇakaṃ 16
vanamālādi pavitra pramāṇāni
vanamālāpavitraṃtu pratimāyāma saṃmitaṃ
kaniṣṭhika pavitraṃ tu pavitramadhivāsakaṃ 17
pavitraṃ dīkṣitasyaiva nābhimātraṃ pracakṣate
upalipteśu caudeśe darbhānāstīrya bhūtale 18
āsane prāṅmukho vidvān śiṣyaissarvaissahātmavān
gāyatrīṃ ca japedādau sūtraśaṃkūnparīkṣayet 19
devānurūpakaṃ kuryāt pavitraṃ tu pramāṇataḥ
sūtrasaṃkhyā nirṇayavidhiḥ
kaniṣṭhaṃtaṃ tu vijñeya maṣṭāviṃśati taṃtubhiḥ 20
catuḥpancāśatujñeyaṃ taṃtūnāṃ madhyamaṃ paraṃ
uttamaṃ caiva taṃtūnāṃ aṣṭottaraśataṃ bhavet 21
aṣṭottara sahasraṃtu taṃtūnāṃ parisaṃkhyayā
vanamālākhyayā viṣṇordattāsā muktidāyinī 22
sapta viṃśatikaṃ jñeyaṃ taṃtūnā madhivāsake
vanamālā viśeṣeṇa cottamādhama madhyamaṃ 23
tadarthaṃ sārthamevaṃca taṃtūnāṃ parisaṃkhyayā
taṃtūnāṃ dvādaśaṃ jñeyaṃ parivārapavitrakaṃ 24
saptaviṃśati taṃtūnā mācārasya pavitrakaṃ
picaviṃśati taṃtūnāṃ ghaṃṭāyāśca pavitrakaṃ 25
aṣṭāviṃśati taṃtūnāṃ kuṃḍasya capavitrakaṃ
catuśśataṃtu taṃtūnāṃ prāsādasya pavitrakam 26
caturviṃśati taṃtūnāṃ dhūpadaṃḍa pavitrakaṃ
paṃcatriṃśati taṃtūnāṃ dīpadaṃḍa pavitrakaṃ 27
aṣṭāviṃśati taṃtūnāṃ ālayasya pavitrakaṃ

ekonaviśattaṃtūnāṃ gopurasya pavitrakaṃ 28
navatriṃśa cca taṃtūnāṃ balapīṭhapavitrakaṃ
upapīṭhapavitraṃ tu paṃcaviṃśati taṃtubhiḥ 29
sūtraṃtu triguṇaṃ kṛtvā prākārasya pavitrakaṃ
pavitra graṃdhi saṃkhyā
evaṃ kṛtvā pavitrāṇi (tatograṃdhīśca)punargraṃdhīṃśca baṃdhayet 30
aṃguṣṭha mātramānaṃtu graṃdhitānāṃ yadhātadhaṃ
kaniṣṭhāṃgulimānaṃ vā madhyamāṃguli mānataḥ, 31
vanamālā pavitretu śatamaṣṭottaraṃ śubhaṃ
adhivāsa pavitreṣu graṃdhayodvādaśasmṛtāḥ 32
graṃdhayaścāṣṭa vijñeyā parivāra pavitrake
graṃdhayo dvādaśaproktāṃ kṣemāyuścavi varthanaṃ 33
anyeṣu ca pavitreṣu yathāsaṃbhava mācaret
kuṃkumośīrakarpūra caṃdanādi vilepayet 34

prathamehanirātrau
pavitrāṇi vinirmāya śāstroktavidhinā tataḥ
sāyaṃkāletu saṃprāpte ṛtvigbhissahadeśikaḥ 35
yajamānena sahito saṃviśedbhagavadgṛhaṃ
bhagavatprārthanā
tatradevaṃ namabhyarcya daṃḍavatpraṇameccavai 36
uddhāya prāṃjanirbhūtvā gādhāmenā mudīrayet
saṃvatsaropacāreṣu yatkiṃcillopasaṃbhave 37
taddoṣaśamanārdhāya bhavatprītyai karomyahaṃ
pavitrāropaṇaṃ deva tasmādanugṛhāṇamāṃ 38
pavitrāthivāsaḥ
iti vijñāpya deveśaṃ viṣvakcenaṃ tu pūjayet
pūṇyāvāhacanaṃ kṛtvā pavitrāṇica prokṣayet 39
rātrau pratisaraṃbaddhvā darbhāṇāṃ naṃstaresvanet
dvitīya divase
prātaruddhāyani yato daṃtadhāvana pūrvakaṃ 40
snānaṃsaṃdhyādikaṃ kṛtvā japtvāmaṃtraṃ yadhāvidhi
bhagavaṃtaṃ samabhyarcya kalaśaisnapanaṃ caret 41
mahāhavirmi vedyādha vaiṣṇavān bhojaye ttataḥ
rātrautu nityapūjāṃte cakrābjaṃ vartayedguruḥ 42
pūrvoktena krameṇaiva maṃḍale devatān yajet
upoṣṭya deśikeṃdrastu tūryaghoṣe pravartite 43
pātrasthāni pavitrāṇi (dhānyarāśiṣu vinyaset) dhānyarāśyau vinikṣipet
gaṃdhādibhissamabhyarca puṣbāṇi vikire ttataḥ 44
vastreṇācchādya pātrāṇi maṃtreṇā streṇapūjayet
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
athivāsapavitreṣu devaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ yajet
gāyatyrā viṣṇumaṃtraiśca cābhi maṃtyratataḥ paraṃ
dīkṣitānāṃ pavitreṣu hetirājaṃ prapūjayet
cakrarājasya gāyatyrā cābhimaṃtraṇamācaran
parivārapavitreṣu samāvāhyakhageśvaraṃ
tanmaṃtreṇa ca gāyatyrā deśikeṃdrobhimaṃtrayet]
śālāyāśca caturdikṣu (baliṃdatvāyādhākramaṃ)bilamannena kārayet 45
pavitrādhivāsa
catusthānārcanaṃ kṛtvāhomaṃ kuryādyadhāvidhi
homaḥ
adhivāsapavitrāṇi gṛhītvādeśikassvayaṃ 46
kuṃbheca karakekuṃḍe maṃḍale ca yathākramaṃ
tadviṣṇoriti maṃtreṇa nyasanīyāni padmaja 47

tṛtīya divase pavitrāropaṇaṃ
tataḥprabhāte deveśaṃ cakrābjesthāpya pūjayet
arghyādi dhūpadīpāṃtaṃ naivedyaṃ tu caturvidhaṃ 48
homaṃtupūrvavatkṛtvāhomaśeṣaṃ samāpayet
tatodhṛvādi biṃbānāṃ karmārcānāṃ yathākramaṃ 49
viṣvaksenādi bhaktānāṃ chaṃḍādīnāṃ tadhaivaca
durgārudreṃtra devānāṃ (khageśasya tadhaivacaḥ) garuḍastyacabhaktitaḥ 50
ghaṭārghyasṛskṛvādīnāṃpātrāṇāṃdhūpadīpayoḥ
kuṃbhānāṃ toraṇānāṃ ca balipīṭhāṃtamevahi 51
evaṃ pavitrāṇyāropya tūryaghoṣepravartite
bhaktānāṃ dhāraṇārthāyakalpitāni yadhātathaṃ 52
pavitrāropaṇaṃ kāryaṃ vimānegopureṣuca
pavitrāṇi samādāya nyasya devasyapādayoḥ 53
yajamānā yadātavyaṃ dhārayan deśika purā
ṛtvijāṃbrāhmaṇādīnāṃ bhaktānāṃcai vadāpayet 54
pavitradhāraṇabhedaḥ
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā ścaiva dhārayyuścahāravat
yajñopavīta vaddhāryaṃ vaiśyairvaiṣṇavalakṣaṇaiḥ 55
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
yobhaktyādharatecedaṃ pavitraṃ śraddhayānvitaḥ
tasyatatsaṃ vatcarakṛtakarmalopovinasyati
dadaṃti sarvakarmāṇi tasyapūrṇaṃ mahāphalaṃ]
tatodevasya pārśvetu sthitvedaṃ prārdhayedguruḥ
bhagavatprārthanā
bhamavan puṃḍarīkākṣa bhaktābhīṣṭa pradāyakā 56
saṃvatsarasya madhyetu jñānato jñānanato pivā
mayāca madanujñātai stathānyaiḥ paricārakaiḥ 57
pūjālopādikaṃ yaccakṛtaṃ taddoṣaśāṃtaye
karmasaṃpūraṇārthāya pavitrāropaṇaṃ mayā 58
kṛtaṃtadanugṛhṇīṣva (bhaktānugraha kāmyayā) madanugraha kāmyayā
asvataṃtraścakiṃcijñaḥ kiṃcatkartu maśaktavān 59
tasmāttvatpādavinyasta bhārohaṃ preritastvayā
sarvaṃ karomi deveśa rakṣamāṃ śaraṇāgataṃ 60
iti saṃpārdhyadeveśaṃ yānamāropyadeśikaḥ
pavitradhārakairbhaktairviśeṣotsava mācaret 61
utsavāṃto citasnānaṃ maṃdire kārayettataḥ
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
(pavitradhāraṇaphalaḥ)
naivedyāṃtaṃ samabhyarcya devāgāreniveśayet
navāhaṃ saptapaṃcāhaṃ tyrahamekāhamevavā
yāmaṃ vāpyarthayāmaṃ vā muhūrta madhavāguruḥ
devadevaṃ samabhyarchya saviśeṣaṃ yadhātadhā
utsamāṃtetu divase sāyaṃkāletu deśikaḥ
yānamāropya deveśaṃ grāmaṃ nītvā pradakṣiṇaṃ
nṛttavāditra nirghoṣai rālayaṃtu samāviśet
brāhmaṇānbhojayitvātu yathā bhavavistaraṃ]
ācāryo devamabhyarcya praviśedyāgamaṃṭapaṃ 62
tatramaṃḍalamadhyasthaṃ kuṃḍasthaṃ kuṃbhagaṃtathā
pavitrāvarohaṇaṃ
demudvāsya vidhivannitya pūjāṃ samācaret 63
ekaviṃśaddinevāsi caturdaśa dinepivā
saptame divasenāpi trirātre vāvarohaṇaṃ 64
pavitrāṇi sarvāṇyapi ācāryā yvadadyāt
tatpanitrāṇi saṃchāvā nācāryāya pradāpayet
tatpradānaṃ hareḥ prītyai bhaviṣyatisasaṃśayaḥ 65
anyathā viniyuktaśce drārārāṣṭraṃ vinasyati
ācārya dakṣiṇādi
tatkāle yajamānastu ācāryaṃ toṣayeddhanaiḥ 66
iti samyaksamākhyātaṃ pavitrāropaṇaṃ (kramaṃ) paraṃ
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
pavitrāropaṇavidhirnāmaṣaḍviṃśodhyāyaḥ
śrīśrīśrī
<27>
oṃ
śrīmatpāṃcarātra divyāgame śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
saptaviṃśodhyāyaḥ
saṃvatsarotsavavidhiḥ
vasaṃtotsavaḥ
śrībhagavān
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
adhātassaṃ pravakṣyaumi saṃvatsara vratakramaṃ]
vasaṃtadeva tṛptyarthaṃ varṣādau pūjayeddhariṃ
vidhivatsnapanaṃ kṛtvā (nūtanāṃbarabhūṣaṇaiḥ)navābharaṇa vastrakaiḥ 1
vasaṃtaṛtu saṃprāptaiḥ puṣpamālyairmanoharaiḥ
alaṃkṛtya tadodevaṃ dakṣiṇāmukha marcayet 2
maṃdirevāpi codyāne nadītīre yathāruci
devamabhyarcya gaṃthādyaiḥ phalādīni nivedayet 3
śrīrāmanavamyutsavaḥ
caitremāsi sitepakṣe navamyā ca punarvasau
śrīrāmajanmadivase devadevaṃ jagatpatim 4
napabhiḥ kalaśaissnāpya haviraṃtaṃ namarcayet
sītālakṣmaṇa saṃyuktaṃ hanumatsahitaṃ vibhum 5
śrīrāmaṃ ḍolikādautu vinyasyotsava mācaret
damanotsavaḥ
caitramāsetu paurṇamyāṃ kuryāddamanikotsavam 6
puṣaistu maṃṭapaṃ kṛtvādamanīdalasaṃyutaiḥ
alaṃkuryāttatodevaṃ puṣpairdamanikādalaiḥ 7
saṃpūjya vidhinākuryāddhomaṃda manikādalaiḥ
nṛsiṃhajayaṃtī
caitre śravaṇanakṣatre sāyaṃ kāle nṛkesariḥ 8
bhūmāvāvirabhūttasmāddevaṃ tasmin viśeṣataḥ
arcayitvā viśeṣeṇa śītalādīnni vedayet 9
nīrājanaṃ tatodatvā stotraissaṃcoṣayeddharim
kalhārotsavaḥ
kalhārakusumai reva devasyotsava mācaret 10
vaiśākhe śuklapakṣetu paurṇamāsyāṃ yadhāvithi
catusthāṇacananaṃ kṛtvā grāmotsava madhācaret 11
divase pūrvabhāge vāmadhyehnetsacopaṃ hitat
vāmanajayaṃtī
nakṣatre śravaṇecaiva pūrvāhnetu trivikramaḥ 12
prādurāśīttadātasya cotsavaṃ kārayeddvijaḥ
hanumajjayaṃtī
hanumajjanmanakṣatre sītārāghavasaṃyutam 13
mārutiṃ vithivatsnāpya viśeṣotsava mācaret
svāpotsavaḥ
āṣāḍhetu sitepakṣe dvādaśyāṃ devamarcayet 14
śayanārthaṃ tu devasya māseṣu ca catuṣvapi
viviktebhimate deśe paryaṃkaṃ (paryaṃkaṃkārayedguruḥ) tatrakārayet 15
devībhyāṃ sahadeveśaṃ śāyayitvārcatettataḥ
[athikapāṭhāṇi
śayyāberetu kurvītasvāpaṃ deśikasattavaṃ]
bodhanaikādasīyāvattā vatkālaṃ prapūjanaṃ 16
śrīkṛṣṇa janmotsavaḥ
śrāvaṇe kṛṣṇapakṣetu rohiṇyāmaṣṭamītithau
śrīkṛṣṇabhagavajjanma (tasmin)dineśauriṃ samarcayet 17
snapanaṃ tu yathāśakti grāme kuryānmahotsavam
yetaddivasamārabhyamāsaṃ pakṣaṃ navāhakaṃ 18
saptāhaṃ vādhapaṃcāhaṃ tyrahamekāhamevavā
śrīkṛṣṇalīlānaṭakaiḥ grāmotsavamadhācaret 19
pratinityaṃ viśeṣārcā mācarettu yadhāvasu
varāhajayaṃtī
māse bhādrapadecaiva śravaṇarkṣaṃ gateśaśiḥ 20
āvirāsīdbhūvarāho viśeṣṇārcayeccataṃ
snapanaṃ cotsavaṃ caiva pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ 21
vīralakṣmyitsavavidhiḥ
adhacāśvayujemāsi vīralakṣmyotsavaṃ caret
pratipaddinamārabhya navāhotsava mācaret 22
saṃsnāpya kalaśairnitya malaṃkṛtyatu deśikaḥ
catusthānārcanaṃ kṛtvā haviraṃtaṃ prapūjyaca 23
yānedevīṃ samāropya devaṃ grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ
evaṃ pratidinaṃkṛtvā navamyāṃ kuṃbhatoyataḥ 24
prokṣayennityapūjāṃte devīṃ devenapūjayet
vijayadaśamī
daśamyā maśvamāropya devaṃ grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ 25
nītvāśamīṃ samāsādya tanmūlesthāvyayānakaṃ
viṣvaksenaṃ tu saṃprūjya puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ 26
prokṣyapuṇyāhatoyena dhanurbhāṇau prapūjayet
pūrvavadbāṇamokṣaṃtu kṛtvā devaṃ capūjayet 27
śamīpatradhāraṇa phalaṃ
śamīpatrāṇi saṃgṛhya pūjaye ddevapāvayoḥ
depasya padavinyastha śamīpatrāṇiyenarāḥ 28
śirasādhārayiṣyaṃti te dhṛvaṃ muktakilbiṣāḥ
vaibhavenatato devaṃ nayeddevālayaṃ prati 29
āsthānamaṃṭapebhyarcya snapanaṃ kārayettataḥ
maṃdire saṃpraviśyaiva nityotsava madhācaret 30
dīpāvalyutsavaḥ narakacaturdaśī
māsicāśvayuje kṛṣṇecaturdasyāṃ hariḥpurā
narakaṃ saṃharatta smāttannāmneta ccaturdaśī 31
etasyāṃ devadevaṃtu satailābhyaṃ janesatu
saṃsnāpya nūtanairvasrai ralaṃkṛtya vibhūṣaṇaiḥ 32
caturvidhāni cānānni pānīyāni samarpayet
dīpāvalī
amāvāsyāṃtu sāyāhne nityapūjāṃvidhāyaca 33
devālayeṣu staṃbheṣu prākāre gopureṣu ca
dīpānāṃ paṃktayasthsāpyanānākāraiḥ pṛdhak pṛdhak 34
tatodevaṃtu devībhyā māropya gajavāhane
chatracāmara vādyaiśca bhaṭasaṃghaiśca sāyudhaiḥ 35
prākārevāpi grāmevā kāray dutsavaṃ tataḥ
devaṃ praveśayeddhāmni mahatāvibhavenatu 36
bodhanotsavaḥ
kārtike śuklapakṣetu dvādasyāṃ bodayeddariṃ
uttiṣṭhayitimaṃtreṇa vādyaghoṣāpurassaraṃ 37
tato devaṃ tu saṃsnāpya paṃcaviṃśatibhirghaṭaiḥ
homaṃtu vidhinākṛtvā cā bhyarcyavidhicoditaṃ 38
utsavaṃ tupra kurvīta yadhāvibhavistaraiḥ
kṛttikādīpotsavaḥ
kārtikyāṃ vahninakṣatre rohiṇyāṃ vāparehani 39
paurṇamāsyāṃ vṛṣlagne dīpāropaṇa mācaret
tadarthaṃ kautukaṃ baddhvā dhānyarāśau yathāvidhi 40
depasyaṃtu purobhāge dīpapātraṃtu vinyaset
lohajaṃmṛṇmaye vādhatatprātregoghṛtaṃkṣipet 41
abhāvetilatailaṃ vācānyadhādoṣa māpnuyāt
ṛjavartiṃtatonyasya jvālayeddhomavahninā 42
agnimaṃtraṃ tatojaptvā dīpamabhyarcyabhaktitaḥ
taddīpenaivasarvāṃśca dīpānāropaye ddvijaiḥ 43
paricāraiśca bhaktaiśca nānāvādya purassaram
devaṃyāne samāropya darśaye ddīpamālikāḥ 44
mahāhavirni vedyādhacāpūpān pṛthukānapi
nivedya gamayeddevaṃ maṃdirābhyaṃtaraṃ tataḥ 45
dhanurmāsotsavaḥ
dhanurmāsetu saṃprāpte devadevaṃ prapūjayet
aruṇodayavelāyāṃ mudgānnādīnni vedayet 46
māsāṃtamevaṃ kṛtvātu tasmīnviṃśaddineṣuca
maṃṭape devamāsthāpya kuryādadhya yanotsavam 47
ṛgvedaṃ pūrvadigbhāge yajurvedaṃtu dakṣiṇe
paścime sāmavedaṃtu adharvaṃ cottaretathā 48
itihāsaṃ cāgnideśe naiṛtyāṃ stotramucyate
dharmaśāstraṃtu vāyuvye caiśānyāṃ maṃtrapāṭhakaṃ 49
bhakairviracitaistotre rdeśabhāṣārusāribhiḥ
evamādibhiranyaiśca kārayedutsavaṃ mahat 50
tilapadmotsavaḥ
māghaśukletu paṃcamyāṃ tilapadmotsavaṃ bhavet
devasya puratobhāgegomayāliptabhūtale 51
raṃgavalyādikaṃ kṛtvā (vastreṇācchādyadeśikaḥ)vastreṇācchādayettataḥ
kṛṣṇājinaṃ tatonyasya viṣṇusūktaṃ japanguruḥ 52
punarvastraṃ samāstīrya tilān tasyoparinyaset
dvaubhāraṃcaika bhāraṃ vā samāstīryatilopari 53
dhānyapūrṇāni pātrāṇi dvedve pratidiśaṃ nyaset
tailaghṛtadadhyādikuṃbhānāṃsthāpanaṃ
dakṣiṇe prasthatailena pūritaṃ kuṃbha muttare 54
tatpramāṇājya sahitaṃ kuṃbhamanyaṃ niveśayet
dadnāprasthacatuṣkena pūritaṃ kalaśaṃ nyaset 55
purobhāgetatogaṃdha puṣpādyaiḥ pūjayettataḥ
padmamadhye śriyopetaṃ garuḍādi samanvitam 56
[adhikapāṭhāni
devaṃ saṃpūjyavidhivaddeśiko śraddhayānvitaḥ]
tilaissahasraṃ juhuyādacāryomūlavidyayā
tatpadmaṃ bhaktito dadyāddevāgre vāripūrvakam 57
priyatāṃ bhagavān viṣṇuritimaṃtraṃ samuccaran
evamukta prakāreṇa tilavadmaṃ dadātiyaḥ 58
ātmānaṃ ca pitrūn sarvān punāti ca parāvarān
śrīmahālakṣmutsava
phālguṇe śuklaphalguṇyāṃ mahālakṣmyotsavaṃ (caret)hitam 59
tadarthaṃmaṃṭapaṃ kuryā caturdvāra samanvitam
tatrasiṃhāsane sthāpya padmeṣṭadalasaṃyute 60
nīlamegha nibhaṃ viṣṇuṃ sukhāsīnaṃ śriyāsaha
vāsudevādikān dikṣu pūjaye (tkramayogataḥ)tkamalāsana 61
vidikṣuhastina ścaiva svarṇapīṭhasya pārśvayoḥ
pūjanīyaunidhītatra iṃdrādīnparito yajet 62
dvāratoraṇakuṃbhā dīnabhyarcya vidhipūrvakam
tato gururmahālakṣmīṃ pūjaye dviṣṇunāsaha 63
padmabilvadalādyaiśca caruṇāpāyasenaca
dvādaśākṣaramaṃtreṇa śrīdevyāhomamācaret 64
japeyuḥ ṛtvijodikṣu śrīsūktāni yadhākramam
lakṣmīstotrān paṭhettatra bhaktiyuktenacetasā 65
ityevaṃ pūjayeddevaṃ vatsarāṃtaṃ yathāvidhi
rājārāṣṭrābhivṛddhisyā ddurbhikṣaṃ haratedhṛvam 66
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
saṃvatsarotsavavidhirnāma saptaviṃśodhyāyaḥ
<28>
oṃ
śrīmatpāṃcarātra divyāgame śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
aṣṇaviṃśodhyāyaḥ
sālagrāma lakṣaṇam
sālagrāmalakṣaṇa śravaṇe brahma praśnaḥ
brahmā
bhagavandevadeveśa sarvasaṃpatkaraprabho
sālagrāmādimūrtīnāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vaktumarhasi 1
bhagavatbativacanaṃ
śrībhagavānuvācaḥ
sālagrāmalakṣaṇaṃ
śruṇubrahman pravakṣyāmi sālagrāmasyalakṣaṇam
yasyārcanaṃ sadānrūṇāṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃ (bhavet smṛtaṃ)śubham 2
sālagrāmasya pratiṣṭhāvarjanaṃ
sālagrāme sadāśauri stiṣṭhatyevanasaṃśayaḥ
pratiṣṭhāpya samācīnaṃ sadāpūjyomumukṣubhiḥ 3
teṣāṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃ (vakṣye)vatsasamāsācchruṇupāṃpratam
kūrmamūrtila lakṣaṇaṃ
kūrmasyādunnataḥ pṛṣṭhośvetabhāgokhurāṃ citaḥ 4
dvicakro bhūdharākhyoyaṃ manoradhaphalapradaḥ
maṃdhara dharakūrma lakṣaṇaṃ
biṃdudvayānvito cakraśaṃkhadvayamu topica 5
dīrghadakṣinā nāmāsyuḥ bhūgevalayapaṃcakaḥ
sāmaṃdaradharaḥ kūrmamūrtisyātsarvakāmadaḥ 6
ādikūrma lakṣaṇaṃ
vṛttāyatākūrmamūrtiḥ kanakacchavi saṃyutaḥ
snuhipuṣpākṛtirvāsi cakrasyo bhayapārśvataḥ 7
ādikūrmaiti khyātassarya kāmaphalapradaḥ
kūrmalakṣaṇaṃ
vartulāma karākārā dīrghadvārātu vaidvija 8
nābhicakrayutā samyakkūrmākārātu porśvataḥ
sthirāsanācatatpṛṣṭh unnatānīla lohitā 9
kūrmamūrti ritikhyātā putrapautrābhivṛddhidā
kṣatriyāṇāṃ (tadārājñāṃ)cahitathā sarvaśatruvināśinī 10
lakṣmīvarāhalakṣaṇaṃ
iṃdranīlanibhasthūla śśīrekhālāṃchitaśśubhaḥ
dakṣiṇetu sthite cakre samalagne pradeśataḥ 11
vanamālāyuto lakṣmīvārāhaśśubhaḍassṛtaḥ
atasīkusumaprakhyā nīlotpala nibhastadā 12
bhūvarāha lakṣaṇaṃ
dīrghadvāramuto dīrghadvārayukta ssagaṃhvaraḥ
pṛṣṭhonnatastu dīrghāsyo vāmataccakramunnataḥ 13
pṛṣṭharekhā samāyukto varāhākṛti revaca
āsyamātraṃtu vā brahmān pādalāṃgūlakaṃtathāḥ 14
dṛśyatekauṃstubhākāra rekhāpṛṣṭh ca pārśvake
bhūvarāhayiti pokto rājyalakṣmī pradnṛṇām 15
varāhamūrti strīvidhaḥ
nānāratna yutaścāpi kālavarṇayutopi ca
dīrghadvārayutassnigdho dvāramadhye dvicakrayut 16
vanamālā yutaśchāpi varāhaḥ parikīrtitaḥ
putrapautra dhanaiśvarya (gobhūdhānya vivarthanaḥ) bhūprabhurjñāna vardhanaḥ 17
[adhika pāṭhāni
ekādaśyāṃ viśeṣeṇa pūjanaṃ mokṣasiddidaṃ]
lakṣmīnarasiṃhaḥ
vāmebhāge sthite cakre kṛṣṇavarṇa ssabiṃdukaḥ
lakṣmīnṛsiṃhavikhyāto bhuktimukti phalapradaḥ 18
vīralakṣmīnṛsiṃhaḥ
dvicakrastu bṛhadvaktraḥ kapilaḥ kanakaprabhaḥ
vīralakṣmīnṛsiṃhākhyo brahmacāribhirarcitaḥ 19
vidāraṇanṛkhaṃha lakṣaṇaṃ
kapilo graṃdhimūlārdho śaktibiṃdu khurākṛtiḥ
karālavadano devaḥ sṛdhucakra ssudaṃṣṭrakaḥ 20
vidāraṇābhithāna śca nṛsiṃhodīrghakesaraḥ
brahmacaryedha pūjyaśca nānyadhāpūjito bhavet 21
jvālāvidāraṇa nārasiṃhaḥ
aṃtaścakro bṛhadvīryaḥ dīkṣiṇonnata mastakaḥ
jvālāvidāraṇonārasiṃhaudaṃṣṭropaśobhitaḥ 22
brahmacarya vihīnena (pūjite)pūjito naṣṭadāyakaḥ
nṛsiṃhalakṣaṇaṃ
nārasiṃhastri biṃduśca kapilābhobṛhadbilaḥ 23
daṃṣṭrākarālavadano bṛhadāsyoti rūkṣakaḥ
adhavānārasiṃhaśca udarevā caturmukhaḥ 24
jhaṣākṛtirvā cakrasya pārśvdaṃṣṭrākṛtistadhāḥ
nārasiṃha itikhyāto yatibhiḥ pūjyaucyate 25
kapila nārasiṃha lakṣaṇaṃ
sthūlacakradvayaṃ madhye guṃjālākṣmaktakartanaḥ
sphuṭite viṣame cakre nārasiṃhastu kāpilaḥ 26
saṃpūjya muktimāpnoti saṃgrāme vijayībhavet
sarvatomukhanārasiṃhaḥ
saptacakrobahumukhaḥ samaṃtāssvarṇabhūṣitaḥ 27
bahuvargaṃ mokṣadosyānnṛsiṃhosarvatomukhaḥ
viliktāsyo vāmacakro vartulaḥ kapilaprabhaḥ 28
bhītākhyanṛsiṃhaḥ nārasiṃhogṛhasthānāṃ bhītido bhītināmakaḥ
pātālanṛsiṃha lakṣaṇaṃ
trāditrayodaśāṃtaistu cakrairyuktaṃ bahūdaraṃ 29
bahuvarṇayutaṃ vāpi bahudvārasamanvitam
pātālanarasiṃhākhyaṃ (yatīnāṃ mokṣasiddhidaṃ)bhikṣūṇāmamṛta pradam 30
tattanmūrtyuktacihnaisturahitaścedvi śeṣataḥ
pātāla narasiṃhovā bahurūpodhanāphavet 31
kukṣinārasiṃha lakṣaṇaṃ
alpiṣṭha malpacakrāḍhya malpabhāraṃ mahodaram
tatkukṣinārasiṃhākhyaṃ vanavāsi bhirarcitam 32
rākṣasa nṛsiṃhaḥ
bahucchidraṃ bhinnadaktraṃ suvarṇaghanakāṃtimat
dvicakraṃ rākṣasaṃjñeyaṃ nṛsiṃhaṃ gṛhadāhakam 33
vidyujihvānṛsiṃhaḥ
dūrvābhonnataśīrṣaṃ ca vidyujjihvānṛsiṃhakaḥ
dvicakra taśśobhayuto makhodāridyra dorcitaḥ 34
athomukha nṛsiṃhaḥ
mukhe pārśveca pṛṣṭhca tricakrairupaśobhitaḥ
adhomukhanṛsiṃhākhya marcakānāṃ vimuktidaṃ 35
bālanṛsiṃhaḥ
sūkṣmaraṃdhraṃdvi cakrāḍhyaṃ vanamālāvibhūṣitam
tadbālanarasiṃhākhyaṃ nṛṇāṃ saṃsāramocakam 36
vibhīṣaṇa nṛsiṃhaḥ
dīrghākārodīrghacakro ṛṣakāsyo bṛhattaraḥ
vibhīṣaṇanṛsiṃhākhyo nṛṇāṃ duḥkhapradassadā 37
dadhināmana lakṣaṇaṃ
atihrasvovartulāsyo vāmanaḥ parikīrtitaḥ
atasī kusumaprakhyo biṃdavaḥ pariśobhitaḥ 38
cakradvaya samāyukta śśvetabiṃduyutemakhe
dadhivāmana saṃjñāsyā dgobhūdhānyadhanapradaḥ 39
abhīṣṭavāmana lakṣaṇaṃ
vartulaṃ snigdhamatyaṃtaṃ spaṣṭacakra samanvitam
hrasvamunnatamaścaiva dīrghāsya mati gaṃhvare 40
spuradrekhāmalayutaṃ nābhistanyonnatā bhavet
kesarāṃbhaṃcavai brahman dṛsyate cakrapārśvataḥ 41
abhiṣṭavāmanaṃ jñeyaṃ sukhasaubhāgyadāyakam
annavṛddhiṃ prajāvṛddhiṃ bhūlābhaṃ diśatibhṛśam 42
vāmana lakṣaṇaṃ
madhyacakramatihrasva matisnigdhaṃtu kāṃsyavat
spaṣṭacakraṃvāmanaṃ syāt sṛṇāmīpsitakāmadam 43
bālavāmana lakṣaṇaṃ
atasī kusumaprakhyaṃ kiṃcidunnatamastakam
kiṃcida spaṣṭacakraṃ ca kāmadaṃ bālavāmanam 44
bṛhatkavāmanaḥ
vartulaṃ nīlamekhābhaṃ vanamāla vibhūṣitam
sūkṣmaraṃdhraṃ bṛhatkukṣiṃvāmanaṃcakrayukśubhaṃ 45
paraśurāma lakṣaṇaṃ
pītakṛṣṇāruṇopeto dīrghākārobṛhadbilaḥ
bhittabhāgagataṃ cakraṃ vāmevā dakṣiṇepivā 46
cakrabhāge bhavedbiṃdu paraśvākṛtirevavā
pṛṣṭhvā pārśvatovāpi rekhādaṃṣṭrākṛtirbhavet 47
(jāmadagni savijñeyo)jāmadagnistu tanmūrtiḥpūjyasyābhūbhṛtāmapi
śāṃtaparaśurāmaḥ
kevalaṃ lāṃchitaṃ vṛṣṭh paraśvākṛti rekhayā 48
sacakro jāmadagnistu śāṃtākhyo śāṃtidorcite
virarāmaḥ
bāṇatūṇīra cāpādyāḥ kuṃḍalasṛksṛvānvitaḥ 49
sūkṣmakesara cakrāḍhyo vīrarāma śśriyāsaha
sītārāmaḥ
vadane pyekavadana śca tu ścakrāṃ baraḥ prabhuḥ 50
cāpabāṇāṃ kuśacchatra dhvajacāmara saṃyutaḥ
vanamālā dharodeva ssitārāmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ 51
ṣaṭcakrasītārāmaḥ
astrakṣatena dhanuṣānūpureṇa cavāṃchitaḥ
bilatraya samāyukta ṣṣaṭcudarśana saṃyutaḥ 52
śyāmalonnatapṛṣṭha śca sthūlajaṃbūphalaṃ yathā
sītābilasamāyukto dīrghajaṃ būśilopamaḥ 53
sītārāmassavijñeya stārakabrahma saṃjñikaḥ
sa evamaṣṭacakrastu trilokeṣuca durlabhaḥ 54
daśakaṃṭhakulāṃtaka rāmaḥ
sacakraḥkukkuṭākāra śśyāmalaḥ vṛṣṭhamunnataḥ
rekhādvaya samāyukto dvārapārśvetu padmaja 55
thanurākṛti rekhācadṛśyate pārśvatodhavā
pṛṣṭhato vābhavedrāmo daśakaṃṭhāṃtakaśśubhaḥ 56
bālarāmaḥ
pṛṣṭhabhāge paṃcarekhāḥ cāpabāṇauca pārśvayoḥ
bālarāmodvi cakrāḍhyaḥ putradāyīna saṃśayaḥ 57
vijaya rāmaḥ
divyabāṇena saṃyukto cāpatūṇīra saṃyutaḥ
karāladanorakta biṃduyukcakraśobhitaḥ 58
śrīdoviyarāmākhyo keśalīkṛta cakriṇaḥ
hṛṣṭarāmaḥ
mūrthni mālādhanurbāṇaḥ pārśvekhurayutastadhā 59
hṛṣṭharāmodvi cakrāḍhyo bhuktimukti phalapradaḥ
kodaṃḍarāmaḥ
bāṇatūṇīra cāpāḍhyo vartulaḥ kiṃcidāyataḥ 60
kodaṃḍarāmaśca krāḍhyo nīlāṃbudanibhaśśubhaḥ
kavitvarāma
ekacakraṃ tu vadane śyāmavarṇa nsuśobhanaḥ 61
sārāmamūrtī vijñeyaḥ pūjakasya kavitvadā
yaduvīrakṛṣṇa
kṛṣṇotikṛṣṇonasthūlaḥ cakraścātīva śobhanaḥ 62
yaduvīra itikhyāta śśrīyaśaḥ puṣṭidonṛṇām
śrīkṛṣṇaḥ
kṛṣṇaḥ pītaḥ kṛśatanuḥ bhittipārśvetu cakrayut 63
dvāratulyo bhavennābhiḥ kūrmākārastu pṛṣṭhataḥ
pārśva(pārśacakrayutokṛṣṇaḥ dharmadaṛ pāpanāśakṛt)cakrāḍhya kṛṣṇoyaṃ dharmadaḥ pāpanāśanaḥ 64
govākṛṣṇa lakṣaṇaṃ
(vanamālikācakrayutaḥ śrīvatseva ca lāṃchitaḥ) vanamālāyutaścakra yutaśśrīvatsalāṃchanaḥ
daṃḍaśṛṃgayutaḥ pārśve veṇunāśobhitemukhe 65
gopālakṛṣṇamūrtisyāt gobhūdhānyadhanapradaḥ
madanagopāla lakṣaṇaṃ
śiraścakradvayeveṇudaṃḍa śśrīvatsalāṃchanaḥ 66
mālāyuktaḥ pārśvabhāge taruyugmaścakalpakam
sasyānmadanagopāla kṛṣṇaḥ kuṃḍalaśobhitaḥ 67
putrapautradhanaiśvarya sarvalokaikapaśyadaḥ
saṃtānagopāla murti lakṣaṇaṃ
dīrghākāraḥ kṛṣṇavarṇopyarthacaṃdrāsyacakrayut 68
kṛṣṇassaṃtānagopālaḥ putrapautrābhivṛddhidaḥ
bālakṛṣṇaḥ
unnatomurdhni kṣṇābhonimnodhastrāttribiṃdukaḥ 69
sabālakṛṣṇdve cakredīrghāsyoputrabhāgyadaḥ
śrīkṛṣṇaḥ
samacakraṃ dvāradeśe kṛṣṇavarṇassuśobhanaḥ 70
sā kṛṣṇamūrtī vijñeyā pūjitāsaukhyadāyanī
buddhamūrti lakṣaṇaṃ
aṃtargaṃhvarasaṃyuktā cakrahīnāyathābhavet 71
buddhamūrti ritikhyātā dadātiparamaṃ padam
kalikimūrti lakṣaṇaṃ
atirakta ssūkṣmabilaḥsṛṣṭhacakraḥ sthirāsanaḥ 72
kṛpāṇākṛtirekhāca dvārasyoparipṛṣṭhak
mlecchanāśobhavetkalkī sarvakilbiṣanāśanaḥ 73
matsamūrtiḥ
dīrghākāraḥ śyāmavarṇaḥ vanamālāvibhūṣitaḥ
arcane sarvasaṃsiddhiḥ putrapautrapravarthanam 74
keśavamūrti lakṣaṇaṃ
rekhātrayaṃ dvādeśe pṛṣṭhpadmasyalābhanaṃ
saubhāgyaḥ keśavodadyācca tuṣkoṇaddicakrayut
śyāmanārāyaṇa lakṣaṇaṃ
śyāmavarṇayuto vaktrenābhicakrastadhonnataḥ 76
dīrgharekhātrayopeto dakṣiṇe suṣiraṃ pṛthak
śyāmanārāyaṇaḥ prokto bhuktimukti phalapradaḥ 77
lakṣmīnārāyaṇaḥ
ekavaktraścatuścakro vartulaśyāmavarṇakaḥ
dhvajavajrāṃ kuśopeto mālāyukta ssabiṃdukaḥ 78
nātihrasvonāti dīrghaḥ lakṣminārāyaṇanmṛtaḥ
tanyadarśanamātreṇa cābhīṣṭa phalamāpnuyāt 79
sūdhavamūrti lakṣaṇaṃ
mathuvarṇo madhyacakrasnigdhaścakra dharastathā
mādhavassatu vijñeyo yatīnāṃ mokṣadāyakaḥ 80
goviṃdamūrtiḥ
nātisthūlaḥ paṃcavaktro goviṃdodaśacakrakaḥ
kṛṣṇavarṇasamāyukto sarvakāmaphalapradaḥ 81
viṣṇumūrti lakṣaṇaṃ
vāmacakro bṛhaddvāra munnatomadhya nimnaḥ
sakāpilassnigdhavarṇoviṣṇussarpabhalapradaḥ 82
madhusūdana lakṣaṇaṃ
madhusūdana nāmāsyā dekacakro mahādyutiḥ
sarvavarṇa samāyukto mahāteyapradaśśubhaḥ 83
trivikramaḥ
cakradvayayuto vaktro ekacakraṃtu pārśvataḥ
trivikramastrikoṇāḍhyo vipraiḥ pūjyastu netaraiḥ 84
śrīdharaḥ
mālavatcridharodevaḥ cihnito vanamālāyā
kadaṃbakusumākāro rekhāpaṃcakasaṃyutaḥ 85
cakradvayaśca vadane sarvakāmaphalapradaḥ
hṛṣīkeśaḥ
arthacaṃdrākṛtirdevo hṛṣīkeśa udāhṛtaḥ 86
bilecakrayuto deva ssarvābhīṣṭapradāyakaḥ
padmanābhaḥ
niṣkesaraṃ tūrthvacakre madhyacakraṃ sa kesaram 87
padmanābhassavijñeya ssarvakāmaphalapradaḥ
dāmodaraḥ
dāmodarastathāsthūlo madhyacakraḥ prakīrtitaḥ 88
dūrvābhodvāra saṃkīrṇaṃ pītarekhāyutaśśubhaḥ
saṃkarṣaṇamūrti lakṣaṇaṃ
dvicakra ekasaṃlagna ekabhāgaśca puṣkaraḥ 89
saṃkarṣaṇassavijñoyo raktābhaśśobhanapradaḥ
vāsudevamūrtiḥ
(samacakraudvāta deśe)dvāradveśe samecakre dṛśyete cātiśobhane 90
vāsudevassavijñeyo śuklābhaśca śubhapradaḥ
pradyumnaḥ
makarābhamukhe rekhā pārśvataḥ pṛṣṭhatopivā 91
napradyumna ssūkṣmacakraḥ pītavarṇaśśubhapradaḥ
anirudda
kṛṣṇavarṇassamadvāre cakrabhittisamīpagam 92
alpacakraṃ bhavedūrthvaṃ pārśvecaṇaka puṣpavat
aniruddha itikhyāta ssarvalokasukhāvahaḥ 93
puruṣottamamūrti lakṣaṇaṃ
mathyacakraśśyāmavarṇo mastakepṛthucakrakaḥ
puruṣottamanāmāsaupūjakasya suśobhanaḥ 94
adhokṣajaḥ
adhokṣajoraktarekhā vṛttadehastucakravān
kiṃcitkapilasaṃyukto sūkṣmovāsthūlaevavā 95
adhokṣajobilayutaḥ pūjakasya śubhapradaḥ
acyutamūrtiḥ
caturbhujocāṣṭacakrairvāma dakṣiṇa (pārśvataḥ)pārśyakaiḥ 96
adhiṣṭhito mukhepyeka cakraḥ kuṃḍilaśobhitaḥ
śaṃkhanaṃdakapadmādiśārṅakomodakī dharaḥ 97
musaladhvaja dhṛkchveta cchatramabhrāṃkuśairvṛtaḥ
socyutaḥ kadhito nāmnā durlabhastasyadarśanam 98
janārdhanaḥ
pūrvabhāgaikavadanaḥ pascātrekhā (samanvitatā)syasaṃyutā
janārdanaśca kradharaḥ strīpradoarināśanaḥ 99
upeṃdromaṇivarṇābho hrasva cakrātikomalaḥ
māyāyutassūkṣmabilassarvasaukhyapradāyakaḥ 100
śyāmalākomakabhastu sacapārśvetu cakrayut
albadāsamāyuktaḥ haririṣṭapradāyakaḥ 101
hāyagrīva
hayagrīvo pi ऩganān raktapītavi miśritaḥ
anakuśākṛti govaktre cakradvaya samanvitaḥ 102
badmākṛtistathā pārśve kuṃḍalākṛti revavā
jñānadomokṣado loke bhogadovāpi padmaja 103
aṣṭabhuja nṛsiṃhāḥ
catuścakroṣṭa cakraśca dīrghāsyoyonnatākṛtiḥ
soṣṭabāhunṛsiṃhasyā dyatibhiḥ pūjanīyakaḥ 104
divyavāsudevamūrti
ṣaḍbilodvādaśaścakraḥ vanamālāvibhūṣitaḥ
vāsudevoti divyoya mitiśāstreṣu niścitaḥ 105
sudarśana nārasiṃhaḥ
āṣṭavaktrnnata vapuḥ vivṛtāsyo bhayapradaḥ
ṣoḍaśaiśca krarājābhisaṃyuto śatṛmārakaḥ 106
sudarśana nṛsiṃhoyaṃ gṛhārbāyāṃ visarjayet
sālagrāma pūjāvidhiḥ
ityevaṃ lakṣaṇānsarvān parīkṣyārcanamācaret 107
sālagrāmaśilānāṃ tu bhakti yuktena cetasā
devālayeṣu narvekṣu tattanmūrtyanukūlinam 108
sālagrāmaśilāṃ tīrthaberārthaṃ parikalbayet
sālagrāmābhiṣekaṃ rrāniṣiddhaḥ
nakadāvihitaṃ rātrau sālagrāmābhiṣecanam 109
śaṃkhevinyasyayo bhaktyāpūjate tulasīdalaiḥ
[adhikapāṭhāni
patadārabhya adhyāyāṃtamadhipāṭhayeva]
sālagrāma pūjāvitīrthaphalaviśeṣaḥ
sālagrāmaṃtu sassarva kāmānāpnoti niścayaḥ 110
sālagrāmābhiṣiktaṃ yattoyaṃ pi batimānavaḥ
tasya sarvāṇi pāpāni nasyaṃttyeva nasaṃśayaḥ 111
itisaṃkṣevataḥ proktau sālagrāmasya lakṣaṇam
yasyaśravaṇamātreṇa dhūtapāponarobhavet 112
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
sālagrāmalakṣaṇaṃ nāma aṣṭaviṃśodhyāyaḥ
śrīśrīśrī

śrīmatpāṃcarātra divyāgame śrīpuruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
ekānatriṃśodhyāyaḥ
snapanavidhiḥ
śrī bhagavān
snapanakālanirṇayaḥ
mnapanānāṃ vidhiṃpakṣye śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana
viṣṇude cāyanecaiva grahaṇecaṃdra sūryayoḥ 1
rāṅñaśca janmanakṣatre phalguṇyāṃ śravaṇepi ca
utsavānāṃ samāraṃbhe cāṃte caivaviśeṣataḥ 2
deśakṣobhedha cotpāte nityapūjāvilopane
chaṃḍāladyaiśca saṃspṛṣṭh devabiṃbādikaṃ tathā 3
utpātādiṣu (sarveṣu) saṃprāpte biṃbanaṃcalanādiṣu
ityādiṣu nimitteṣu snapanaṃ kārayedguru 4
snapanamaṃṭapa nirmāṇavidhi
kalpayetsnavanārthaṃtu maṃṭapaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam
ṣoḍaśastaṃbhasaṃyuktaṃ dvādaśāṣṭacatustathā 5
staṃbhairyutaṃ yathāśakti ralaṃkārai ralaṃkṛtam
maṃṭapaṃ kalpayitvā dautasya cottarapārśvataḥ 6
kuṃḍa nirmāṇa, jala bhānajanasthāpanādi kalaśāvāhanavidhi
homārthaṃ kārayetkuṃḍa mīśānyāṃ jalabhājanaṃ
kalaśānloha jānvāpi mṛṇayānvā yathāvasu 7
saṃgṛhyakṣāghayettyairnyasettāṃdarbha saṃstare
adhomukhān tadūrthvetu punardarbhān paristaret 8
saṃprokṣyabārghyatoyena vikiretteṣu cākṣatān
uttānānitataḥ kṛtvāto yairāpūrayettataḥ 9
kalaśānuguṇenaiva sūtrāṇyāsphālyadeśikaḥ
koṣṭhānivibhajetteṣu pīṭhāni parikalpayet 10
śālitaṃḍulapīṭhorthvaṃ tilaiḥ pīṭhaṃ prakalpayet
tadarthārthapramāṇena tatpīṭhekalaśānnyaset 11
ekāśītistadhaikona paṃcāśadaśa saptaca
jaghanyamnapane coktamuttamādivibhedataḥ 12
śatadvaya samāyukta ssaptatissapta repica
paṃcāsītitathācaika viṃśatirmadhyame trithā 13
trinapta tyuttareyukta ccatuśśatamataḥ param
ekāśītyuttareyukta dviśataṃ paṃcaviṃśatiḥ 14
uttamasnapane cokta muttamādi vibhedataḥ
(evaṃ jñātvātubhedāni) evaṃ vijñāya tadbhedān yatkarmaṇi yaducyate 15
tadevasnapanaṃ kāryaṃ jaghanyetu kaniṣṭhikam
kalaśeṣudravya nirṇayaḥ
ucyate snapanaṃ brahman madhyame kalaśe ghṛtam 16
uṣṇodakaṃ tu pūrvasyāṃ agneye ratnamucyate
phalaṃyāmye ca nairutyāṃ lohaṃ bhadraṃcavāruṇe 17
kṛṣṇāgaruṃ ca vāyavye cottare taṃḍulaṃ smṛtam
dhānyamīśāna kuṃbhe ca bahiḥ prācīnakuṃbhake 18
tulasīṃ ca vinikṣipya cāgneye dadhivinyaset
yāmye siddhārthakaṃ caiva nairutyāṃ kṣiramucyate 19
paścimeghanasāraṃ ca vāyavye madhunikṣipet
uttare paṃcagavyaṃ syā dīśānyekhādi ratvacam 20
evaṃ nikṣipya kuṃbheṣu kūrcānapi vinikṣipet
[adhikapāṭhīṇi
cūtapallavaratnādi dhātubījānyadhakramam
phalāni pratimānyasya navavastraiśca veṣṭhayet]
(evaṃ jñātvātu bhedāni)navena vāsasācchādya tatrahomaṃ samārabhet 21
kalaśāvāhanahomaḥ
aṣṭākṣareṇa maṃtreṇa samidhājya carūn hunet
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ vāśatamaṣṭottaraṃ tuvā 22
tataḥpūrṇāhutīṃ hutvā saṃpātājyena secayet
kalaśādhi devatāvāhanavidhiḥ
kalaśeṣu yajeddevān vāsudevaṃ ghṛtetathā 23
prācyādiṣu krameṇaiva puruṣaṃ satyamacyutam
anaṃtaṃ ca vidhikthseṣu keśavādīn yajettataḥ 24
bāhyeṃdrādicaturdhikṣu viṣṇvādīnpūjayetkramāt
vidhikdhseṣuca kuṃbheṣu śrīdharādīnsrapūjayet 25
snapanavidhiḥ
devamarghyaissamabhyarcya rakṣāsūtraṃ tu baṃdhayet
daṃtakāṣṭhaṃ sugaṃdhaṃ ca tailamāmalakaṃ tataḥ 26
puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ tathādīpaṃ samarbyakalaśaiḥ pṛthak
devasya snapanaṃ kuryātpratiṣṭhāyāṃ yadhoditam 27
haridrācūrṇapūrṇaṃtu kuṃbhamādāya deśikaḥ
śrīsūktenaiva devaṃtu snāpayitvā (yathāvithi)tataḥpanam 28
sahasradhārāsnapanaṃ nṛsūkteva tu kārayet
bhagavadārādhanaṃ
plotaśāṭyā tu saṃmṛjya vasrairābharaṇai(rvibhuṃ)ssaha 29
gaṃdhamālyai ralaṃkṛtya pāyasādīnni vedayet
tāṃbūlaṃ ca tato datvā daṃḍavatpraṇametkṣitau 30
bhagavatsrārthavaṃ
yatphalaṃ samanuddiśya snapanaṃ tu kṛtaṃ mayā
tatphalaṃ saṃprayacca sva nārāyaṇa namostute 31
iti saṃprārthya deveśaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ niveśyaca
ārādhanādikaṃ sarvaṃ yathāvidhi samācaret 32
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrī puruṣottama saṃhitāyāṃ
snapana vidhānaṃ nāma ekonatriṃśodhyāyaḥ

prāyaścittavidhi
brahmauvāca
śravaṇārdhaṃ brahma praśnaḥ
śrotumicchāmi bhagavan prāyaścittānyaśeṣataḥ
tatsarvaṃ vistareṇaiva brūhidevadayānidhe 1
bhagavatprativacanaṃ
śrībhagavānuvāca
prāyaścittāvasara nirūpaṇaṃ
prāyaścittavidhiṃ vakṣye śruṇuṣva kamalāsana
devayakṣamunīṃdrādyrairanyaiḥ paurāṇikaistathā 2
devamunyādi pratiṣṭi tabiṃbeṣu aṃgabhaṃgejīrṇe
pratiṣṭhitetu biṃbetu yadaṃgaṃbhaṃgameṣyati
tadaṃgaṃsaṃdhayedyatnāt suvarṇenaiva nānyathā 3
bhagneśilāmaye biṃbe devamunyādi kalpite
tadaṃgaṃ rukmajaṃ kṛtvā tatsaṃdheyaṃ yadhāpuram 4
devādisthāpite biṃbe hīnāṃge mṛṇmayesati
nakadācitparityājyaṃ tatsaṃdheyaṃ yadhāvidhi 5
evaṃ bhaṃgeca jīrṇeca aṃgaṃ naṃdheya mādarāt
mahāṃgādi bhaṃge biṃbaṃ jīrṇeca
mahāṃgopāṃgapratyaṃga bhaṃgaścedvai pramādataḥ 6
tattadrūpānusāreṇa saṃdheyaṃ pūrvavadbudhaḥ
uttamāṃga vihīnetu tadbiṃbaṃ parivarjayet 7
[athikapāṭhāni
yaḥpūjayati tadbiṃbaṃ lobhamohadi hetubhiḥ
tadgrāmanāśanaṃ siddhaṃ rājārāṣṭraṃ ca naśyati]
hīnāṃgāni tu biṃbāni tānihitvā punasṛjet
aṃgamātra samādhāne sṛṣṭho narvātmanāpica 8
kuṃbhāvāhanavithiḥ
sauvarṇaṃ rājitaṃ tāmraṃ paṃcalohamayaṃtuvā
tattadrūpānusāreṇa supatrāṇi ca kārayet 9
kṣālayecchuddhatoyena dhūpayedgaṃdhacūrṇakaiḥ
saṃprokṣya puṇyatoyena paṃcagavyena secayet 10
dhānyāditritayenaiva pīṭhaṃ kuryā ttadagrataḥ
tanmadhye vilikhetpadma maṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikam 11
ācchādya navavastreṇa prokṣaye nmūlavidyayā
gaṃdhapuṣpākṣatakuśaiḥ pallavaiḥ pūrayetkramāt 12
tattadbiṃbasamīpetu sthāpayetkuṃbha muttamam
tataśśaktīssamākṛṣya svasvamaṃtraistu deśikaḥ 13
devaṃ saṃpūjyavidhivatkālaṃ vijñāpayetsudhīḥ
darśayitvā mahāmudrāṃ cakramudrāṃ pradarśyaca 14
sthāneviviktebhimate sthāvayetkuṃbhamuttamam
mūlaberetu saṃprāptesthāvaye tpūrvapatkramāt 15
anyadhākautuke vāpi vidyamānekva cidbhavet
pūjālopona kartavyaḥ kadāciccha nasaṃśayaḥ 16
govālarajjubhiryadvā jīrṇabiṃbaṃ samuddharet
vyāhṛtyāvādhasalile nikṣipe ddeśikottamaḥ 17
dūrvābhirmadhuyuktābhi ssvasvamaṃtreṇahomayet
nirmāyapūrvavadbiṃbaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya yathāvidhi 18
kuṃbhamadhyagatāṃśaktiṃ brahmaraṃdhreṇamārgaṃtaḥ
yadhoktavidhinācaiva biṃbasyāṃ taḥ praveśayet 19
praṇavena brahmaraṃdhraṃ pidhāya parameṣṭhinā
tattasthāneṣu saṃsthāpya pūjāṃ kuryā (viśeṣataḥ)dgarīyasīm 20
aṃgajīrṇetu kartavya
brahma
vidhaubrahmapraśnaḥ

varṣodakaistu saṃssṛṣṭh biṃbe pāduka yostathā
ālaye madhusaṃprāpte vajrapātepi vā guruḥ 43
biṃbasya calanecaiva patane dahanepica

tasmātsarvaprayatnena saṃprokṣaṇa mathācaret
saṃprokṣaṇavidhiḥ

[athikapāṭhāni
aṃkurānarpayitvātu yathāśāstravithānataḥ]
devasya tu purobhāge dhānyapīṭhaṃ prakalpyaca
mahākuṃbhaṃtu saṃsthāpya sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutam 47

mūlamaṃtreṇa juhuyācchāṃtihomapurassaram, 48
uttamatritayānāpi madhyama tritayenavā
tatodevasya hastetu kaṃkaṇaṃ baṃdhayeddvijaḥ 49
doṣaprābalyakaṃ dṛṣṭvānnapanaṃ kārayedguruḥ
mahākuṃbhaṃ samādāya ``vedavāditra nisvanaiḥ 50
maṃdiraṃ triḥ parikramya devasya puratobhuvi
dhānyapīṭhetu saṃsthāpya viṣṇusūktaiśca vāruṇaiḥ 51
aghamarṣaṇasūkaina śāṃtisūktaiśca prokṣayet

śāṃtihoma
brahma

utsaveṣu pratiṣṭhāyāṃ yadhātadavadhāraya
agnikāryakramaḥ
pacanālaya māsādya dvārasthānarcaye tkramāt 2
kuṃḍasya paścimebhāge prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ
kūrmāsaneco paviśya prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ caret 3
ātmano dakṣiṇe pārśvekṣipetpuṣpākṣatādikam
vāmetaṃ homadravyāṇi nikṣipya nyāsamācaret 4
kuṃḍasaṃskārādi
ṛcā eṣahi deveti sākṣatodakapāṇinā
agneḥpurastānnikṣipya cātmanobhimukhaṃ smaret 5
sumukhaḥ ssuprasannaśca varado bhavasarvadā
iti saṃprārthyamatimān agnikuṃḍaṃ caprokṣayet 6
[adhikapāṭhāni
kuṃḍamadhye śriyaṃ dhyātvā ṛtusnātāṃ vidhānataḥ
agni madhy maṅāviṣṇuṃ thyāyeddeśikasattamaḥ]
akṣatairarcayeddikṣu vidikṣu ca yadhākramam
(adaucāha vanīyaṃ ca)āhavanīyyeti padaṃ gorhapatyamanaṃtaram 7
dakṣiṇāgniṃ ca sabhyaṃ ca avanadhyāgni mevaca
ratnāgniṃ caivadāvāgniṃ dviśīrṣakamiti bṛvan 8
praṇavādinamoṃ taiśca pariṣecanamācaret
darbhairagniṃ parastīrya agneruttarapārśvataḥ 9
dvidhādarbhā ssamāstīrya (pātrānāsāda yetkramāt)pātrāsādanamācaret
pātrāpādanaṃ
dvādaśaṃ vāṣṭapātraṃ vāṣaṭ pātraṃ vācatuṣṭayaṃ 10
eteṣvekaṃ prakurvīta yadhākarmānu sārataḥ
pātrasaṃskāraḥ
kṛtvāpavitraṃ darbhābhyāṃ prokṣaṇyāṃjalamāvapet 11
trirutpūrya pavitreṇa pātrāṇāṃ prokṣaṇaṃcaret
praṇītāsaṃskāraḥ
uttānāni tataḥkṛtvā praṇītāyāṃ jalaṃkṣipet 12
utpūrya pūrvavannī raṃ uddharenmukhasammitam
agneruttaradarbheṣu praṇītāṃ vinyasettataḥ 13
darbhaiḥpracchādya tatpātraṃ brahmāṇaṃ dakṣiṇercayet
ājyasaṃskāraḥ
ājyapātraṃ vilāpyāgnau sthālyāmājyaṃ nirūpyaca 14
āṃgārānuttarasdhānvai nirūhyo parivinyaset
ājyasthālīṃ tatotpūrya pavitreṇa yadhāvithi 15
aṃguṣṭhopaka niṣṭābhyāṃ graṃthiṃ visrasya kṣālayet
sṛksṛva saṃskāraḥ
prāgagramagnau praharet sṛksṛvaukṣālayettataḥ 16
(uṣṇodakenatadanu mārjayetpaṃcabhiḥ kuśaiḥ) suksṛvau mārjaye ddarbhaiḥ caturbhirgarbhavarjitaiḥ
kṣālayitvātato darbhānagnau vinyanya deśikaḥ 17
aṃguṣṭāgrasamasthaulyaṃprādeśatrayasammitam
paridhayaḥ
paridhiṃ prakṣipetpaścā dudagagraṃ yathābhavet 18
tarjanyagra samasthaulyaṃ nyūnaprādeśadīrghikam
parithiṃ vinyasedyāmye prāgagraṃtu yadhābhavet 19
madhyāṃguli samaṃsthaulyaṃ nyūnaprādeśakatrayam
paridhiṃ vinyasetsaumye cāgraṃ pūrvavadiṣyate 20
kaniṣṭāgrasamasthaulyvau dvādaśāṃgula sammitau
āmārau nikṣipedghūrthva mukhāvagnī śakoṇayoḥ 21
idmahomaṃ
idhmaprakṣepaṇaṃ cāgnau kṛtvātūryapurassaraṃ
nṛveṇājyaṃ samādāya sṛcaṃ saṃpūryadeśikaḥ 22
āghārahomaḥ
vāyuvya koṇamārabhya vaiśvānaradi gaṃtakam
hunetpracāvatiṃ thyāyan pradhamāghārahomake 23
nairuterdiśamārabhya raudrakoṇāṃ tamevahi
iṃdraṃdhyāyan tataḥpaścāt dvitīyāghārahomake 24
cakṣurhomaḥ
caturgṛhītaṃ kṛtvājyaṃ cakṣurhomaṃ namācaret
madhyevyāhṛtibhirhu tvā thyāyedagni manuttamaṃ 25
saptajihvahomaḥ
madhyame viśvarūpāca kālītatpūrvato hunet
karālīyāmyataścaiva paścimetu manojavā 26
sulohitā cottaretu dūmrākṣācāgnikoṇake
nairutyāṃ vispuliṃgācajihvānvetāsuhomayet 27
[adhikapāṭhāni
vaiṣṇavāgniṃ prakartavyaṃ saṃskārai ścatuṣoḍaśaiḥ
taddhomaṃ ca prakurvīta samidhājyacarukramāt]
gāyatyrā pradhamaṃhutvā dvitīyaṃ viṣṇumaṃtrakaṃ
tṛtīyaṃ mūlamaṃtrṇa samithājyacarūno hunet 28
tatona pāyinmatvā hutvābhāgavatānprati
digīśāncakramaṃ proktaṃ nityahome vidhīyate 29
parivārādidevatā homaḥ
utsave parivārāṃśca saptāvaraṇa devatāḥ
tithyādi ṛkṣadevāṃ śca tathā vaikuṃṭhapārṣadāḥ 30
caturviṃśati mūrtīśca maṃḍalasthānyathāvidhi
uddiśya tattaddevāṃ śca samithājya carūn hunet 31
caruṃ puruṣasūktena ṣoḍaśācahuti bhirhunet
prāyaścittahomaḥ pūrṇāhutipraṇītimārjanaṃ
prāyaścittāhutīrhutvā pūrṇāhuti mathācaret 32
praṇītimārjanaṃ kṛtvā sṛcaṃ saṃpūryavāriṇā
īśānakoṇa mārabhya bahiḥkuṃḍā tpradakṣiṇam 33
agni pradakṣiṇaṃ dhārāhutiḥ
thārābhisseca yitvātu svaśiraḥ prokṣayeddvijaḥ
phalaṃ samarpya devāya prārthayedbhakti saṃyutaḥ
bhagavatsamarpaṇaṃ
itisaṃkṣepataḥ prokto agni kāryavidhiḥ paraḥ
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahovaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottamasaṃhitāyā
agni kāryavidhirnāma ekatriṃśodhyāyaḥ

utsavaśabdanirvacanaṃ

ekakāletu madhyāhne kṛtaṃ tadadhamaṃ bhavet
balipradānāvasaraḥ
balidāna vihīnena notsavo'bhyudayāvahaḥ 6
(tasmādbalipradānaṃtu pratyahaṃ kārayeddhvija)tasmādbali pradānena kāraye dutsavaṃ param
balidravyabhedaḥ
prātastu taṃḍulai reva baliṃ dadyādyathā kramam 7
annenaivatu mathyāhne sāyāhnekusumaiśsubhaiḥ
balipradānavidhiḥ
tadarthaṃ balipātraṃtu svarṇādidravya nirmitam 8
samādāya balidravyaiḥ pūrayeddeśikottamaḥ
devasyāgretu tatpātraṃ (cālayaṃ saṃpraveśayet)vinyasyāvāhayecchuciḥ 9
pātrasthaṃ devamarghyādvaiḥ pūjayitvā yadhoditaiḥ
pātrastha dalamadhyetu viṣṇvādīnarcaye tkramāt 10
paricārakasya śirasi tatpātraṃ nyasyamaṃgalaiḥ
bhadraṃkarṇeti maṃtreṇa baliberaṃ gurussvayam 11
śibhikāyāṃ samāropyaveda ghoṣapurassaram
chatracāmarabheryādi nṛttavāditranisvanaiḥ 12
pradakṣiṇakrameṇaiva ālayaṃ tu paribhramet
baliṃ datvātoyapūrvaṃ toyottaramapikramāt 13
tatpīṭhaṃ tu parikramya darśaye daṣṭamaṃ galān
evamuktaprakāreṇa sarveṣvāvaraṇeṣvapi 14
yathākramaṃ baliṃ datvā(vinyasyā vāhayeddhariṃ)praviśedgarbhamaṃdiram 15
mūlamaṃtreṇa datvārghyaṃ mūlabereniyojayet
viṣvakcenasya śirasi baliśeṣaṃ kṣipettataḥ 16
balipradānakāle nṛttagītādibhedāḥ
nṛttaṃ tu bahudhājñeyaṃ parivārādi toṣaṇam
gītaṃ madhyamasaṃyuktaṃ garuḍanya tu śasyate 17
gauḍarāgaṃ tadhānṛttaṃ viṣṇukrāṃtamiti smṛtam
nṛttamabjabhavasyāpi svastikāhvaya mucyate 18
svaraṃ gāṃthāramityuktaṃ mukhārī rāgamevaca
iṃdrasya sarvatobhadraṃ āgneḥ kheṭakamucyateḥ 19
kuṭṭimaṃ varuṇasyāpi vāyośca pṛṣṭha kuṭṭimam
yamasya cakranṛttaṃsyāt kāṃtāraṃ nairutestathā 20
vāmajānuḥ kuberasya īśānasyorthvanṛttakam
svarabhedaḥ
ṣaḍjamasvara miṃdrasya āgneṛṣabhaviṣyat 21
gāṃdhāraṃ yamadesa nairutermadhya manmṛtaḥ
paṃcamaṃ varuṇasyāpi mārutasya tu daivataḥ 22
kuberasya niṣādasyā dīśānasya ta devatu
tālabhedaḥ
iṃdrasya samatālaṃsyā (dravivikramamagnaye)dagnerbaṃ thāvatālakaṃ 23
yamasya bhṛṃgīṇī tālaṃ nairutermallatālakam
maṃgalaṃ varuṇasyāpi vāyośca jayatālakam 24
bhadratālaṃ kuberasya (raudresyānnaṃti tālakaṃ) ḍhakkarī śūlinobhavet
rāgabhdaḥ
iṃdrasya nādanāmātu vahnestu lalitaṃbhavet 25

megharaṃ jī ca vāruṇyāṃ vāyoścaiva vasaṃtakam 26
kuberasya tu śrīrāgaṃ śaṃkarasyatu śāṃkaram
evamukta prakāreṇa nṛttagītādikaṃ caret 27
[adhikapāṭhāṇi
anyeṣāṃ devatānāṃ tubhadratālaṃ praśastyate

madhyamaṃ svaramityāhu ritiśāstraviniścayaḥ]
balipradānaphalaṃ

vasiṣṭaḥ

nakadāpi pravaktavyaṃ gopyaṃ gopyaṃ manīṣibhiḥ

durlabhaṃ sarvalokānāṃ devānāṃca sadāpriyaṃ
ṛṣayaḥ mahacchāstramidaṃśṛtvā vaśiṣṭhenamahātmanā
ṛṣayohṛṣṭamanasāstaṃstuvaṃtāvabhūvuḥ]
itisaṃkṣevataḥ proktaṃ mudrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃparaṃ
iti śrīpāṃcarātre mahopaniṣadi śrīpuruṣottamasaṃṅitāyāṃ
mudrālakṣaṇīṃnnāmatrayatriṃśodhyāyaḥ
śrīśrīśrī
saṃpūrṇaṃ
